<document path="BUSINESS_STRATEGY.md">
# SABIL (CoolMan) Startup Business Strategy

Prepared for absolute market viability and steady monetization of localized water-point tracking networks.

---

## 🎯 1. Growth Foundations: Why this App Works

Sabil fills a hidden and underserved utility niche across the Middle East, North Africa, and South Asia.

### The Problem with Google Maps / OSM
*   **Stale Metadata**: Google Maps cannot track whether a local public water cooler is functional, empty, or currently broken.
*   **Lack of Granularity**: It does not indicate critical parameters like **cup supplies** or whether a communal glass is the only drinking medium.
*   **Zero Cultural Context**: It fails to recognize the immense societal structure of **"Sadaka Jariyah"** (ongoing charity), where families, companies, and mosques fund these coolers as ongoing blessings.

### The Sabil Solution & Success Triggers
*   **Cultural Symbiosis**: It leverages cultural alignment. Sabil turns water stewardship into a gamified, respectful experience.
*   **Frictionless Verification**: The quick-verification button ("Still Working") lets tourists and workers reporting in real-time update the network's health in a single tap, gaining valuable points.
*   **Defense Against Fraud**: Utilizing PostGIS 15-meter coordinate shields blocks overlapping, duplicate coordinates, and spam reports.

---

## 💰 2. Monetization Matrix (The Money-Rolling Blueprint)

Unlike general software solutions, forcing users to watch traditional interstitial reward ads on a clean humanitarian utility will alienate them. Instead, we implement high-yield B2B sponsorship models and premium enterprise options:

### A. Certified Sponsor Profiles (B2B SaaS / Ad Roll-out)
Local shops, pharmacies, cafes, and mosques pay a modest monthly SaaS fee to run as "Verified Sponsors" on nearby coolers.
*   **What they get**: A stylized green card on the map interface with a clickable link pointing to their store and a verified badge.
*   **Value Proposition**: This functions as hyper-localized charity branding (CSR). For example, "This cooler is maintained by El-Borg Pharmacy (10 meters away) — get 10% off water filters today".

### B. "Refill Sadaka" Direct Funding Pools
Many individuals across the globe want to contribute to charity but lack direct access to physical coolers.
*   **What we build**: Integrate a payment checkout (Stripe/Paymob) inside the map detail page.
*   **The Model**: Users can fund cup refilling or physical repair teams for a cooler. Sabil takes a **7–12% administration & transaction commission** on every single micro-donation, using local partners to fulfill the physical refill and uploading a photo as proof.

### C. Large-Scale Enterprise Sadaka Dashboards
Corporate CSR programs (PepsiCo, Nestlé, real estate developers) fund massive networks of hundreds of street water coolers.
*   **What they get**: We sell them access to an unified Web Dashboard that displays spatial tracking, water consumption metrics, filter lifespan projections, and user feedback ratings.
*   **The Model**: Annual corporate subscriptions (e.g., $240/year per cooler monitored).

---

## 🗺️ 3. 12-Month Product & Feature Roadmap

To maintain user retention and avoid overloading the application, we deploy features selectively:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Phase 1: Local Density (Months 1–3)                   │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ • Seed Maadi, Cairo core sector with 200 locations.    │
│ • Secure 10 initial "Business Sponsors".               │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                           │
                           ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Phase 2: Community & Sponsoring (Months 4–6)           │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ • Launch "Sponsor Assign" control panel on Flutter map. │
│ • Push "Sadaka Cup Refill" Stripe/Checkout modules.    │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                           │
                           ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Phase 3: Hardware Expansion & API (Months 7–12)        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ • Integrate IoT smart weight / flow-rate sensors.      │
│ • Establish REST API data channels for municipalities. │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Future Growth Vectors:
1.  **AI Image Moderation**: Automatically pass photo uploads through Gemini Vision model checks to reject non-cooler photos immediately, alleviating the admin's queue review fatigue. *(Status: planned; to be built from scratch once the app is up and running. See `docs/FINDINGS_LOG.md` section 8.)*
2.  **IoT Weight Monitors**: Sell low-power battery-operated IoT weights to sponsors that slip under the bottle, auto-reporting status updates when weight drops below 5kg.
3.  **Beverage Brand Alliances**: Large mineral water bottling entities can distribute bio-degradable paper cups bearing their brand directly to our highly-ranked sponsor locations.
</document>

<document path="DOCUMENTATION.md">
# SABIL (CoolMan) Software Documentation

This document describes the software architecture, data schemas, verification mechanisms, and API topologies for the Sabil public water locator application.

---

## 🏗️ 1. Architecture Overview

```
 ┌───────────────────────┐
 │   Flutter Client App  │ <── (Mobile, Dart / OpenStreetMap map)
 └───────────┬───────────┘
             │ HTTP (JSON and photo uploads) over port 8000
             ▼
 ┌───────────────────────┐
 │   FastAPI Backend API │ <── (Python / Uvicorn)
 └───────────┬───────────┘
             │ asyncpg
             ▼
 ┌───────────────────────┐
 │ PostgreSQL + PostGIS  │ <── (stores coolers; answers "what is within 5 km?")
 └───────────────────────┘
```

1.  **Mobile client (Flutter/Dart)**: a single map screen with pop-up sheets, plus profile, submissions, sponsor, onboarding and (hidden) admin screens. Map markers are grouped with `flutter_map_marker_cluster`.
2.  **Server (FastAPI/SQLAlchemy)**: asynchronous REST API with rate limits and sign-in. Distance searches use PostGIS.

The code is organised by **feature**: each feature folder holds its own `models.py` (database tables), `schemas.py` (what the API accepts and returns) and `router.py` (the routes). See the folder map and the list of reusable pieces in sections 6 and 7 below.

---

## 🗄️ 2. Database Schema & Relations

The PostgreSQL schema is structured around 4 interactive entities:

```
                  ┌──────────────┐
                  │    users     │
                  └──────┬───────┘
                         │ 1
                         │
                         │ 1..* (Submissions, Approvals, Votes)
                         ▼
  ┌──────────┐ 1      ┌─────────────────┐ 1      ┌────────────────┐
  │ sponsors ├───────>│  water_coolers  ├────────>│ cooler_photos  │
  └──────────┘  0..*  └─────────────────┘   0..*  └────────────────┘
```

### Table Definitions

#### `users`
Tracks registered community contributors and administrators.
*   `id` (UUID): Primary Key.
*   `username` (String(64)): Unique index.
*   `hashed_password` (String(256)): Structured password hash.
*   `trust_score` (Integer): Dynamic score mapping permissions. Default: 0.
*   `badges`: Reserved for future achievements; nothing uses it yet.
*   `created_at` (DateTime): Registration date.

#### `sponsors`
Tracks commercial or religious organizations sponsoring charity coolers.
*   `id` (UUID): Primary Key.
*   `name` (String(255)): Entity name.
*   `sponsor_type` (Enum): Business, Mosque, Individual, NGO.
*   `contact` (String(255), optional): Launch links or telephone numbers.
*   `verified` (Boolean): Administrative endorsement state. Default: False.

#### `water_coolers`
The main spatial entity representing physical drinking water stations.
*   `id` (UUID): Primary Key.
*   `name` (String(255)): Human-readable label or landmark description.
*   `latitude`, `longitude` (Numeric(10, 7)): Decimal map coordinates.
*   `geom` (Geometry(POINT, 4326)): Spatial coordinate mapped to PostGIS indexes.
*   `status` (Enum): Working, Empty, Broken.
*   `last_verified_at` (DateTime): Last confirmation date.
*   `is_approved` (Boolean): Live visibility state.
*   `is_rejected` (Boolean): Deemed spam or non-existent.
*   `pending_status` (String, optional): Staged update proposed by non-trusted reporter.
*   `has_cups` (Boolean, optional): High-level cup count stock.
*   `cups_type` (Enum, optional): Communal, Disposable.
*   `user_id` (UUID, Foreign Key): Identifier of the original reporter.
*   `pending_status_user_id` (UUID, Foreign Key): Submitter of review updates.
*   `sponsor_id` (UUID, Foreign Key): Linked charity sponsor.
*   `iot_weight_kg` (Float, optional): Reserved for a future sensor; nothing uses it yet.

#### `cooler_photos`
Tracks verification photos uploaded for security review.
*   `id` (UUID): Primary Key.
*   `cooler_id` (UUID, Foreign Key): Owning cooler.
*   `file_path` (String(512)): File address on the disk or S3 bucket.
*   `uploaded_at` (DateTime): Time of upload.
*   `is_approved` (Boolean): Admin approved for public view.
*   `photo_category` (Enum): Cooler, Cups.
*   `user_id` (UUID, Foreign Key): Who uploaded it.

---

## 🔒 3. Integrity, Authentication & Security Guidelines

### Password Encryption
All client credentials utilize cryptographic standards:
*   Passes are salted and scrambled via **PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256**.
*   Work factor iterations are set to **260,000 iterations**, avoiding brute-force vectors.

### JWT Structure
Tokens are signed server-side using **HMAC-SHA256**:
*   *Audience parameters*: `sabil-user` or `sabil-admin`.
*   *Expirations*: Standard user token is set to **7 days** (10,080 minutes), while admin tokens auto-invalidate after **8 hours** to limit hijacking windows.

---

## 🎮 4. Verification Mechanics & Trust Logic

A central gamified trust loop handles review queues and defends the database against spam:

1.  **Submission Verification**:
    *   Proposed coolers get queued as `is_approved = False`.
    *   If the submitter is a **Verified Reporter (Trust Score ≥ 500)**, the cooler is auto-approved and goes live instantly.
2.  **Trust Flow Calculations** (all numbers live in `backend/sabil/users/trust.py`):
    *   *Add New Cooler*: Approved: **+50 Trust** to the reporter. Rejected: **-100 Trust**.
    *   *Verify / Confirm*: Tap "Still Working" (restricted by a 1-hour cooldown per user and cooler): **+5 Trust**.
    *   *Propose Status Update*: **+10 Trust**, paid only when the change takes effect: at once for trusted users (score >= 500), otherwise when an admin approves it. One report per cooler every 10 minutes, and reporting the status a cooler already has is refused.
3.  **GPS Duplication Guard**:
    *   The database rejects adding a cooler if another cooler exists within a **15-meter radius** (determined via PostGIS `ST_DWithin` on the coordinates index). This blocks multiple reports for the same cooler.
4.  **Privacy of unapproved items**:
    *   An unapproved cooler (`GET /coolers/{id}`) and an unapproved photo (`/uploads/photos/<file>`) are visible only to admins and to the person who submitted them. Everyone else gets 404.
5.  **Abuse limits** (all in `backend/sabil/core/config.py`):
    *   3 new coolers per minute per IP; 20 photo uploads per minute per IP; 10 login/register attempts per minute per IP.
    *   Uploads: JPEG, PNG or WebP only (checked by the file's real contents), at most 8 MB.
    *   `SABIL_ENV=production` makes the server refuse to start with the default admin password or token secret.

---

## 🌐 5. API Overview

Interactive docs are served at `/docs` when the server runs. Routes by feature:

| Feature | Routes |
|---|---|
| Accounts | `POST /auth/register`, `POST /auth/login`, `GET /auth/me`, `GET /users/leaderboard` |
| Finding coolers | `GET /coolers/nearby`, `GET /coolers/search`, `GET /coolers/my`, `GET /coolers/{id}`, `GET /coolers/{id}/photos` |
| Reporting | `POST /coolers`, `PATCH /coolers/{id}/status`, `POST /coolers/{id}/confirm`, `POST /coolers/{id}/photos` |
| Sponsors (public) | `GET /sponsors`, `GET /sponsors/{id}` |
| Photos | `GET /uploads/photos/{file}` (approval-checked) |
| Admin (needs admin token) | `POST /auth/admin/login`, then everything under `/admin/...`: review queue, approve / reject, force status, delete, photo moderation, sponsor management |

---

## 6. Reusable pieces

Each of these has no knowledge of coolers, so you can copy it into another project.

**Backend (`backend/sabil/`)**

| File | What it gives you | Copy it with |
|---|---|---|
| `core/security.py` | Password hashing and signed login tokens (JWT) with no extra libraries | `core/config.py` (the settings it reads) |
| `core/rate_limit.py` | Per-IP request limits and per-user cooldowns | `core/config.py` |
| `core/validation.py` | Text sanitising for user input | nothing |
| `core/lookup.py` | `get_or_404` helper | nothing |
| `core/dependencies.py` | "Who is asking?" (user / admin / anonymous) for FastAPI routes | `core/security.py` |
| `media/storage.py` | Safe image saving: type check by content, size cap, random file names | `core/config.py` |

**Mobile (`mobile/lib/`)**

| File | What it gives you |
|---|---|
| `core/theme.dart` | All colours (`AppColors`) and the app theme in one place |
| `core/config.dart` | The one server address |
| `services/session_store.dart` | Saved login (token kept in secure storage) |
| `services/api_client.dart` | Building URLs and reading error messages |
| `widgets/app_snackbar.dart`, `photo_pickers.dart`, `auth_dialog.dart` | Message bar, camera/gallery pop-ups, login pop-up |
| `screens/admin/...`, `screens/map/...`, `screens/profile/...` | One folder per screen: the screen file holds the state, the folder holds its pieces |

---

## 7. Folder map

```
backend/
  sabil/
    main.py            starts the app and plugs in the feature folders
    core/              settings, database, security, limits, shared helpers
    users/             accounts, leaderboard, trust rules
    coolers/           the main feature: find, add, report, photos, who-can-see-what
    sponsors/          public sponsor pages
    admin/             admin login, review queue, moderation, sponsor management
    media/             saving image files and serving them safely
  scripts/seed.py      puts sample coolers in an empty database
  tests/               automated tests (need a PostGIS database)
mobile/lib/
  main.dart            app start
  core/                colours and server address
  models/              plain data classes (one per file)
  services/            talking to the server (one file per area) + saved login
  widgets/             small pieces used on several screens
  screens/             the screens, with a sub-folder of pieces for the big ones
docs/FINDINGS_LOG.md   problems found, what was fixed, what is left
```
</document>

<document path="GETTING_STARTED.md">
# Getting Sabil running on a laptop (step by step)

This guide takes you from "nothing installed" to "the app is running on an emulator". Commands are for **Windows PowerShell**; Mac/Linux differences are noted.

**Short answer to "do I still use Docker?"** Yes, but **only for the database**. Docker runs PostgreSQL + PostGIS. The server (FastAPI) and the app (Flutter) run directly on your laptop, not in Docker.

You need three things running, each in its own terminal:

| # | What | Where it runs | Command |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Database | Docker | `docker compose up -d` |
| 2 | Server (backend) | Your laptop, in `backend/` | `uvicorn sabil.main:app --reload` |
| 3 | App | Android emulator | `flutter run` (in `mobile/`) |

---

## Part 1: One-time setup

### 1. Install the tools
- **Git**: https://git-scm.com/downloads
- **Docker Desktop**: https://www.docker.com/products/docker-desktop (start it once; wait until it says "running")
- **Python 3.11 or newer**: https://www.python.org/downloads (tick **"Add python.exe to PATH"** in the installer)
- **Flutter SDK** and **Android Studio** (for the emulator): https://docs.flutter.dev/get-started/install. Afterwards run `flutter doctor` and fix anything marked with a red cross.
- **Antigravity** (Google's AI code editor): install it from Google's site. It works like VS Code.

Check they work (each should print a version):
```powershell
git --version
docker --version
python --version
flutter --version
```

### 2. Get the code from GitHub
Pick a folder (for example `C:\Projects`) and run:
```powershell
cd C:\Projects
git clone https://github.com/iamarwan/CoolMan.git
cd CoolMan
git checkout restructure
```
`restructure` is the branch with all the new work (`main` is still the old layout). If you already have an old copy of the project, **do not clone over it**: rename the old folder first, or clone into a new one.

If Git asks you to sign in, use your GitHub account (a browser window opens).

### 3. Open it in Antigravity
1. Start Antigravity.
2. **File > Open Folder** and choose the `CoolMan` folder (the one containing `backend`, `mobile`, `docker-compose.yml`).
3. Install the extensions it suggests, or open the Extensions panel and install **Python** and **Dart** + **Flutter**.
4. Open its built-in terminal (**Terminal > New Terminal**). You will use this for the commands below; open extra terminal tabs with the **+** button.

You can also use any other terminal; Antigravity is only the editor.

### 4. Create the Python environment (once)
```powershell
cd backend
python -m venv venv
venv\Scripts\activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
```
(Mac/Linux: `source venv/bin/activate`.) Your prompt now starts with `(venv)`.

Tell Antigravity to use it: press `Ctrl+Shift+P`, type **Python: Select Interpreter**, choose the one inside `backend\venv`.

> The repo contains `.vscode/settings.json` pointing at an old `venv\Scripts\python.exe` in the repo root. It is out of date: ignore it and select the interpreter as above.

### 5. Get the Flutter packages (once)
```powershell
cd ..\mobile
flutter pub get
cd ..
```

---

## Part 2: Every time you work on the app

### Step 1: Start the database (Docker)
Make sure Docker Desktop is running, then from the repo root (the folder with `docker-compose.yml`):
```powershell
docker compose up -d
```
This starts a container called `sabil-postgis` (PostGIS 16) on **port 5433**, user `sabil`, password `sabil_pass`, database `sabil_db`. Your data is kept in a Docker volume, so it survives restarts. Stop it with `docker compose down` (data stays; add `-v` only if you want to erase it).

### Step 2: Start the server
In a new terminal:
```powershell
cd backend
venv\Scripts\activate
uvicorn sabil.main:app --reload --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload-exclude "uploads"
```
- Always start it **from the `backend` folder**, and use `sabil.main:app`. The old `main.py` in the project root no longer exists.
- Tables are created automatically on first start.
- Open http://localhost:8000/docs to see and try every endpoint.
- **First time only:** to put sample coolers in the (empty) database, run in another terminal in `backend` with the venv active: `python -m scripts.seed`

### Step 3: Start the app
1. In Android Studio open **Device Manager** and start an emulator (or run `flutter emulators --launch <id>`).
2. In the emulator's **... > Location** panel set a point in Cairo, for example latitude `29.9596`, longitude `31.2575` (Maadi), so the seeded coolers appear.
3. In a new terminal:
```powershell
cd mobile
flutter run
```
The emulator reaches your laptop's server at `10.0.2.2:8000`; that is already set in `mobile/lib/core/config.dart`.

### Using a real phone instead of the emulator
1. Phone and laptop on the same Wi-Fi.
2. Find the laptop's IP: `ipconfig` (look for IPv4, e.g. `192.168.1.20`).
3. Edit `mobile/lib/core/config.dart` and change `baseUrl` to `http://192.168.1.20:8000`.
4. Keep the server started with `--host 0.0.0.0` (as in Step 2) and allow Python through the Windows firewall if asked.
5. `flutter run` with the phone plugged in (USB debugging on).

### Admin screen
In the app, tap the app title 5 times to open the admin login. In development the login is `admin` / `admin123`.

---

## What changed compared to before

| Before | Now |
|---|---|
| `main.py`, `models.py`, etc. in the repo root | Everything in `backend/sabil/`, split by feature |
| `uvicorn main:app` | `uvicorn sabil.main:app` run **from `backend/`** |
| Photos in `uploads/` in the root | `backend/uploads/` (move old photos there, otherwise they show as missing) |
| Photos open to anyone with the link | Unapproved photos/coolers only visible to admin and the person who added them |
| `db_fix.py` for DB changes | Removed. There are no migrations yet (see below) |
| Settings hard-coded | Environment variables (table below) |

### Your existing database
The tables are created if missing, but **existing tables are never altered**. Your old database still has three columns the code no longer uses (`taste_reports`, `is_wheelchair_accessible`, `needs_urgent_refill`). They are harmless. If you ever hit a "column does not exist" error, or want a clean start, the simplest fix is to erase the database and re-seed:
```powershell
docker compose down -v
docker compose up -d
```
then restart the server and run `python -m scripts.seed`. (If you used a natively installed PostgreSQL before, you can stop using it: Docker replaces it.)

---

## Settings (environment variables)

Set them in the terminal **before** starting the server, e.g. `$env:SABIL_ENV="production"` (PowerShell). Anything unset uses the default.

| Variable | Default | What it is |
|---|---|---|
| `SABIL_DATABASE_URL` | `postgresql+asyncpg://sabil:sabil_pass@localhost:5433/sabil_db?ssl=disable` | Database connection (matches `docker-compose.yml`) |
| `SABIL_ENV` | `development` | Set to `production` on a real server: it then **refuses to start** unless `SABIL_JWT_SECRET` (32+ random characters) and `SABIL_ADMIN_PASSWORD` are set |
| `SABIL_JWT_SECRET` | `change-me-in-production` | Signs login tokens. Change on any real server |
| `SABIL_ADMIN_USERNAME` / `SABIL_ADMIN_PASSWORD` | `admin` / `admin123` | In-app admin login. Change the password on any real server |
| `SABIL_ADMIN_TOKEN_MIN` / `SABIL_USER_TOKEN_MIN` | `480` / `10080` | How long logins last, in minutes |
| `SABIL_AUTH_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `10` | Login/register attempts allowed per IP per minute |

You do not need any of these for normal development.

---

## Tests (optional)

Run against a throw-away database, never your real one. From `backend/` with the venv active:
```powershell
docker exec sabil-postgis psql -U sabil -d sabil_db -c "CREATE DATABASE sabil_test;"
pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
$env:SABIL_DATABASE_URL="postgresql+asyncpg://sabil:sabil_pass@localhost:5433/sabil_test?ssl=disable"
python -m pytest tests -q
```
(11 tests; the PostGIS extension is enabled by the test setup.)

## Making a code snapshot for an AI chat
```powershell
python tools/pack_code.py
```
creates `coolman_dump_<date>_<time>.txt` in the repo root with every source file in one text file.

---

## Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Cause | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `docker compose up` fails / "cannot connect to the Docker daemon" | Docker Desktop is not running | Start Docker Desktop, wait until it says running |
| Server error "connection refused" on port 5433 | Database container is not running | `docker compose up -d`, check with `docker ps` |
| `ModuleNotFoundError: sabil` | Started from the wrong folder | `cd backend` first, use `sabil.main:app` |
| `function st_dwithin does not exist` | PostGIS missing (not using the Docker image) | Use `docker compose up -d`, which provides PostGIS |
| App shows no coolers / network error | Server not running, or wrong address | Check http://localhost:8000/docs opens; emulator uses `10.0.2.2`; real phone needs your LAN IP |
| Old photos show as missing | `uploads/` folder moved | Copy the old `uploads` folder into `backend/` |
| Server will not start and mentions JWT secret/admin password | `SABIL_ENV=production` without the required settings | Set them, or unset `SABIL_ENV` for development |
| Login rejected after changes | Old saved token | Log out in the profile screen, or clear the app data |
| `flutter doctor` shows red crosses | Missing Android licences/SDK | `flutter doctor --android-licenses`, install missing parts in Android Studio |
</document>

<document path="README.md">
# Sabil (CoolMan)

A crowd-sourced map of public water coolers (*sabil*) in Egypt. People add coolers, report whether they work or are empty, and attach photos. An admin reviews new submissions. Users earn **trust points** for good reports; trusted users skip the review queue.

| Part | Tech | Folder |
|---|---|---|
| Phone app | Flutter (Dart), OpenStreetMap tiles | `mobile/` |
| Server | FastAPI + async SQLAlchemy | `backend/` |
| Database | PostgreSQL with PostGIS (distance queries) | `docker-compose.yml` |

## Run it

Step-by-step instructions (database, server, app, settings) are in **[GETTING_STARTED.md](GETTING_STARTED.md)**. The short version:

```bash
docker compose up -d                      # database
cd backend
pip install -r requirements.txt
uvicorn sabil.main:app --reload           # server on http://localhost:8000  (docs at /docs)
cd ../mobile && flutter run               # the app
```

## Where to look

| I want to... | Read |
|---|---|
| Understand how the code is organised, file by file | [DOCUMENTATION.md](DOCUMENTATION.md) |
| Reuse a piece in another project | "Reusable pieces" in [DOCUMENTATION.md](DOCUMENTATION.md) |
| See known problems and what has been fixed | [docs/FINDINGS_LOG.md](docs/FINDINGS_LOG.md) |
| Read the product plan | [BUSINESS_STRATEGY.md](BUSINESS_STRATEGY.md) |

## Tests

```bash
cd backend
pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
SABIL_DATABASE_URL="postgresql+asyncpg://sabil:sabil_pass@localhost:5433/sabil_test?ssl=disable" python -m pytest tests -q
```

GitHub also runs `flutter analyze` on every push (`.github/workflows/flutter-analyze.yml`).
</document>

<document path="docker-compose.yml">
services:
  db:
    image: postgis/postgis:16-3.4
    container_name: sabil-postgis
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: sabil
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: sabil_pass
      POSTGRES_DB: sabil_db
    ports:
      - "5433:5432"
    volumes:
      - sabil_pgdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data

volumes:
  sabil_pgdata:
</document>

<document path=".github/workflows/flutter-analyze.yml">
# Runs `flutter analyze` on every push, so the Flutter code is checked
# automatically even when nobody has Flutter installed locally.
name: Flutter analyze

on:
  push:
    paths: ['mobile/**', '.github/workflows/flutter-analyze.yml']
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  analyze:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: mobile
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
        with:
          channel: stable
          cache: true
      - run: flutter pub get
      - name: flutter analyze (problems also shown as annotations)
        run: |
          set +e
          flutter analyze --no-pub > analyze.txt 2>&1
          code=$?
          cat analyze.txt
          awk -F ' • ' '/^ *(error|warning|info) • /{
            sev=$1; gsub(/ /,"",sev); split($3,loc,":");
            lvl=(sev=="error")?"error":(sev=="warning"?"warning":"notice");
            printf "::%s file=mobile/%s,line=%s,col=%s::%s (%s)\n", lvl, loc[1], loc[2], loc[3], $2, $4 }' analyze.txt
          if [ $code -ne 0 ]; then echo "::error title=analyze-raw::$(head -c 3500 analyze.txt | tr '\n' '~' | sed 's/%/%25/g')"; fi
          exit $code
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/main.py">
# main.py
#
# The entry point. It builds the FastAPI app and plugs in the feature routers.
# All real logic lives in the feature folders:
#
#   users/     register, login, leaderboard, trust rules
#   coolers/   find coolers, add one, report status, photos
#   sponsors/  public sponsor pages
#   admin/     admin login, review queue, moderation, sponsor management
#   core/      settings, database, security, rate limiting (shared by all)
#   media/     saving/deleting uploaded image files
#
# Run it from the backend/ folder:   uvicorn sabil.main:app --reload

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from sabil.admin.router import login_router as admin_login_router
from sabil.admin.router import router as admin_router
from sabil.coolers.router import router as coolers_router
from sabil.core.config import check_settings
from sabil.core.database import init_db
from sabil.media.router import router as media_router
from sabil.media.storage import ensure_upload_dirs
from sabil.sponsors.router import router as sponsors_router
from sabil.users.router import router as users_router


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    check_settings()  # production: refuse to start with default secrets
    await init_db()  # runs once at startup: PostGIS extension + create missing tables
    yield


app = FastAPI(title="Sabil / CoolMan", version="2.0.0", lifespan=lifespan)

# CORS: allow Flutter web + Android emulator + local dev
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],          # tighten in production
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# Uploaded photos are served by media_router at /uploads/photos/<file>, with an
# approval check (not as open static files).
ensure_upload_dirs()

app.include_router(users_router)
app.include_router(coolers_router)
app.include_router(sponsors_router)
app.include_router(admin_login_router)
app.include_router(admin_router)
app.include_router(media_router)
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/admin/coolers.py">
# admin/coolers.py
#
# Admin: the review queue and cooler moderation.
# Mounted under /admin by admin/router.py, which also requires an admin token.

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy import and_, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto, WaterCooler
from sabil.coolers.schemas import StatusUpdate, WaterCoolerRead
from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.lookup import get_or_404
from sabil.core.rate_limit import reset_confirm_cooldown
from sabil.media.storage import delete_image
from sabil.users.models import User
from sabil.users.trust import APPROVE_REWARD, REJECT_PENALTY, STATUS_UPDATE_REWARD, reward

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/coolers/pending", response_model=list[WaterCoolerRead])
async def get_pending_coolers(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Review queue: new coolers, proposed status changes, and coolers with unreviewed photos."""
    stmt = select(WaterCooler).where(
        or_(
            and_(WaterCooler.is_approved == False, WaterCooler.is_rejected == False),  # noqa: E712
            WaterCooler.pending_status.is_not(None),
            WaterCooler.photos.any(CoolerPhoto.is_approved == False),  # noqa: E712
        )
    )
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


@router.get("/coolers/current", response_model=list[WaterCoolerRead])
async def get_current_coolers(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Every approved (live) cooler."""
    stmt = select(WaterCooler).where(WaterCooler.is_approved == True)  # noqa: E712
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


@router.patch("/coolers/{cooler_id}/approve")
async def approve_cooler(cooler_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Approve a new cooler and/or a proposed status change."""
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")

    if cooler.pending_status:
        reporter = (
            await db.get(User, cooler.pending_status_user_id)
            if cooler.pending_status_user_id
            else None
        )
        if reporter:
            reward(reporter, STATUS_UPDATE_REWARD)  # paid now that it is approved
        cooler.status = cooler.pending_status
        cooler.pending_status = None
        cooler.pending_status_user_id = None
        reset_confirm_cooldown(cooler_id)

    if not cooler.is_approved and not cooler.is_rejected:
        cooler.is_approved = True
        for photo in cooler.photos:
            photo.is_approved = True
        if cooler.user_id:
            submitter = await get_or_404(db, User, cooler.user_id, "User")
            reward(submitter, APPROVE_REWARD)

    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Approved"}


@router.patch("/coolers/{cooler_id}/reject")
async def reject_cooler(cooler_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")

    if not cooler.is_approved and not cooler.is_rejected and cooler.user_id:
        submitter = await get_or_404(db, User, cooler.user_id, "User")
        reward(submitter, REJECT_PENALTY)

    cooler.is_rejected = True
    cooler.is_approved = False
    cooler.pending_status = None
    cooler.pending_status_user_id = None
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Rejected"}


@router.patch("/coolers/{cooler_id}/reject-status")
async def reject_status_change(cooler_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Throw away a user's proposed status change."""
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    cooler.pending_status = None
    cooler.pending_status_user_id = None
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Status change rejected"}


@router.patch("/coolers/{cooler_id}/force-status")
async def admin_force_status(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    payload: StatusUpdate,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """Set the status directly, with no review."""
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    cooler.status = payload.status
    cooler.pending_status = None
    cooler.pending_status_user_id = None
    cooler.last_verified_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    reset_confirm_cooldown(cooler_id)
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Status forcefully updated"}


@router.delete("/coolers/{cooler_id}")
async def delete_cooler(cooler_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    photo_paths = [p.file_path for p in cooler.photos]
    await db.delete(cooler)
    await db.commit()
    for path in photo_paths:  # remove the image files too, after the rows are gone
        delete_image(path)
    return {"message": "Deleted"}
</document>

# admin/photos.py
#
# Admin: photo moderation. Mounted under /admin by admin/router.py.

import uuid

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, Form, UploadFile
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto
from sabil.coolers.photos import add_photo
from sabil.coolers.schemas import CoolerPhotoRead
from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.lookup import get_or_404
from sabil.media.storage import delete_image

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/coolers/{cooler_id}/photos", response_model=list[CoolerPhotoRead])
async def get_cooler_photos_admin(cooler_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """All photos of a cooler, approved or not."""
    stmt = select(CoolerPhoto).where(CoolerPhoto.cooler_id == cooler_id)
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


@router.post("/coolers/{cooler_id}/photos", response_model=CoolerPhotoRead, status_code=201)
async def admin_upload_cooler_photo(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    photo_category: str = Form("Cooler"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """Admin-uploaded photos are approved immediately."""
    return await add_photo(db, cooler_id, file, photo_category, approved=True)


@router.patch("/photos/{photo_id}/approve")
async def approve_photo(photo_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    photo = await get_or_404(db, CoolerPhoto, photo_id, "Photo")
    photo.is_approved = True
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Photo approved"}


@router.delete("/photos/{photo_id}")
async def delete_photo(photo_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    photo = await get_or_404(db, CoolerPhoto, photo_id, "Photo")
    delete_image(photo.file_path)
    await db.delete(photo)
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Photo deleted"}

<document path="backend/sabil/admin/router.py">
# admin/router.py
#
# Puts the admin API together.
#
#   login_router  POST /auth/admin/login   (open to everyone; it hands out the admin token)
#   router        everything under /admin  (the dependency below makes EVERY route in it
#                                           require a valid admin token)
#
# The actual routes are in admin/coolers.py, admin/photos.py and admin/sponsors.py.

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException

from sabil.admin import coolers, photos, sponsors
from sabil.admin.schemas import AdminLoginRequest
from sabil.core.dependencies import require_admin
from sabil.core.rate_limit import auth_rate_limiter
from sabil.core.security import authenticate_admin, create_admin_token

login_router = APIRouter(tags=["admin"])


@login_router.post("/auth/admin/login", dependencies=[Depends(auth_rate_limiter)])
async def admin_login(payload: AdminLoginRequest):
    if not authenticate_admin(payload.username, payload.password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid credentials.")
    return {"access_token": create_admin_token(payload.username)}


router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["admin"], dependencies=[Depends(require_admin)])
router.include_router(coolers.router)
router.include_router(photos.router)
router.include_router(sponsors.router)
</document>

# admin/schemas.py

from pydantic import BaseModel


class AdminLoginRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str
</document>

# admin/sponsors.py
#
# Admin: create, verify and delete sponsors, and attach them to coolers.
# Mounted under /admin by admin/router.py.

import uuid

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.models import WaterCooler
from sabil.coolers.schemas import WaterCoolerRead
from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.lookup import get_or_404
from sabil.sponsors.models import Sponsor
from sabil.sponsors.schemas import SponsorAssign, SponsorCreate, SponsorRead

router = APIRouter()


@router.post("/sponsors", response_model=SponsorRead, status_code=201)
async def create_sponsor(payload: SponsorCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    sponsor = Sponsor(
        name=payload.name,
        sponsor_type=payload.sponsor_type,
        contact=payload.contact,
    )
    db.add(sponsor)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(sponsor)
    return sponsor


@router.patch("/sponsors/{sponsor_id}/verify", response_model=SponsorRead)
async def verify_sponsor(sponsor_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    sponsor = await get_or_404(db, Sponsor, sponsor_id, "Sponsor")
    sponsor.verified = True
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(sponsor)
    return sponsor


@router.delete("/sponsors/{sponsor_id}")
async def delete_sponsor(sponsor_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Delete a sponsor; the coolers they sponsored stay, just without a sponsor."""
    sponsor = await get_or_404(db, Sponsor, sponsor_id, "Sponsor")
    sponsored = await db.execute(select(WaterCooler).where(WaterCooler.sponsor_id == sponsor.id))
    for cooler in sponsored.scalars().all():
        cooler.sponsor_id = None
    await db.delete(sponsor)
    await db.commit()
    return {"message": "Sponsor deleted"}


@router.patch("/coolers/{cooler_id}/assign-sponsor", response_model=WaterCoolerRead)
async def assign_sponsor(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    payload: SponsorAssign,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    sponsor = await get_or_404(db, Sponsor, payload.sponsor_id, "Sponsor")
    cooler.sponsor_id = sponsor.id
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(cooler)
    return cooler


@router.delete("/coolers/{cooler_id}/assign-sponsor", response_model=WaterCoolerRead)
async def remove_sponsor(cooler_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    cooler.sponsor_id = None
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(cooler)
    return cooler
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/coolers/access.py">
# coolers/access.py
#
# Who may SEE a cooler or a photo. The rule is the same everywhere:
#   - approved things are public
#   - unapproved things are visible only to admins and to the person who
#     submitted them
# Anyone else gets "404 not found" (not 403), so ids cannot be probed.

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto, WaterCooler
from sabil.core.dependencies import Viewer


def can_view_cooler(cooler: WaterCooler, viewer: Viewer) -> bool:
    if cooler.is_approved or viewer.is_admin:
        return True
    return viewer.user_id is not None and viewer.user_id == cooler.user_id


def can_view_photo(photo: CoolerPhoto, cooler: WaterCooler, viewer: Viewer) -> bool:
    """A photo is public only when BOTH the photo and its cooler are approved."""
    if photo.is_approved and cooler.is_approved:
        return True
    if viewer.is_admin:
        return True
    return viewer.user_id is not None and viewer.user_id == photo.user_id
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/coolers/models.py">
# coolers/models.py
#
# The `water_coolers` and `cooler_photos` tables, plus the choice lists (enums)
# they use.

import enum
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Optional

from geoalchemy2 import Geometry
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, Enum as SAEnum, ForeignKey, Index, Numeric, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from sabil.core.database import Base

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from sabil.sponsors.models import Sponsor
    from sabil.users.models import User


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# ENUMS  (a fixed list of allowed values)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

class CoolerStatus(str, enum.Enum):
    Working = "Working"
    Empty = "Empty"
    Broken = "Broken"


class CupsType(str, enum.Enum):
    Communal = "Communal"
    Disposable = "Disposable"


class PhotoCategory(str, enum.Enum):
    Cooler = "Cooler"
    Cups = "Cups"


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# MODELS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

class WaterCooler(Base):
    __tablename__ = "water_coolers"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_wc_approved_status", "is_approved", "status"),
        Index("ix_wc_geom", "geom", postgresql_using="gist"),
    )

    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    latitude: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Numeric(10, 7), nullable=False)
    longitude: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Numeric(10, 7), nullable=False)
    geom: Mapped[object] = mapped_column(
        Geometry(geometry_type="POINT", srid=4326), nullable=False
    )
    status: Mapped[CoolerStatus] = mapped_column(
        SAEnum(CoolerStatus), nullable=False, default=CoolerStatus.Working
    )
    last_verified_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )
    is_approved: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="false")
    is_rejected: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="false")
    pending_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String, nullable=True)
    has_cups: Mapped[Optional[bool]] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    cups_type: Mapped[Optional[CupsType]] = mapped_column(SAEnum(CupsType), nullable=True)

    # Reserved for the IoT roadmap (see BUSINESS_STRATEGY.md); nothing writes it yet.
    iot_weight_kg: Mapped[Optional[float]] = mapped_column(Numeric(5, 2), nullable=True)

    user_id: Mapped[Optional[uuid.UUID]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    pending_status_user_id: Mapped[Optional[uuid.UUID]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    sponsor_id: Mapped[Optional[uuid.UUID]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("sponsors.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )

    user: Mapped[Optional["User"]] = relationship(
        "User",
        back_populates="coolers",
        foreign_keys=[user_id],
    )
    pending_status_user: Mapped[Optional["User"]] = relationship(
        "User",
        foreign_keys=[pending_status_user_id],
    )
    sponsor: Mapped[Optional["Sponsor"]] = relationship(
        "Sponsor", back_populates="coolers", lazy="selectin"
    )
    # cascade: deleting a cooler also deletes its photo rows (the files on disk
    # are removed by the admin delete route).
    photos: Mapped[list["CoolerPhoto"]] = relationship(
        "CoolerPhoto",
        back_populates="cooler",
        lazy="selectin",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )


class CoolerPhoto(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cooler_photos"

    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    cooler_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("water_coolers.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    file_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=False)
    uploaded_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )
    is_approved: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="false")
    photo_category: Mapped[PhotoCategory] = mapped_column(
        SAEnum(PhotoCategory), nullable=False, default=PhotoCategory.Cooler
    )
    user_id: Mapped[Optional[uuid.UUID]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )

    cooler: Mapped["WaterCooler"] = relationship("WaterCooler", back_populates="photos")
    user: Mapped[Optional["User"]] = relationship("User", back_populates="photos")
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/coolers/photos.py">
# coolers/photos.py
#
# Attaching a photo to a cooler. Used by BOTH the user upload route
# (coolers/router.py) and the admin upload route (admin/photos.py), so the
# validation and saving logic exists only once.

import uuid

from fastapi import HTTPException, UploadFile
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto, PhotoCategory, WaterCooler
from sabil.core.lookup import get_or_404
from sabil.media.storage import save_image


def parse_category(value: str) -> PhotoCategory:
    """Turn the text sent by the app into a PhotoCategory (unknown text -> Cooler)."""
    try:
        return PhotoCategory(value)
    except ValueError:
        return PhotoCategory.Cooler


async def add_photo(
    db: AsyncSession,
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    file: UploadFile,
    photo_category: str,
    *,
    user_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
    approved: bool = False,
) -> CoolerPhoto:
    """Save the file, create its database row and return it.

    Users' photos start unapproved; pass approved=True for admin uploads.
    """
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")

    try:
        url_path = save_image(file, str(cooler_id))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    photo = CoolerPhoto(
        cooler_id=cooler.id,
        file_path=url_path,
        photo_category=parse_category(photo_category),
        user_id=user_id,
        is_approved=approved,
    )
    db.add(photo)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(photo)
    return photo
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/coolers/router.py">
# coolers/router.py
#
# The public cooler API: find coolers, add one, report its status, add photos.
# (Admin-only cooler actions are in admin/coolers.py.)
#
# IMPORTANT: FastAPI matches routes top to bottom. "/coolers/my", "/nearby" and
# "/search" must stay ABOVE "/coolers/{cooler_id}", otherwise "{cooler_id}"
# would swallow them.

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, Form, HTTPException, Query, UploadFile
from geoalchemy2 import Geography
from geoalchemy2.elements import WKTElement
from geoalchemy2.functions import ST_DWithin, ST_MakePoint, ST_SetSRID
from sqlalchemy import cast, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto, WaterCooler
from sabil.coolers.photos import add_photo
from sabil.coolers.schemas import (
    CoolerPhotoRead,
    StatusUpdate,
    WaterCoolerCreate,
    WaterCoolerRead,
)
from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.coolers.access import can_view_cooler
from sabil.core.dependencies import Viewer, get_current_user, get_viewer
from sabil.core.lookup import get_or_404
from sabil.core.rate_limit import (
    check_status_cooldown,
    record_status_report,
    check_confirm_cooldown,
    photo_rate_limiter,
    rate_limiter,
    record_confirm,
    reset_confirm_cooldown,
)
from sabil.users.models import User
from sabil.users.trust import (
    APPROVE_REWARD,
    CONFIRM_REWARD,
    STATUS_UPDATE_REWARD,
    is_trusted,
    reward,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/coolers", tags=["coolers"])


def _within_meters(lat: float, lng: float, meters: float):
    """SQL condition: the cooler is within `meters` of this point (true distance on the globe)."""
    point = ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(lng, lat), 4326)
    return ST_DWithin(
        cast(WaterCooler.geom, Geography(geometry_type="POINT", srid=4326)),
        cast(point, Geography(geometry_type="POINT", srid=4326)),
        meters,
        use_spheroid=True,
    )


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# FINDING COOLERS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

@router.get("/my", response_model=list[WaterCoolerRead])
async def get_my_coolers(
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """Coolers I submitted, or where I proposed a status change (My Submissions screen)."""
    stmt = (
        select(WaterCooler)
        .where(
            or_(
                WaterCooler.user_id == current_user.id,
                WaterCooler.pending_status_user_id == current_user.id,
            )
        )
        .order_by(WaterCooler.last_verified_at.desc())
    )
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


@router.get("/nearby", response_model=list[WaterCoolerRead])
async def get_nearby_coolers(
    lat: float = Query(..., ge=-90, le=90),
    lng: float = Query(..., ge=-180, le=180),
    radius_km: float = Query(5.0, gt=0, le=50),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    stmt = select(WaterCooler).where(
        WaterCooler.is_approved == True,  # noqa: E712  (SQLAlchemy needs == here)
        _within_meters(lat, lng, radius_km * 1000),
    )
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


@router.get("/search", response_model=list[WaterCoolerRead])
async def search_coolers(
    q: str = Query(..., min_length=1, max_length=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    term = f"%{q.strip()}%"
    stmt = (
        select(WaterCooler)
        .where(WaterCooler.is_approved == True, WaterCooler.name.ilike(term))  # noqa: E712
        .limit(30)
    )
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


@router.get("/{cooler_id}", response_model=WaterCoolerRead)
async def get_cooler(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    viewer: Viewer = Depends(get_viewer),
):
    """Public for approved coolers; unapproved ones only for admins and the submitter."""
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    if not can_view_cooler(cooler, viewer):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Cooler not found")
    return cooler


@router.get("/{cooler_id}/photos", response_model=list[CoolerPhotoRead])
async def get_cooler_photos_public(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    viewer: Viewer = Depends(get_viewer),
):
    """Approved photos only. Used by the app to show a cooler's gallery."""
    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    if not can_view_cooler(cooler, viewer):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Cooler not found")
    stmt = select(CoolerPhoto).where(
        CoolerPhoto.cooler_id == cooler_id,
        CoolerPhoto.is_approved == True,  # noqa: E712
    )
    return (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REPORTING ON A COOLER
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

@router.patch("/{cooler_id}/status", response_model=WaterCoolerRead)
async def update_cooler_status(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    payload: StatusUpdate,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """Report a new status. Trusted users change it at once; others wait for an admin."""
    check_status_cooldown(current_user.id, cooler_id)

    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    if not cooler.is_approved:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cooler is not yet approved.")
    if payload.status == cooler.status and not cooler.pending_status:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="The cooler already has this status. Use \"Still working\" to confirm it.",
        )

    if is_trusted(current_user):
        cooler.status = payload.status
        cooler.pending_status = None
        cooler.pending_status_user_id = None
        cooler.last_verified_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        reset_confirm_cooldown(cooler_id)
        reward(current_user, STATUS_UPDATE_REWARD)  # applied at once, so paid at once
    else:
        # Points for this are paid when an admin approves it (admin/coolers.py).
        cooler.pending_status = payload.status.value
        cooler.pending_status_user_id = current_user.id

    record_status_report(current_user.id, cooler_id)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(cooler)
    return cooler


@router.post("/{cooler_id}/confirm")
async def confirm_cooler_working(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """The 'Still working' button: refreshes the cooler's last-verified time."""
    check_confirm_cooldown(current_user.id, cooler_id)

    cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    if not cooler.is_approved:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cooler is not yet approved.")

    cooler.last_verified_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    reward(current_user, CONFIRM_REWARD)

    await db.commit()
    record_confirm(current_user.id, cooler_id)
    await db.refresh(current_user)
    return {"message": "Confirmed", "trust_score": current_user.trust_score}


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# ADDING COOLERS AND PHOTOS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=WaterCoolerRead,
    status_code=201,
    dependencies=[Depends(rate_limiter)],
)
async def create_cooler(
    payload: WaterCoolerCreate,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """Submit a new cooler. Refuses a spot that already has one within 15 metres."""
    already_there = await db.execute(
        select(WaterCooler.id).where(_within_meters(payload.latitude, payload.longitude, 15.0)).limit(1)
    )
    if already_there.first() is not None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="A water cooler has already been reported at this exact location.",
        )

    auto_approved = is_trusted(current_user)

    cooler = WaterCooler(
        name=payload.name,
        latitude=payload.latitude,
        longitude=payload.longitude,
        geom=WKTElement(f"POINT({payload.longitude} {payload.latitude})", srid=4326),
        status=payload.status,
        is_approved=auto_approved,
        has_cups=payload.has_cups,
        cups_type=payload.cups_type,
        user_id=current_user.id,
    )
    db.add(cooler)

    if auto_approved:
        reward(current_user, APPROVE_REWARD)

    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(cooler)
    return cooler


@router.post(
    "/{cooler_id}/photos",
    response_model=CoolerPhotoRead,
    status_code=201,
    dependencies=[Depends(photo_rate_limiter)],
)
async def upload_cooler_photo(
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID,
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    photo_category: str = Form("Cooler"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    """Add a photo. It stays hidden until an admin approves it."""
    return await add_photo(db, cooler_id, file, photo_category, user_id=current_user.id)

<document path="backend/sabil/coolers/schemas.py">
# coolers/schemas.py

import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerStatus, CupsType, PhotoCategory
from sabil.core.validation import sanitize_text
from sabil.sponsors.schemas import SponsorRead


class WaterCoolerCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=255)
    latitude: float = Field(..., ge=-90, le=90)
    longitude: float = Field(..., ge=-180, le=180)
    status: CoolerStatus = CoolerStatus.Working
    has_cups: Optional[bool] = None
    cups_type: Optional[CupsType] = None

    @field_validator("name")
    def validate_name(cls, v):
        return sanitize_text(v)


class WaterCoolerUpdate(BaseModel):
    # NOT USED YET: no route edits a cooler's details. Kept until it is decided
    # whether to build that edit endpoint or delete this class.
    name: Optional[str] = Field(None, min_length=1, max_length=255)
    status: Optional[CoolerStatus] = None
    latitude: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=-90, le=90)
    longitude: Optional[float] = Field(None, ge=-180, le=180)
    has_cups: Optional[bool] = None
    cups_type: Optional[CupsType] = None

    @field_validator("name")
    def validate_name(cls, v):
        if v is not None:
            return sanitize_text(v)
        return v


class StatusUpdate(BaseModel):
    """Body of PATCH /coolers/{id}/status and PATCH /admin/coolers/{id}/force-status."""
    status: CoolerStatus


class CoolerPhotoRead(BaseModel):
    id: uuid.UUID
    cooler_id: uuid.UUID
    file_path: str
    uploaded_at: datetime
    is_approved: bool
    photo_category: PhotoCategory

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)


class WaterCoolerRead(BaseModel):
    id: uuid.UUID
    name: str
    latitude: float
    longitude: float
    status: CoolerStatus
    last_verified_at: datetime
    created_at: datetime
    is_approved: bool
    is_rejected: bool
    pending_status: str | None = None
    has_cups: bool | None = None
    cups_type: CupsType | None = None

    iot_weight_kg: float | None = None

    user_id: uuid.UUID | None = None          # who submitted it
    pending_status_user_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
    sponsor: SponsorRead | None = None
    photos: list[CoolerPhotoRead] = []

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

# core/all_models.py

# SQLAlchemy only knows about a table once its model class has been imported.
# Models refer to each other by name (e.g. relationship("WaterCooler")), so ALL
# of them must be imported before the first query. Importing this one file does
# exactly that. If you add a new model file, add it here.

from sabil.users.models import User  # noqa: F401
from sabil.sponsors.models import Sponsor, SponsorType  # noqa: F401
from sabil.coolers.models import (  # noqa: F401
    WaterCooler,
    CoolerPhoto,
    CoolerStatus,
    CupsType,
