<document path="backend/sabil/core/config.py">
# core/config.py
#
# Every setting and tunable number lives in THIS file, so you never have to
# hunt through the code to find "where is the timeout / secret / limit set?".
#
# Values that differ between your laptop and a real server are read from
# environment variables (os.getenv). The second argument is the default that is
# used when the variable is not set.

import os
import logging

# SABIL_ENV=production makes the server REFUSE to start with unsafe settings
# (see check_settings at the bottom). Anything else counts as development.
ENVIRONMENT = os.getenv("SABIL_ENV", "development").strip().lower()
IS_PRODUCTION = ENVIRONMENT == "production"

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# DATABASE
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv(
    "SABIL_DATABASE_URL",
    "postgresql+asyncpg://sabil:sabil_pass@localhost:5433/sabil_db?ssl=disable",
)

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# LOGIN TOKENS (JWT)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

# WARNING: the defaults below are for local development only.
# Set SABIL_JWT_SECRET / SABIL_ADMIN_PASSWORD in any real deployment.
DEFAULT_JWT_SECRET = "change-me-in-production"
JWT_SECRET = os.getenv("SABIL_JWT_SECRET", DEFAULT_JWT_SECRET)
JWT_ISSUER = "sabil-api"

JWT_AUDIENCE_ADMIN = "sabil-admin"
JWT_EXPIRES_ADMIN_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("SABIL_ADMIN_TOKEN_MIN", "480"))        # 8 hours

JWT_AUDIENCE_USER = "sabil-user"
JWT_EXPIRES_USER_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("SABIL_USER_TOKEN_MIN", "10080"))        # 7 days

ADMIN_USERNAME = os.getenv("SABIL_ADMIN_USERNAME", "admin")
DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD = "admin123"
ADMIN_PASSWORD = os.getenv("SABIL_ADMIN_PASSWORD", DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD)

PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS = 260_000

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# FILE UPLOADS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

# Folders are relative to the directory you start the server from (backend/).
UPLOAD_ROOT = "uploads"                       # served publicly at /uploads
PHOTO_DIR = os.path.join(UPLOAD_ROOT, "photos")
PHOTO_URL_PREFIX = "/uploads/photos"          # what gets stored in the database

# Largest accepted upload. The app shrinks photos to about 1920 px before
# sending, which is far below this.
MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024  # 8 MB

# Browser/phone-reported content type -> file extension we save it with.
ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = {
    "image/jpeg": "jpg",
    "image/png": "png",
    "image/webp": "webp",
}

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# ABUSE PROTECTION
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

# Submission rate limit: at most N create/upload requests per IP per window.
RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS = 3
RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS = 60.0

# Photo uploads have their own, higher limit: adding one cooler with 5 photos
# is 1 create + 5 uploads, and the photos must not be refused halfway.
PHOTO_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS = 20
PHOTO_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS = 60.0

# Login protection: at most N login/register attempts per IP per window.
# Counted separately from the submission limit above.
AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS = int(os.getenv("SABIL_AUTH_MAX_ATTEMPTS", "10"))
AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS = 60.0

# A user may report a status change on the same cooler once per this many
# seconds (stops point-farming by repeating the same report).
STATUS_REPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 600.0        # 10 minutes

# A user may press "Still working" on the same cooler once per this many seconds.
CONFIRM_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 3600.0             # 1 hour


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# STARTUP SAFETY CHECK
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

MIN_SECRET_LENGTH = 32


def settings_problems() -> list[str]:
    """Return a list of unsafe settings (empty list = all good)."""
    problems = []
    if JWT_SECRET == DEFAULT_JWT_SECRET or len(JWT_SECRET) < MIN_SECRET_LENGTH:
        problems.append(
            f"SABIL_JWT_SECRET must be set to a random value of at least "
            f"{MIN_SECRET_LENGTH} characters."
        )
    if ADMIN_PASSWORD == DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD:
        problems.append("SABIL_ADMIN_PASSWORD must be set (the default is public).")
    return problems


def check_settings() -> None:
    """Called once at startup. Production: refuse to start. Development: warn."""
    problems = settings_problems()
    if not problems:
        return
    if IS_PRODUCTION:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Refusing to start with unsafe settings:\n  - " + "\n  - ".join(problems)
        )
    logging.getLogger("sabil").warning(
        "Unsafe default settings (fine for local development only): %s",
        " ".join(problems),
    )
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/core/database.py">
# core/database.py
#
# Connects to PostgreSQL and hands out database sessions.
#
#   engine         the connection pool to the database
#   Base           the parent class of every model (every table class inherits it)
#   get_db()       a FastAPI dependency: gives each request its own session
#   init_db()      creates the PostGIS extension and any missing tables

from typing import AsyncGenerator

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import create_async_engine, async_sessionmaker, AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase

from sabil.core.config import DATABASE_URL

engine = create_async_engine(
    DATABASE_URL,
    echo=False,
    pool_pre_ping=True,          # detect stale connections
    pool_size=10,
    max_overflow=20,
)
AsyncSessionLocal = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


async def get_db() -> AsyncGenerator[AsyncSession, None]:
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
        yield session


async def init_db() -> None:
    """Create the PostGIS extension and any tables that do not exist yet.

    Used by the server on startup and by scripts/seed.py.
    """
    # Imported here (not at the top) because the model files import `Base`
    # from THIS file; importing them at the top would be circular.
    import sabil.core.all_models  # noqa: F401  (registers every table with Base)

    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.execute(text("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgis"))
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/core/dependencies.py">
# core/dependencies.py
#
# FastAPI "dependencies": small functions that FastAPI runs BEFORE a route.
# A route lists them with Depends(...), for example:
#
#     async def create_cooler(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)): ...
#
# FastAPI calls get_current_user first and passes the result in. If it raises an
# error the route never runs.

import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.security import TokenError, decode_admin_token, decode_user_token
from sabil.users.models import User

# Reads the "Authorization: Bearer <token>" header. auto_error=False lets us
# send our own error message when it is missing.
bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


def _bearer_token(credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None) -> str:
    if credentials is None or credentials.scheme.lower() != "bearer":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Missing bearer token.")
    return credentials.credentials


def _http_error(exc: TokenError) -> HTTPException:
    code = status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN if exc.wrong_role else status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED
    return HTTPException(status_code=code, detail=exc.detail)


def require_admin(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(bearer_scheme),
) -> dict:
    """Allow the request only if it carries a valid ADMIN token."""
    token = _bearer_token(credentials)
    try:
        return decode_admin_token(token)
    except TokenError as exc:
        raise _http_error(exc) from exc


async def get_current_user(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> User:
    """Return the logged-in User for this request, or answer 401."""
    token = _bearer_token(credentials)
    try:
        payload = decode_user_token(token)
    except TokenError as exc:
        raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    try:
        user_id = uuid.UUID(payload.get("sub"))
    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid token subject.")

    user = await db.get(User, user_id)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="User not found.")
    return user


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# "WHO IS ASKING?" (for pages that anyone may open, but show more to some people)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

@dataclass
class Viewer:
    """The person making a request. Both fields empty = an anonymous visitor."""

    user_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
    is_admin: bool = False


def get_viewer(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer_scheme),
) -> Viewer:
    """Identify the caller if a valid token was sent. Never raises: a missing
    or bad token simply means "anonymous"."""
    if credentials is None or credentials.scheme.lower() != "bearer":
        return Viewer()
    token = credentials.credentials
    try:
        decode_admin_token(token)
        return Viewer(is_admin=True)
    except TokenError:
        pass
    try:
        return Viewer(user_id=uuid.UUID(decode_user_token(token).get("sub")))
    except (TokenError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
        return Viewer()
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/core/lookup.py">
# core/lookup.py
#
# One helper for the "find this row by id or answer 404" pattern that every
# route needs.

import uuid
from typing import TypeVar

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

T = TypeVar("T")


async def get_or_404(db: AsyncSession, model: type[T], row_id: uuid.UUID, label: str) -> T:
    """Return the row of `model` with this id, or raise "<label> not found" (404).

        cooler = await get_or_404(db, WaterCooler, cooler_id, "Cooler")
    """
    row = await db.get(model, row_id)
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"{label} not found")
    return row
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/core/rate_limit.py">
# core/rate_limit.py
#
# Two small in-memory abuse limiters.
#
# NOTE: both keep their state in a Python dictionary, so it is forgotten when
# the server restarts and is NOT shared between several server processes. That
# is fine for one server; for several, move this state into Redis or the database.

import time
import uuid
from collections import defaultdict

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request

from sabil.core.config import (
    AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
    AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS,
    CONFIRM_COOLDOWN_SECONDS,
    PHOTO_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS,
    PHOTO_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS,
    RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS,
    RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS,
    STATUS_REPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS,
)

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 1) SUBMISSION RATE LIMIT  (per IP address)
#    Use it on a route with: dependencies=[Depends(rate_limiter)]
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

_requests_by_ip: dict[str, list[float]] = defaultdict(list)

# Photo uploads (per IP), separate from creating coolers.
_photo_uploads_by_ip: dict[str, list[float]] = defaultdict(list)

# Login / register attempts (per IP). Separate from the submission limit so a
# person adding a cooler is never blocked from logging in, and vice versa.
_auth_attempts_by_ip: dict[str, list[float]] = defaultdict(list)


def _check_ip_limit(
    store: dict[str, list[float]],
    request: Request,
    max_requests: int,
    window_seconds: float,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    host = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
    now = time.time()
    window_start = now - window_seconds
    store[host] = [t for t in store[host] if t > window_start]
    if len(store[host]) >= max_requests:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail=message)
    store[host].append(now)


async def rate_limiter(request: Request) -> None:
    _check_ip_limit(
        _requests_by_ip,
        request,
        RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS,
        RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS,
        "Too many requests. Please wait before submitting again.",
    )


async def photo_rate_limiter(request: Request) -> None:
    _check_ip_limit(
        _photo_uploads_by_ip,
        request,
        PHOTO_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_REQUESTS,
        PHOTO_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS,
        "Too many photo uploads. Please wait a minute and try again.",
    )


async def auth_rate_limiter(request: Request) -> None:
    """Use on login/register routes to slow down password guessing."""
    _check_ip_limit(
        _auth_attempts_by_ip,
        request,
        AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
        AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS,
        "Too many login attempts. Please wait a minute and try again.",
    )


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 2) "STILL WORKING" CONFIRM COOLDOWN  (per user + cooler)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

# (user id, cooler id) -> time of that user's last confirmation of that cooler
_last_confirm: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID], float] = {}


def check_confirm_cooldown(user_id: uuid.UUID, cooler_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    """Raise 429 if this user confirmed this cooler too recently."""
    elapsed = time.time() - _last_confirm.get((user_id, cooler_id), 0.0)
    if elapsed < CONFIRM_COOLDOWN_SECONDS:
        remaining_minutes = int((CONFIRM_COOLDOWN_SECONDS - elapsed) / 60)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=429,
            detail=(
                f"You can confirm this cooler again in {remaining_minutes} "
                f"minute{'s' if remaining_minutes != 1 else ''}."
            ),
        )


def record_confirm(user_id: uuid.UUID, cooler_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    _last_confirm[(user_id, cooler_id)] = time.time()


def reset_confirm_cooldown(cooler_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    """Forget every confirmation of a cooler (its status just changed)."""
    for key in [k for k in _last_confirm if k[1] == cooler_id]:
        del _last_confirm[key]


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 3) STATUS-REPORT COOLDOWN  (per user + cooler)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

_last_status_report: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID], float] = {}


def check_status_cooldown(user_id: uuid.UUID, cooler_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    """Raise 429 if this user reported a status for this cooler too recently."""
    elapsed = time.time() - _last_status_report.get((user_id, cooler_id), 0.0)
    if elapsed < STATUS_REPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS:
        remaining = max(1, int((STATUS_REPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS - elapsed) / 60))
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=429,
            detail=f"You already reported this cooler. Try again in {remaining} minute{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}.",
        )


def record_status_report(user_id: uuid.UUID, cooler_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    _last_status_report[(user_id, cooler_id)] = time.time()
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/core/security.py">
# core/security.py
#
# PURE security logic: password hashing and login tokens (JWT).
#
# "Pure" means this file knows nothing about FastAPI or the database, so you can
# copy it into any other Python project unchanged. The part that plugs this into
# FastAPI ("who is the logged-in user for this request?") is in core/dependencies.py.

import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import secrets
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sabil.core.config import (
    ADMIN_PASSWORD,
    ADMIN_USERNAME,
    JWT_AUDIENCE_ADMIN,
    JWT_AUDIENCE_USER,
    JWT_EXPIRES_ADMIN_MINUTES,
    JWT_EXPIRES_USER_MINUTES,
    JWT_ISSUER,
    JWT_SECRET,
    PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS,
)


class TokenError(Exception):
    """Raised when a token is missing, forged, expired or of the wrong kind.

    wrong_role=True means "the token is genuine, but it is not an <admin/user>
    token" (the API answers 403 for that case instead of 401).
    """

    def __init__(self, detail: str, wrong_role: bool = False):
        super().__init__(detail)
        self.detail = detail
        self.wrong_role = wrong_role


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# PASSWORD HASHING
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    salt = secrets.token_hex(16)
    dk = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode(), salt.encode(), PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS)
    return f"{salt}${dk.hex()}"


def verify_password(password: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
    try:
        salt, dk_hex = hashed.split("$", 1)
        dk = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode(), salt.encode(), PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS)
        return secrets.compare_digest(dk.hex(), dk_hex)
    except Exception:
        return False


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# JWT PRIMITIVES
# A JWT is three base64 pieces joined by dots: header.payload.signature
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def _b64url_encode(data: bytes) -> str:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(data).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")


def _b64url_decode(data: str) -> bytes:
    padded = data + "=" * (-len(data) % 4)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(padded.encode("ascii"))


def _sign(signing_input: str) -> str:
    digest = hmac.new(
        JWT_SECRET.encode("utf-8"),
        signing_input.encode("ascii"),
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).digest()
    return _b64url_encode(digest)


def _build_token(sub: str, role: str, audience: str, expires_minutes: int) -> str:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    exp = now + timedelta(minutes=expires_minutes)
    header = {"alg": "HS256", "typ": "JWT"}
    payload = {
        "sub": sub,
        "role": role,
        "iss": JWT_ISSUER,
        "aud": audience,
        "iat": int(now.timestamp()),
        "exp": int(exp.timestamp()),
    }
    encoded_header = _b64url_encode(
        json.dumps(header, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    )
    encoded_payload = _b64url_encode(
        json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    )
    signing_input = f"{encoded_header}.{encoded_payload}"
    return f"{signing_input}.{_sign(signing_input)}"


def decode_token(token: str, expected_audience: str, expected_role: str) -> dict:
    """Check a token and return its payload, or raise TokenError."""
    try:
        header_part, payload_part, signature_part = token.split(".")
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise TokenError("Invalid token format.") from exc

    signing_input = f"{header_part}.{payload_part}"
    if not secrets.compare_digest(_sign(signing_input), signature_part):
        raise TokenError("Invalid token signature.")

    try:
        payload = json.loads(_b64url_decode(payload_part))
    except Exception as exc:
        raise TokenError("Invalid token payload.") from exc

    now_ts = int(datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp())
    if payload.get("exp", 0) < now_ts:
        raise TokenError("Token expired.")
    if payload.get("iss") != JWT_ISSUER or payload.get("aud") != expected_audience:
        raise TokenError("Invalid token claims.")
    if payload.get("role") != expected_role:
        raise TokenError(f"{expected_role.capitalize()} token required.", wrong_role=True)
    return payload


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# ADMIN / USER TOKENS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_admin_token(username: str) -> str:
    return _build_token(username, "admin", JWT_AUDIENCE_ADMIN, JWT_EXPIRES_ADMIN_MINUTES)


def create_user_token(user_id: uuid.UUID) -> str:
    return _build_token(str(user_id), "user", JWT_AUDIENCE_USER, JWT_EXPIRES_USER_MINUTES)


def decode_admin_token(token: str) -> dict:
    return decode_token(token, JWT_AUDIENCE_ADMIN, "admin")


def decode_user_token(token: str) -> dict:
    return decode_token(token, JWT_AUDIENCE_USER, "user")


def authenticate_admin(username: str, password: str) -> bool:
    return secrets.compare_digest(username, ADMIN_USERNAME) and secrets.compare_digest(
        password, ADMIN_PASSWORD
    )
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/core/validation.py">
# core/validation.py
#
# Input cleaning shared by every schema that accepts free text.

import re

# Basic text sanitization regex to prevent basic XSS or script injections.
# Only allows letters, numbers, spaces, and basic punctuation.
STRICT_TEXT_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[\w\s.,!?'\"()-]*$")


# A contact is an email, phone number, handle or link, so it also needs @ + : /
CONTACT_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[\w\s.,@+:/()\-]*$")


def sanitize_text(v: str) -> str:
    if not v:
        return v
    v = v.strip()
    if not STRICT_TEXT_PATTERN.match(v):
        raise ValueError("Input contains invalid characters.")
    return v


def sanitize_contact(v: str) -> str:
    """Like sanitize_text, but allows what emails, phone numbers and links need."""
    if not v:
        return v
    v = v.strip()
    if not CONTACT_PATTERN.match(v):
        raise ValueError("Contact contains invalid characters.")
    return v
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/media/router.py">
# media/router.py
#
# Serves uploaded photos. This replaces a plain "static files" folder, because a
# static folder would let anyone who knows a URL view photos that are still
# waiting for review.
#
# The URL stays /uploads/photos/<file>. The file is sent only if
#   - its photo AND its cooler are approved (public), or
#   - the caller is an admin, or the user who uploaded it.
# Otherwise the answer is 404.

import os

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.access import can_view_photo
from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto, WaterCooler
from sabil.core.config import PHOTO_DIR, PHOTO_URL_PREFIX
from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.dependencies import Viewer, get_viewer

router = APIRouter(tags=["media"])


@router.get("/uploads/photos/{filename}")
async def get_photo_file(
    filename: str,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    viewer: Viewer = Depends(get_viewer),
):
    if filename != os.path.basename(filename):  # no folders, no ".."
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Photo not found")

    photo = (
        await db.execute(
            select(CoolerPhoto).where(CoolerPhoto.file_path == f"{PHOTO_URL_PREFIX}/{filename}")
        )
    ).scalars().first()
    cooler = await db.get(WaterCooler, photo.cooler_id) if photo else None
    path = os.path.join(PHOTO_DIR, filename)

    if photo is None or cooler is None or not can_view_photo(photo, cooler, viewer) or not os.path.isfile(path):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Photo not found")

    public = photo.is_approved and cooler.is_approved
    return FileResponse(
        path,
        headers={"Cache-Control": "public, max-age=86400" if public else "private, no-store"},
    )
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/media/storage.py">
# media/storage.py
#
# Saving and deleting uploaded image files on disk. Knows nothing about coolers
# or the database, so it can be reused in any project.

import os
import uuid

from fastapi import UploadFile

from sabil.core.config import ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES, MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, PHOTO_DIR, PHOTO_URL_PREFIX


def ensure_upload_dirs() -> None:
    os.makedirs(PHOTO_DIR, exist_ok=True)


def _looks_like(head: bytes) -> str | None:
    """Return 'jpg', 'png' or 'webp' from the file's first bytes (its real type), else None."""
    if head.startswith(b"\xff\xd8\xff"):
        return "jpg"
    if head.startswith(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"):
        return "png"
    if head[:4] == b"RIFF" and head[8:12] == b"WEBP":
        return "webp"
    return None


def save_image(file: UploadFile, name_prefix: str) -> str:
    """Write an uploaded image to disk and return the URL path it is served at.

    Raises ValueError if the file is not a JPEG, PNG or WebP image (checked by the
    file's real first bytes, not just the type the client claims) or is too large.

    The file extension comes from the validated content type, never from the
    user-supplied filename (a crafted filename could otherwise write outside the
    upload folder), and the name contains a random id so two uploads can never
    overwrite each other.
    """
    ext = ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.get(file.content_type)
    if ext is None:
        raise ValueError("Invalid file type. Use JPEG, PNG, or WebP.")

    chunk_size = 64 * 1024
    head = file.file.read(12)
    if _looks_like(head) != ext:
        raise ValueError("The file is not a real JPEG, PNG, or WebP image.")

    filename = f"{name_prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.{ext}"
    full_path = os.path.join(PHOTO_DIR, filename)
    written = len(head)
    try:
        with open(full_path, "wb") as buf:
            buf.write(head)
            while chunk := file.file.read(chunk_size):
                written += len(chunk)
                if written > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Image is too large (maximum {MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES // (1024 * 1024)} MB)."
                    )
                buf.write(chunk)
    except ValueError:
        if os.path.exists(full_path):
            os.remove(full_path)  # never keep a partial file
        raise
    return f"{PHOTO_URL_PREFIX}/{filename}"


def delete_image(url_path: str) -> None:
    """Delete a previously saved image, given the path returned by save_image()."""
    full_path = url_path.lstrip("/")
    if os.path.exists(full_path):
        os.remove(full_path)
</document>

# sponsors/models.py
#
# The `sponsors` table: people/organisations who sponsor one or more coolers.

import enum
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Optional

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, Enum as SAEnum, Integer, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from sabil.core.database import Base

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from sabil.coolers.models import WaterCooler


class SponsorType(str, enum.Enum):
    Business = "Business"
    Mosque = "Mosque"
    Individual = "Individual"
    NGO = "NGO"


class Sponsor(Base):
    __tablename__ = "sponsors"

    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    sponsor_type: Mapped[SponsorType] = mapped_column(SAEnum(SponsorType), nullable=False)
    contact: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)
    verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="false")
    views_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    clicks_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )

    coolers: Mapped[list["WaterCooler"]] = relationship("WaterCooler", back_populates="sponsor")
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/sponsors/router.py">
# sponsors/router.py
#
# Public sponsor pages. (Creating, verifying and assigning sponsors is
# admin-only and lives in admin/sponsors.py.)

import uuid

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.coolers.models import WaterCooler
from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.lookup import get_or_404
from sabil.sponsors.models import Sponsor
from sabil.sponsors.schemas import SponsorRead, SponsorReadWithCount

router = APIRouter(prefix="/sponsors", tags=["sponsors"])


def _with_count(sponsor: Sponsor, cooler_count: int) -> SponsorReadWithCount:
    return SponsorReadWithCount(
        **SponsorRead.model_validate(sponsor).model_dump(),
        cooler_count=cooler_count,
    )


@router.get("", response_model=list[SponsorReadWithCount])
async def list_sponsors(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Every sponsor with the number of approved coolers they sponsor."""
    count_subq = (
        select(
            WaterCooler.sponsor_id,
            func.count(WaterCooler.id).label("cooler_count"),
        )
        .where(WaterCooler.is_approved == True, WaterCooler.sponsor_id.is_not(None))  # noqa: E712
        .group_by(WaterCooler.sponsor_id)
        .subquery()
    )

    stmt = (
        select(Sponsor, func.coalesce(count_subq.c.cooler_count, 0).label("cooler_count"))
        .outerjoin(count_subq, Sponsor.id == count_subq.c.sponsor_id)
        .order_by(Sponsor.name)
    )
    rows = (await db.execute(stmt)).all()
    return [_with_count(sponsor, count) for sponsor, count in rows]


@router.get("/{sponsor_id}", response_model=SponsorReadWithCount)
async def get_sponsor(sponsor_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    sponsor = await get_or_404(db, Sponsor, sponsor_id, "Sponsor")

    count = (
        await db.execute(
            select(func.count(WaterCooler.id)).where(
                WaterCooler.sponsor_id == sponsor.id,
                WaterCooler.is_approved == True,  # noqa: E712
            )
        )
    ).scalar() or 0
    return _with_count(sponsor, count)
</document>

# sponsors/schemas.py

import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from sabil.core.validation import sanitize_contact, sanitize_text
from sabil.sponsors.models import SponsorType


class SponsorCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=255)
    sponsor_type: SponsorType
    contact: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=255)

    @field_validator("name")
    def validate_name(cls, v):
        return sanitize_text(v)

    @field_validator("contact")
    def validate_contact(cls, v):
        if v is not None:
            return sanitize_contact(v)
        return v


class SponsorRead(BaseModel):
    id: uuid.UUID
    name: str
    sponsor_type: SponsorType
    contact: Optional[str] = None
    verified: bool
    views_count: int = 0
    clicks_count: int = 0
    created_at: datetime

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)


class SponsorReadWithCount(SponsorRead):
    cooler_count: int = 0


class SponsorAssign(BaseModel):
    """Body of PATCH /admin/coolers/{id}/assign-sponsor."""
    sponsor_id: uuid.UUID

# users/models.py
#
# The `users` table. One row per person who registered in the app.

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Optional

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from sabil.core.database import Base

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # only for editors/type-checkers; avoids circular imports at runtime
    from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerPhoto, WaterCooler


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True)
    hashed_password: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=False)
    trust_score: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default="0")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )

    badges: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)  # JSON array string of badges

    coolers: Mapped[list["WaterCooler"]] = relationship(
        "WaterCooler",
        back_populates="user",
        foreign_keys="[WaterCooler.user_id]",
    )
    photos: Mapped[list["CoolerPhoto"]] = relationship("CoolerPhoto", back_populates="user")
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/users/router.py">
# users/router.py
#
# Registering, logging in, "who am I", and the leaderboard.

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from sabil.core.database import get_db
from sabil.core.dependencies import get_current_user
from sabil.core.rate_limit import auth_rate_limiter
from sabil.core.security import create_user_token, hash_password, verify_password
from sabil.users.models import User
from sabil.users.schemas import UserAuthResponse, UserCreate, UserRead

router = APIRouter(tags=["users"])


def _auth_response(user: User) -> dict:
    """The user's public details plus a fresh login token."""
    return {
        "id": str(user.id),
        "username": user.username,
        "trust_score": user.trust_score,
        "access_token": create_user_token(user.id),
    }


@router.post(
    "/auth/register",
    response_model=UserAuthResponse,
    status_code=201,
    dependencies=[Depends(auth_rate_limiter)],
)
async def register(payload: UserCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    existing = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.username == payload.username))
    if existing.scalars().first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Username already taken.")
    user = User(
        username=payload.username,
        hashed_password=hash_password(payload.password),
        trust_score=0,
    )
    db.add(user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    return _auth_response(user)


@router.post(
    "/auth/login",
    response_model=UserAuthResponse,
    dependencies=[Depends(auth_rate_limiter)],
)
async def user_login(payload: UserCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.username == payload.username))
    user = result.scalars().first()
    if not user or not verify_password(payload.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid credentials.")
    return _auth_response(user)


@router.get("/auth/me", response_model=UserRead)
async def get_me(current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return {
        "id": str(current_user.id),
        "username": current_user.username,
        "trust_score": current_user.trust_score,
    }


@router.get("/users/leaderboard")
async def get_leaderboard(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """Top 10 users by trust score."""
    stmt = select(User).order_by(User.trust_score.desc()).limit(10)
    users = (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()
    return [
        {"rank": i + 1, "username": u.username, "trust_score": u.trust_score}
        for i, u in enumerate(users)
    ]
</document>

# users/schemas.py
#
# Schemas describe the JSON the API accepts and returns. They are NOT database
# tables (those are in models.py).

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class UserCreate(BaseModel):
    """Body of POST /auth/register and POST /auth/login."""
    username: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=64, pattern=r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$")
    password: str = Field(..., min_length=8, max_length=128)


class UserRead(BaseModel):
    """Response of GET /auth/me."""
    id: str
    username: str
    trust_score: int


class UserAuthResponse(BaseModel):
    """Response of register/login: the user plus their login token."""
    id: str
    username: str
    trust_score: int
    access_token: str
</document>

<document path="backend/sabil/users/trust.py">
# users/trust.py
#
# The TRUST SYSTEM rules in one readable place.
#
# Every user has a trust_score. Good behaviour raises it and, once it is high
# enough, the user is "trusted": their new coolers and status changes go live
# immediately instead of waiting for an admin to review them.
#
#   +50   a cooler of yours is approved (by an admin, or automatically if you are trusted)
#   -100  a cooler of yours is rejected (the score never drops below 0)
#   +10   a status change you reported is approved (at once if you are trusted;
#         one report per cooler every 10 minutes)
#   +5    you press "Still working" on a cooler
#
# Change a number here and it changes everywhere.

from sabil.users.models import User

AUTO_APPROVE_THRESHOLD = 500
APPROVE_REWARD = 50
REJECT_PENALTY = -100
STATUS_UPDATE_REWARD = 10
CONFIRM_REWARD = 5


def is_trusted(user: User) -> bool:
    """True when the user has earned instant approval (no admin review needed)."""
    return user.trust_score >= AUTO_APPROVE_THRESHOLD


def reward(user: User, points: int) -> None:
    """Add points to the user's score. A negative number is a penalty (floor of 0)."""
    user.trust_score = max(0, user.trust_score + points)
</document>

<document path="backend/scripts/seed.py">
# scripts/seed.py
#
# Inserts 5 sample coolers in Maadi, Cairo so the map is not empty.
# Run from the backend/ folder while the database is running:
#
#     python -m scripts.seed

import asyncio

from geoalchemy2.elements import WKTElement

from sabil.coolers.models import CoolerStatus, WaterCooler
from sabil.core.database import AsyncSessionLocal, init_db

SAMPLE_COOLERS = [
    {"name": "Maadi Metro Station Cooler", "latitude": 29.9596, "longitude": 31.2575, "status": CoolerStatus.Working},
    {"name": "Road 9 Public Cooler", "latitude": 29.9621, "longitude": 31.2601, "status": CoolerStatus.Working},
    {"name": "Maadi Corniche Cooler", "latitude": 29.9558, "longitude": 31.2329, "status": CoolerStatus.Empty},
    {"name": "Maadi Grand Mall Cooler", "latitude": 29.9609, "longitude": 31.2572, "status": CoolerStatus.Broken},
    {"name": "Maadi Sporting Club Cooler", "latitude": 29.9719, "longitude": 31.2498, "status": CoolerStatus.Working},
]


async def seed():
    await init_db()

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as session:
        for data in SAMPLE_COOLERS:
            geom = WKTElement(f"POINT({data['longitude']} {data['latitude']})", srid=4326)
            cooler = WaterCooler(
                name=data["name"],
                latitude=data["latitude"],
                longitude=data["longitude"],
                geom=geom,
                status=data["status"],
                is_approved=True,
            )
            session.add(cooler)
        await session.commit()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(seed())
</document>

<document path="backend/tests/conftest.py">
# tests/conftest.py
#
# Shared set-up for the tests. They talk to a REAL PostGIS database, so point
# SABIL_DATABASE_URL at a throw-away database before running, for example:
#
#   SABIL_DATABASE_URL="postgresql+asyncpg://sabil:sabil_pass@localhost:5433/sabil_test?ssl=disable" \
#       python -m pytest tests -q
#
# (run from the backend/ folder; create the empty database first)

import struct
import uuid
import zlib

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from sabil.core import rate_limit
from sabil.main import app


def tiny_png() -> bytes:
    """A valid 1x1 PNG image, built by hand so no image library is needed."""
    def chunk(kind: bytes, data: bytes) -> bytes:
        body = struct.pack(">I", len(data)) + kind + data
        return body + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(kind + data))

    return (
        b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
        + chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", 1, 1, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0))
        + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(b"\x00\xff\x00\x00"))
        + chunk(b"IEND", b"")
    )


def reset_limits() -> None:
    """Forget all rate-limit counters so one test cannot affect another."""
    for store in (
        rate_limit._requests_by_ip,
        rate_limit._photo_uploads_by_ip,
        rate_limit._auth_attempts_by_ip,
    ):
        store.clear()
    rate_limit._last_confirm.clear()


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def client():
    with TestClient(app) as c:
        yield c


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _fresh_limits():
    reset_limits()
    yield
    reset_limits()


def bearer(token: str) -> dict:
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}


@pytest.fixture()
def make_user(client):
    def _make(prefix="user"):
        name = f"{prefix}{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        r = client.post("/auth/register", json={"username": name, "password": "password123"})
        assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
        reset_limits()
        return bearer(r.json()["access_token"])
    return _make


@pytest.fixture()
def admin(client):
    r = client.post("/auth/admin/login", json={"username": "admin", "password": "admin123"})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    reset_limits()
    return bearer(r.json()["access_token"])
</document>

<document path="backend/tests/test_rules_and_uploads.py">
# tests/test_rules_and_uploads.py
#
# Covers BE-05 (trust points cannot be farmed), BE-06 (sponsor contact accepts
# emails and phone numbers) and BE-08 (upload size and real file type).

import random

from tests.conftest import reset_limits, tiny_png


def _approved_cooler(client, owner, admin):
    r = client.post("/coolers", headers=owner, json={
        "name": "Rules cooler", "latitude": 27.0 + random.random() * 0.9,
        "longitude": 30.0 + random.random() * 0.9})
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    cid = r.json()["id"]
    client.patch(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/approve", headers=admin)
    reset_limits()
    return cid


def _score(client, headers):
    return client.get("/auth/me", headers=headers).json()["trust_score"]


def test_status_report_points_are_paid_only_when_approved(client, make_user, admin):
    owner, reporter = make_user("own"), make_user("rep")
    cid = _approved_cooler(client, owner, admin)
    before = _score(client, reporter)

    r = client.patch(f"/coolers/{cid}/status", headers=reporter, json={"status": "Broken"})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["pending_status"] == "Broken"
    assert _score(client, reporter) == before                      # nothing yet

    # repeating the report is refused (cooldown), so it cannot be farmed
    assert client.patch(f"/coolers/{cid}/status", headers=reporter, json={"status": "Empty"}).status_code == 429

    client.patch(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/approve", headers=admin)    # admin approves the change
    assert _score(client, reporter) == before + 10


def test_rejected_status_change_earns_nothing(client, make_user, admin):
    owner, reporter = make_user("own"), make_user("rep")
    cid = _approved_cooler(client, owner, admin)
    before = _score(client, reporter)
    client.patch(f"/coolers/{cid}/status", headers=reporter, json={"status": "Empty"})
    client.patch(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/reject-status", headers=admin)
    assert _score(client, reporter) == before


def test_reporting_the_current_status_is_refused(client, make_user, admin):
    owner = make_user("own")
    cid = _approved_cooler(client, owner, admin)
    r = client.patch(f"/coolers/{cid}/status", headers=make_user("rep"), json={"status": "Working"})
    assert r.status_code == 400


def test_sponsor_contact_can_be_an_email_or_phone(client, admin):
    for contact in ("noor@example.com", "+20 100 123 4567", "https://example.com/about"):
        r = client.post("/admin/sponsors", headers=admin,
                        json={"name": "Noor Foundation", "sponsor_type": "NGO", "contact": contact})
        assert r.status_code in (200, 201), (contact, r.text)
        client.delete(f"/admin/sponsors/{r.json()['id']}", headers=admin)
    bad = client.post("/admin/sponsors", headers=admin,
                      json={"name": "X", "sponsor_type": "NGO", "contact": "<script>alert(1)</script>"})
    assert bad.status_code == 422


def _cooler_for_upload(client, owner):
    r = client.post("/coolers", headers=owner, json={
        "name": "Upload cooler", "latitude": 26.0 + random.random() * 0.9,
        "longitude": 29.0 + random.random() * 0.9})
    return r.json()["id"]


def test_upload_must_really_be_an_image(client, make_user):
    owner = make_user()
    cid = _cooler_for_upload(client, owner)
    fake = client.post(f"/coolers/{cid}/photos", headers=owner,
                       files={"file": ("a.png", b"this is not an image at all", "image/png")})
    assert fake.status_code == 400
    wrong_label = client.post(f"/coolers/{cid}/photos", headers=owner,
                              files={"file": ("a.jpg", tiny_png(), "image/jpeg")})
    assert wrong_label.status_code == 400
    ok = client.post(f"/coolers/{cid}/photos", headers=owner,
                     files={"file": ("a.png", tiny_png(), "image/png")})
    assert ok.status_code == 201


def test_upload_size_is_capped(client, make_user):
    owner = make_user()
    cid = _cooler_for_upload(client, owner)
    big = tiny_png() + b"\x00" * (9 * 1024 * 1024)
    r = client.post(f"/coolers/{cid}/photos", headers=owner,
                    files={"file": ("a.png", big, "image/png")})
    assert r.status_code == 400 and "too large" in r.json()["detail"]
</document>

<document path="backend/tests/test_visibility_and_limits.py">
# tests/test_visibility_and_limits.py
#
# Covers findings BE-01 (photos not lost to the rate limit), BE-03 (unapproved
# photos are private), BE-04 (unapproved coolers are private), BE-07 (login
# limit) and the cooler-with-photos delete fix.

import os
import random

from tests.conftest import reset_limits, tiny_png


def _new_cooler(client, headers, name="Test cooler"):
    lat = 29.0 + random.random() * 0.8
    r = client.post("/coolers", headers=headers,
                    json={"name": name, "latitude": lat, "longitude": 31.0 + random.random() * 0.8})
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    return r.json()["id"]


def _upload(client, headers, cooler_id):
    return client.post(f"/coolers/{cooler_id}/photos", headers=headers,
                       files={"file": ("a.png", tiny_png(), "image/png")},
                       data={"photo_category": "Cooler"})


def test_unapproved_cooler_is_private(client, make_user, admin):
    owner, other = make_user("own"), make_user("oth")
    cid = _new_cooler(client, owner)
    assert client.get(f"/coolers/{cid}").status_code == 404                   # anonymous
    assert client.get(f"/coolers/{cid}", headers=other).status_code == 404    # someone else
    assert client.get(f"/coolers/{cid}", headers=owner).status_code == 200    # submitter
    assert client.get(f"/coolers/{cid}", headers=admin).status_code == 200    # admin
    assert client.get(f"/coolers/{cid}/photos").status_code == 404
    client.patch(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/approve", headers=admin)
    assert client.get(f"/coolers/{cid}").status_code == 200                   # now public


def test_unapproved_photo_file_is_private(client, make_user, admin):
    owner, other = make_user("own"), make_user("oth")
    cid = _new_cooler(client, owner)
    assert _upload(client, owner, cid).status_code == 201
    photo = client.get(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/photos", headers=admin).json()[0]
    url = photo["file_path"]

    assert client.get(url).status_code == 404
    assert client.get(url, headers=other).status_code == 404
    r = client.get(url, headers=owner)
    assert r.status_code == 200 and "no-store" in r.headers["cache-control"]
    assert client.get(url, headers=admin).status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/uploads/photos/..%2Fphotos%2Fx.png").status_code == 404

    client.patch(f"/admin/photos/{photo['id']}/approve", headers=admin)
    assert client.get(url).status_code == 404          # photo approved, cooler is not
    client.patch(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/approve", headers=admin)
    r = client.get(url)
    assert r.status_code == 200 and "max-age" in r.headers["cache-control"]


def test_adding_photos_is_not_blocked_by_the_create_limit(client, make_user):
    owner = make_user()
    cid = _new_cooler(client, owner)
    codes = [_upload(client, owner, cid).status_code for _ in range(6)]
    assert codes == [201] * 6
    # creating coolers is still limited to 3 per minute
    results = [client.post("/coolers", headers=owner,
                           json={"name": f"x{i}", "latitude": 28.0 + i * 0.01, "longitude": 30.5}).status_code
               for i in range(3)]
    assert results[-1] == 429


def test_login_attempts_are_limited(client):
    codes = [client.post("/auth/login", json={"username": "nobody_x", "password": "wrongpass1"}).status_code
             for _ in range(12)]
    assert codes[:10] == [401] * 10 and codes[10:] == [429, 429]
    assert client.post("/auth/admin/login", json={"username": "admin", "password": "x"}).status_code == 429
    reset_limits()
    assert client.post("/auth/admin/login", json={"username": "admin", "password": "admin123"}).status_code == 200


def test_deleting_a_cooler_removes_its_photos_and_files(client, make_user, admin):
    owner = make_user()
    cid = _new_cooler(client, owner)
    _upload(client, owner, cid)
    url = client.get(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}/photos", headers=admin).json()[0]["file_path"]
    assert os.path.isfile(url.lstrip("/"))
    assert client.delete(f"/admin/coolers/{cid}", headers=admin).status_code == 200
    assert not os.path.exists(url.lstrip("/"))
</document>

<document path="docs/FINDINGS_LOG.md">
# Findings Log

Everything found while cleaning up and splitting the project, so it can be worked
through AFTER the restructuring is finished. Nothing in the "Open" sections has
been changed yet unless the Status says so.

**Status values:** `FIXED` (done, in a commit) · `OPEN` (confirmed, not fixed) ·
`VERIFY` (seen in earlier analysis, must be re-confirmed in the real code) ·
`DECIDE` (needs a product decision from you) · `APPROVAL` (a deletion; needs your yes)

**Severity:** High = security or data loss · Medium = wrong behaviour users can hit ·
Low = clutter or polish

Last updated after **Phase 4 (admin and profile splits) and the cross-check**. Branch: `restructure`.

---

## 1. Fixed during Phase 1 (backend restructure)

| ID | Sev | Finding | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| FX-01 | Medium | `PATCH /admin/coolers/{id}/force-status` accepted any text and crashed with a 500 (database enum error) on a bad value. The status is now validated against the `CoolerStatus` enum (returns 422). | FIXED |
| FX-02 | High | Photo upload built the saved filename from the client-supplied filename (`rsplit(".")`), so a crafted name could write outside the upload folder (path traversal). The extension now comes from the validated content type. | FIXED |
| FX-03 | Medium | Photo filenames were `<cooler>_<unix-second>.<ext>`: two uploads to one cooler in the same second overwrote each other's file while both database rows pointed at it. Names now contain a random id. | FIXED |
| FX-04 | Low | User and admin photo upload routes were near-copies with different error text. Now one shared helper (`coolers/photos.py` + `media/storage.py`). | FIXED |
| FX-05 | Low | ~15 lookups used `cast(id, String) == text` (defeats the index, hides typos). Route ids are now typed `UUID` and looked up with one `get_or_404` helper. | FIXED |
| FX-06 | Low | Removed the unused fields `taste_reports`, `is_wheelchair_accessible`, `needs_urgent_refill` (your request). | FIXED |
| FX-07 | Low | Inline schemas in `main.py` merged into the feature `schemas.py` files; settings/constants gathered into `core/config.py` and `users/trust.py`. | FIXED |

**Intended behaviour differences from the old code** (everything else matched in a
100-step end-to-end comparison on a real PostGIS database):
- Invalid status on `PATCH /coolers/{id}/status`: was `400` with a custom message, now `422` (FastAPI validation). The mobile app only ever sends valid values and never branches on 400/404/422, so it is unaffected.
- A malformed (non-UUID) id in a URL: was `404`, now `422`. Expected from the UUID typing; not exercised in the comparison run.
- Admin photo upload with a bad file type: error text now matches the user route ("Invalid file type. Use JPEG, PNG, or WebP.").

---

## 2. Open: backend

| ID | Sev | Finding | Evidence | Status | Suggested fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| BE-01 | Medium | **Photos can be silently lost.** Create-cooler and upload-photo share one rate limit (3 per minute per IP), and the failed requests still count. Adding a cooler with 3 photos is 4 requests, so the last is refused with 429. | Reproduced in the comparison run ("rate limit 5 uploads"). Whether the app shows an error is **VERIFY** (see MB-01). | FIXED: photo uploads have their own limit (20/min per IP); creating coolers stays 3/min | Separate buckets (or a higher photo limit), and make the app surface the 429. |
| BE-02 | High | **Unsafe defaults.** Without environment variables the admin login is `admin` / `admin123` and tokens are signed with `change-me-in-production`; anyone who knows this can forge tokens. | `core/config.py` | FIXED: `SABIL_ENV=production` refuses to start with default secrets; development only warns | In production mode, refuse to start unless real values are set. |
| BE-03 | High | **Unapproved photos are public.** `/uploads` is served as static files, so anyone with a URL can view photos still waiting for review. | `main.py` mounts `StaticFiles` | FIXED: `/uploads/photos/<file>` is now a route; unapproved photos only for admin or uploader (404 otherwise) | Serve approved photos only (via a route that checks `is_approved`), or store unapproved files in a private folder. |
| BE-04 | Medium | **Unapproved and rejected coolers are readable by anyone** who has the id (`GET /coolers/{id}` has no approval check). | Reproduced ("get unapproved cooler" returned 200) | FIXED: `GET /coolers/{id}` and its photo list return 404 for unapproved coolers unless admin or submitter | Return 404 unless approved, or the viewer is the submitter or an admin. |
| BE-05 | Medium | **Trust points can be farmed.** A status report gives +10 immediately, even while it is only pending review, with no cooldown, so repeating it climbs toward the 500-point auto-approve level. | `coolers/router.py` `update_cooler_status` | FIXED: points for a status report are paid only when it takes effect (admin approves, or you are trusted); one report per cooler per 10 minutes; reporting the current status is refused | Award the points only when the change is approved; add a per-cooler cooldown. |
| BE-06 | Medium | **Sponsor contact cannot hold an email or a `+20…` phone number.** The shared text sanitizer rejects `@` and `+`. Same behaviour in the old code. | Reproduced (`a@b.com` returned 422) | FIXED: contact accepts emails, `+20...` phone numbers and links (own validator) | Give `contact` its own validator that allows `@ + . -`. |
| BE-07 | Medium | **No brute-force protection on login.** `/auth/login`, `/auth/register` and `/auth/admin/login` have no rate limit. | Only create-cooler and upload-photo use `rate_limiter` | FIXED: 10 login/register attempts per IP per minute (separate from the submission limit) | Add a per-IP limit to the auth routes. |
| BE-08 | Medium | **No upload size limit, and file type is trusted from the client header.** A very large file is written to disk in full; a non-image can be sent labelled `image/png`. | `media/storage.py` | FIXED: 8 MB cap, and the file's real first bytes must match JPEG/PNG/WebP; partial files are deleted | Cap the size and check the file's magic bytes. |
| BE-09 | Medium | **Confirm cooldown and rate limiter live in memory.** They reset on every restart and are not shared if you run more than one server process. | `core/rate_limit.py` | OPEN | Move to the database or Redis if you scale beyond one process. |
| BE-10 | Low | **CORS allows every origin with credentials** (`allow_origins=["*"]`). | `main.py` | OPEN | List the real origins before deploying. |
| BE-11 | Low | **Sponsor `views_count` / `clicks_count` are never incremented**, so the API always returns 0. | No code touches them | DECIDE | Build the counting, or remove the columns. |
| BE-12 | Low | **`WaterCoolerUpdate` is unused.** No route edits a cooler's name, cups info or location, so a typo can only be fixed by delete and re-add. | `coolers/schemas.py` (marked unused) | DECIDE | Build one admin edit route (name, cups; location needs `geom` + duplicate check) or delete the class. |
| BE-13 | Low | **`iot_weight_kg` is unused** (IoT roadmap only). `badges` is unused too, but you chose to keep it. | grep | DECIDE | Keep as roadmap, or remove. |
| BE-14 | Low | **No real migrations.** `create_all` never alters existing tables, so changes need hand-written SQL (`db_fix.py` is exactly that). Your existing database still has the 3 removed columns. | `db_fix.py`, `core/database.py` `init_db` | OPEN | Adopt Alembic; until then run the `ALTER TABLE ... DROP COLUMN` from `GETTING_STARTED`/the Phase 1 notes. |
| BE-15 | Low | **There are no tests in the repo.** The 100-step comparison I used lives only in my scratch workspace. | no `tests/` folder | PARTLY DONE: `backend/tests/` has 11 tests (run with pytest). The old 100-step scenario is not saved | Save the scenario as `backend/tests/` so every later phase has a safety net. |
| BE-16 | Low | **Blocking file write inside an async route** (`shutil.copyfileobj`). Fine at low traffic. | `media/storage.py` | OPEN | Use a thread or `aiofiles` later. |
| BE-17 | Low | **Three overlapping ways to update a cooler's status** (Confirm, Change status, Add photo), plus the pending/approve flow. Hard to reason about and to explain to users. | Code + UI | DECIDE | Decide what each flow is for during the "behaviour clutter" phase. |
| BE-18 | Low | **`cups_type` is never set by the app** (so it is always null); only `has_cups` is used. | Earlier analysis | VERIFY | Check in the Flutter add-cooler dialog (Phase 3); keep or remove. |

### What each candidate was for (explained to the owner; still waiting for answers on the ones marked ASK)

| Item | What it was for | Verdict so far |
|---|---|---|
| `__pycache__/`, `routers/__pycache__/`, `services/__pycache__/` | Python's automatic cache of compiled files. Never written by hand; any machine recreates them. | Safe to delete |
| `routers/auth.py`, `services/dependencies.py` | An earlier attempt to split `main.py` into folders (they import with the old flat names like `from database import ...`). The same code now lives in `backend/sabil/users/router.py`, `core/dependencies.py` and `core/rate_limit.py`. | Safe to delete |
| `db_fix.py` | Run once by hand to add two columns (`is_rejected`, `pending_status_user_id`) to an existing database, because `create_all` never changes existing tables. It cannot run any more (old import path). | Safe to delete; the lasting fix is BE-14 (migrations) |
| `package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `vite.config.ts`, `metadata.json`, `.env.example`, old `README.md` | Generated by Google AI Studio when the project was first created there (a web-app template with a Gemini API key). Nothing in them is used by the Flutter app or the server. | ASK: was there ever a web version, or a plan for the Gemini photo check mentioned in BUSINESS_STRATEGY.md? |
| `coolman_dump_20260705_061337.txt` | A text snapshot of the whole code on 5 July, made by `packer.py.txt` so the code could be pasted or uploaded to an AI chat in one file. | Out of date; safe to delete |
| `packer.py.txt` | The script that makes those snapshots. It skips Android, iOS, venv and similar folders to keep the file small. | ASK: do you still use it? If yes, keep it as `tools/pack_code.py` |
| `.vscode/settings.json` | Tells VS Code to use a Windows virtual environment at `venv/Scripts/python.exe` in the repo root. | ASK: do you still develop on Windows with that folder? The server code is now in `backend/`, so the path may need to change rather than go |
| `docker-compose.yml` | Starts the PostGIS database. | KEEP (needed) |

Other: GitHub reports the repository moved to `https://github.com/iamarwan/CoolMan.git`;
the local remote still points at the old URL (works, but update it when convenient).

---

## 5. Limits of what has been verified

- **Backend:** verified against a real PostGIS database (route list, table definitions, and a 100-step end-to-end comparison old vs new). 97/100 identical; the other 3 are listed in section 1.
- **Mobile:** the Flutter/Dart SDK cannot be downloaded in my workspace, so Dart code **cannot be compiled or analyzed here**. For every mobile phase I will check imports and names with a script, but **you need to run `flutter analyze` and `flutter run`** after each phase. Everything stays on the `restructure` branch so it is easy to revert.
- Not exercised: the Flutter app against the new backend, real photo uploads from a phone, and the in-memory limiters across a server restart.

---

## 6. Progress through the restructure

| Phase | Scope | State |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Backend split, verify, docs | Done (commit `e582504`) |
| 2 | Mobile foundations: theme, config, shared widgets, split models and API files | Done, but **not compiled** (no Flutter SDK here): run `flutter analyze` |
| 3 | Mobile: split `map_screen.dart` | Done (1,621 to 484 lines; `flutter analyze` passes on GitHub) |
| 4 | Mobile: split `admin_screen.dart`, `admin_widgets.dart`, other screens | Done: admin split into `screens/admin/` (8 files), profile into `screens/profile/` (4 files). `my_submissions`, `sponsor` and `onboarding` screens left whole (under 320 lines each) |
| 5 | Cleanup deletions (section 4) and docs refresh | Done 2026-10-16. A fresh dated dump is in the repo root; `GETTING_STARTED.md` rewritten for a new laptop |
| After | Work through sections 2 and 3 of this log | Not started |

### Temporary compatibility shims (remove in Phase 5)

- `mobile/lib/models/water_cooler.dart` re-exports `cooler_photo.dart` and `sponsor.dart`.
- `mobile/lib/services/api_service.dart` re-exports `user_profile.dart` and `leaderboard_entry.dart`.

They keep the old imports in the screens working. Once the screens import the specific files, the re-exports can go.

### Static check limits (Phase 2)

A script checked that every import, export and `part` resolves, that braces balance (`sponsor_screen.dart` was already off by one parenthesis before my changes, a quirk of the checker's string handling), and that shared names are imported. It is not a compiler, so `flutter analyze` is the real test.

### Extra bug found and fixed while testing (batch 2-3)

- **Deleting a cooler that had photos failed with a server error** (the photo rows were not deleted with it). Now the photos are deleted with the cooler, and their image files are removed from disk too.
- The admin photo gallery in the app now sends the admin's token with image requests, because unapproved photos are no longer public.

### Behaviour changes from batch 4 (for the app)

- Reporting a status change now requires an approved cooler, and returns an error if the cooler already has that status ("Use Still working") or if you reported it in the last 10 minutes. The app shows these messages as it does any other error.

---

## 7. Cross-check after the splits (app vs server, and duplicated rules)

### What was checked and passed

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Every server call in the app (31) against the server's route list (method and path) | All match. |
| Fields the app reads from `WaterCooler`, `CoolerPhoto`, `Sponsor`, `UserProfile` against what the server sends | The app reads nothing the server does not send (`cooler_count` comes from the sponsor list/detail routes, which do send it). |
| `flutter analyze` on GitHub after every phase | Clean after fixes (see "Caught by the analyzer" below). |
| Backend tests (11) against a real PostGIS database | All pass. |

### New findings

| ID | Sev | Finding | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| XC-01 | Medium | **The app did not know the new server rule from batch 4**: tapping the status the cooler already has returned an error ("Use Still working"). | FIXED: the highlighted status button now does nothing. The 10-minute report cooldown still shows the server's message as a normal error. |
| MB-11 | Low | **Nine `catch (_)` blocks hide the reason for a failure.** Expected ones: `session_store.dart`, `api_client.dart` (fall back to default text), `admin_login_dialog.dart` (shows "Invalid credentials"). To review: `admin/photo_gallery_sheet.dart` (x2), `admin/sponsor_assign_sheet.dart`, `map_screen.dart` (two places), `sponsor_screen.dart`. | OPEN: check each shows the person something useful. |
| MB-12 | Low | `sponsor_screen.dart` says "Maintains N Coolers **in Cairo**" with the city typed in. | OPEN |
| MB-13 | Low | `admin/admin_login_dialog.dart` and `create_sponsor_dialog.dart` dispose their text controllers right after the dialog closes; Flutter can throw "used after being disposed" while the close animation runs. Also a variable named `pass` holds a controller. | OPEN (VERIFY on a device) |
| MB-14 | Low | **Reward amounts and rules are typed into the app** (`+50`, `+5`, `-100`, "500 points", "60 minutes") in `map_screen.dart`, `cooler_sheet.dart`, `my_submissions_screen.dart`, `onboarding_screen.dart`, `profile/trust_level.dart`. They duplicate `backend/sabil/users/trust.py` and the confirm cooldown, and will drift if the server numbers change. Extends MB-07, MB-08 and MB-09. | OPEN: one fix (the server sends its rules, or one shared Dart constants file) |
| MB-15 | Low | The words `Working` / `Empty` / `Broken`, the sponsor types (`Business`, `Mosque`, `Individual`, `NGO`) and the photo categories are plain text repeated in about 8 files. A typo would compile fine. | OPEN: Dart `enum`s |
| BE-19 | Low | `GET /coolers/{id}` is never called by the app (dead route). | DECIDE: keep for future clients or remove |
| RC-11 | Low | `my_submissions_screen.dart` has its own colour and icon logic for submission states (Approved / Rejected / Pending). It is a different idea from cooler status, so it was left alone, but it could join `widgets/status_style.dart`. | OPEN |

### Caught by the analyzer while splitting (all fixed, none left)

1. A script that added `super.key` to the new public widgets also rewrote *uses* of those widgets and removed the key from `AdminScreen` (`use_key_in_widget_constructors`).
2. A constructor ended up with the key declared twice (`duplicate_definition`).
3. One unused import (`profile_error_card.dart`).

Lesson for later phases: scripted rewrites of Dart need `flutter analyze` right after, which the GitHub check now does on every push.

---

## 8. Planned for later (not started)

### AI photo moderation (Gemini Vision)

Idea: when someone uploads a photo, send it to a Gemini Vision model to check it really shows a water cooler (or cups) and reject spam or unrelated pictures before an admin has to look. It is listed in `BUSINESS_STRATEGY.md` (roadmap, Phase 2 area).

Decision (2026-10-16): build it on a clean slate **after the app is running well**. The leftover Google AI Studio files (`package.json`, `vite.config.ts`, `.env.example` with a Gemini key placeholder, etc.) were deleted because they were an unrelated web template.

Notes for when it is built:
- Hook point: `backend/sabil/media/storage.py` `save_image` / `coolers/photos.py` upload route, after the magic-byte check.
- Keep the key in a new `SABIL_GEMINI_API_KEY` setting in `core/config.py`, never in the repo.
- Treat the AI verdict as advice (auto-flag or auto-hide), keep admin as final say, and fail open (photo goes to the normal queue) if the AI call errors or times out. Add retries/fallback around the call.
</document>

// lib/main.dart

import 'core/theme.dart';
import 'services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'screens/map_screen.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'screens/onboarding_screen.dart';

void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await ApiService.restoreSession();
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final onboardingComplete = prefs.getBool('onboarding_complete') ?? false;
  runApp(SabilApp(showOnboarding: !onboardingComplete));
}

class SabilApp extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool showOnboarding;
  const SabilApp({super.key, this.showOnboarding = false});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'Sabil',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: AppTheme.light,
      home: showOnboarding ? const OnboardingScreen() : const MapScreen(),
    );
  }
}

// App-wide settings. Change the server address HERE and nowhere else.

class ApiConfig {
  ApiConfig._();

  /// Where the backend server is.
  ///
  /// 10.0.2.2 is how the Android EMULATOR reaches the computer it runs on
  /// (port 8000 = the FastAPI server). On a real phone use your computer's
  /// Wi-Fi address instead, e.g. 'http://192.168.1.20:8000'.
  static const String baseUrl = 'http://10.0.2.2:8000';

  /// How long to wait for the server before giving up.
  static const Duration timeout = Duration(seconds: 15);
}

class CoolerPhoto {
  final String id;
  final String coolerId;
  final String filePath;
  final String uploadedAt;
  final bool isApproved;
  final String photoCategory;

  const CoolerPhoto({
    required this.id,
    required this.coolerId,
    required this.filePath,
    required this.uploadedAt,
    required this.isApproved,
    this.photoCategory = 'Cooler',
  });

  factory CoolerPhoto.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => CoolerPhoto(
        id: json['id'] as String,
        coolerId: json['cooler_id'] as String,
        filePath: json['file_path'] as String,
        uploadedAt: json['uploaded_at'] as String,
        isApproved: json['is_approved'] as bool? ?? false,
        photoCategory: json['photo_category'] as String? ?? 'Cooler',
      );
}
