<document path="mobile/lib/models/water_cooler.dart">
// lib/models/water_cooler.dart

import 'cooler_photo.dart';
import 'sponsor.dart';

// CoolerPhoto and Sponsor used to live in this file. They are re-exported so
// screens that only import water_cooler.dart keep working; new code can import
// cooler_photo.dart / sponsor.dart directly.
export 'cooler_photo.dart';
export 'sponsor.dart';

class WaterCooler {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final double latitude;
  final double longitude;
  final String status;
  final String lastVerifiedAt;
  final String? createdAt;
  final bool isApproved;
  final bool isRejected;
  final String? pendingStatus;
  final String? userId;           // who submitted it
  final List<CoolerPhoto> photos;
  final bool? hasCups;
  final String? cupsType;
  final Sponsor? sponsor;

  const WaterCooler({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.latitude,
    required this.longitude,
    required this.status,
    required this.lastVerifiedAt,
    this.createdAt,
    required this.isApproved,
    this.isRejected = false,
    this.pendingStatus,
    this.userId,
    this.photos = const [],
    this.hasCups,
    this.cupsType,
    this.sponsor,
  });

  factory WaterCooler.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => WaterCooler(
        id: json['id'] as String,
        name: json['name'] as String,
        latitude: (json['latitude'] as num).toDouble(),
        longitude: (json['longitude'] as num).toDouble(),
        status: json['status'] as String,
        lastVerifiedAt: json['last_verified_at'] as String,
        createdAt: json['created_at'] as String?,
        isApproved: json['is_approved'] as bool? ?? false,
        isRejected: json['is_rejected'] as bool? ?? false,
        pendingStatus: json['pending_status'] as String?,
        userId: json['user_id'] as String?,
        photos: (json['photos'] as List<dynamic>? ?? [])
            .map((e) => CoolerPhoto.fromJson(e as Map<String, dynamic>))
            .toList(),
        hasCups: json['has_cups'] as bool?,
        cupsType: json['cups_type'] as String?,
        sponsor: json['sponsor'] != null
            ? Sponsor.fromJson(json['sponsor'] as Map<String, dynamic>)
            : null,
      );

  static String statusLabel(String status) => switch (status) {
        'Working' => 'Working',
        'Empty' => 'Empty',
        'Broken' => 'Broken',
        _ => status,
      };
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin_screen.dart">
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';
import '../widgets/app_snackbar.dart';
import '../widgets/photo_pickers.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';
import 'admin/admin_common.dart';
import 'admin/admin_login_dialog.dart';
import 'admin/create_sponsor_dialog.dart';
import 'admin/current_tab.dart';
import 'admin/pending_tab.dart';
import 'admin/photo_gallery_sheet.dart';
import 'admin/sponsor_assign_sheet.dart';
import 'admin/sponsors_tab.dart';

class AdminScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AdminScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<AdminScreen> createState() => _AdminScreenState();
}

class _AdminScreenState extends State<AdminScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  final ApiService _api = ApiService();
  final ImagePicker _imagePicker = ImagePicker();

  late final TabController _tabController;
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  bool _authorized = false;

  List<WaterCooler> _pending = [];
  List<WaterCooler> _current = [];
  List<Sponsor> _sponsors = [];
  
  bool _loadingPending = true;
  bool _loadingCurrent = true;
  bool _loadingSponsors = true;
  
  String _query = '';
  Timer? _debounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 3, vsync: this);
    _searchController.addListener(_onSearchChanged);
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _authorizeAndLoad());
  }

  void _onSearchChanged() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), () {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _query = _searchController.text.trim().toLowerCase());
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _tabController.dispose();
    _searchController.removeListener(_onSearchChanged);
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchPending() async {
    if (!_authorized) return;
    setState(() => _loadingPending = true);
    try {
      final result = await _api.fetchPendingCoolers();
      setState(() {
        _pending = result;
        _loadingPending = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _loadingPending = false);
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Failed to load pending coolers.', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _fetchCurrent() async {
    if (!_authorized) return;
    setState(() => _loadingCurrent = true);
    try {
      final result = await _api.fetchCurrentCoolers();
      setState(() {
        _current = result;
        _loadingCurrent = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _loadingCurrent = false);
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Failed to load live coolers.', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _fetchSponsors() async {
    if (!_authorized) return;
    setState(() => _loadingSponsors = true);
    try {
      final result = await _api.fetchAllSponsors();
      setState(() {
        _sponsors = result;
        _loadingSponsors = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _loadingSponsors = false);
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Failed to load sponsors.', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _approve(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    setState(() => _pending.removeWhere((c) => c.id == cooler.id));
    try {
      if (cooler.isApproved) {
        // Already approved cooler, so this must be a photo review card.
        // Approve all unapproved photos for this cooler.
        for (final p in cooler.photos) {
          if (!p.isApproved) {
            await _api.approvePhoto(p.id);
          }
        }
        _snack('✓ Photos for "${cooler.name}" approved.');
      } else {
        await _api.approveCooler(cooler.id);
        _snack('✓ "${cooler.name}" approved.');
      }
      _fetchCurrent();
    } catch (e) {
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Approval failed — refreshing.', isError: true);
      _fetchPending();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _reject(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    setState(() => _pending.removeWhere((c) => c.id == cooler.id));
    try {
      if (cooler.pendingStatus != null) {
        await _api.rejectStatusChange(cooler.id);
        _snack('"${cooler.name}" status update rejected.');
      } else if (cooler.isApproved) {
        // Already approved cooler, so this must be a photo review card.
        // Delete all unapproved photos for this cooler.
        for (final p in cooler.photos) {
          if (!p.isApproved) {
            await _api.deletePhoto(p.id);
          }
        }
        _snack('Photos for "${cooler.name}" rejected.');
      } else {
        await _api.rejectCooler(cooler.id);
        _snack('"${cooler.name}" rejected.');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Rejection failed — refreshing.', isError: true);
      _fetchPending();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deleteSpam(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    setState(() => _current.removeWhere((c) => c.id == cooler.id));
    try {
      await _api.deleteCooler(cooler.id);
      _snack('"${cooler.name}" permanently deleted.');
    } catch (e) {
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Delete failed — refreshing.', isError: true);
      _fetchCurrent();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _forceUpdateStatus(WaterCooler cooler, String newStatus) async {
    try {
      await _api.forceUpdateStatus(coolerId: cooler.id, newStatus: newStatus);
      _snack('✓ "${cooler.name}" marked $newStatus.');
      _fetchCurrent();
    } catch (e) {
      if (_handleAdminAuthError(e)) return;
      _snack('Force update failed: $e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _uploadPhotoAdmin(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    final category = await pickPhotoCategory(context);

    if (category == null || !mounted) return;
    
    final source = await pickImageSource(context);
    if (source == null) return;

    final picked = await _imagePicker.pickImage(
      source: source,
      imageQuality: 80,
      maxWidth: 1280,
      requestFullMetadata: false,
    );
    if (picked == null) return;

    try {
      await _api.adminUploadPhoto(
        coolerId: cooler.id,
        imageFile: File(picked.path),
        photoCategory: category,
      );
      _snack('Photo uploaded successfully!');
      _fetchCurrent();
    } catch (e) {
      _snack('Failed to upload photo: $e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _showPhotoGallery(BuildContext context, WaterCooler cooler) async {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (_) => AdminPhotoGallerySheet(cooler: cooler, api: _api),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _showSponsorSheet(BuildContext context, WaterCooler cooler) async {
    await showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (_) => AdminSponsorAssignSheet(
        cooler: cooler,
        api: _api,
        sponsors: _sponsors,
        onAssigned: () {
          _fetchCurrent();
          _snack('✓ Sponsor assigned successfully.');
        },
        onRemoved: () {
          _fetchCurrent();
          _snack('Sponsor association removed.');
        },
      ),
    );
  }


  void _snack(String message, {bool isError = false}) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    showAppSnackbar(context, message, isError: isError);
  }

  bool _handleAdminAuthError(Object e) {
    if (e.toString().contains('401')) {
      ApiService.clearAdminToken();
      _authorized = false;
      _snack('Admin session expired. Please log in again.', isError: true);
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _authorizeAndLoad());
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  Future<void> _authorizeAndLoad() async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final ok = await showAdminLoginDialog(context, _api);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (!ok) {
      Navigator.of(context).maybePop();
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _authorized = true);
    await _fetchPending();
    await _fetchCurrent();
    await _fetchSponsors();
  }


  List<WaterCooler> get _filteredPending => _query.isEmpty
      ? _pending
      : _pending.where((c) => c.name.toLowerCase().contains(_query)).toList();

  List<WaterCooler> get _filteredCurrent => _query.isEmpty
      ? _current
      : _current.where((c) => c.name.toLowerCase().contains(_query)).toList();

  List<Sponsor> get _filteredSponsors => _query.isEmpty
      ? _sponsors
      : _sponsors.where((s) => s.name.toLowerCase().contains(_query)).toList();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate50,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Administration Controller',
            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18, letterSpacing: -0.5)),
        centerTitle: true,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.domain_add_rounded),
            tooltip: 'Add Sponsor Profile',
            onPressed: () => showCreateSponsorDialog(context,
                api: _api, notify: _snack, onCreated: _fetchSponsors),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
            tooltip: 'Force refresh',
            onPressed: () {
              _fetchPending();
              _fetchCurrent();
              _fetchSponsors();
            },
          ),
        ],
        bottom: PreferredSize(
          preferredSize: const Size.fromHeight(112),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 12),
                child: TextField(
                  controller: _searchController,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    hintText: 'Search reviewers feed…',
                    hintStyle:
                        TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.65)),
                    prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search_rounded,
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7)),
                    suffixIcon: _query.isNotEmpty
                        ? IconButton(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.clear_rounded,
                                color: Colors.white),
                            onPressed: () => _searchController.clear(),
                          )
                        : null,
                    filled: true,
                    fillColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
                    contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 0),
                    border: OutlineInputBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                      borderSide: BorderSide.none,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              TabBar(
                controller: _tabController,
                isScrollable: true,
                tabAlignment: TabAlignment.center,
                indicatorColor: Colors.white,
                indicatorWeight: 3,
                labelColor: Colors.white,
                unselectedLabelColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.55),
                labelStyle: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13),
                tabs: [
                  Tab(
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text('Review Queue'),
                        if (_pending.isNotEmpty) ...[
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          AdminCountBadge(count: _pending.length),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Tab(
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text('Live Map'),
                        if (_current.isNotEmpty) ...[
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          AdminCountBadge(
                              count: _current.length,
                              color: AppColors.success),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Tab(
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        const Text('Sponsors'),
                        if (_sponsors.isNotEmpty) ...[
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          AdminCountBadge(
                              count: _sponsors.length,
                              color: AppColors.info),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
      body: TabBarView(
        controller: _tabController,
        children: [
          AdminPendingTab(
            loading: _loadingPending,
            coolers: _filteredPending,
            onApprove: _approve,
            onReject: _reject,
            onViewPhotos: (c) => _showPhotoGallery(context, c),
          ),
          AdminCurrentTab(
            loading: _loadingCurrent,
            coolers: _filteredCurrent,
            onDelete: _deleteSpam,
            onForceStatus: _forceUpdateStatus,
            onAddPhoto: _uploadPhotoAdmin,
            onViewPhotos: (c) => _showPhotoGallery(context, c),
            onManageSponsor: (c) => _showSponsorSheet(context, c),
          ),
          AdminSponsorsTab(
            loading: _loadingSponsors,
            sponsors: _filteredSponsors,
            api: _api,
            onRefresh: _fetchSponsors,
            snack: _snack,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/map_screen.dart">
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map_marker_cluster/flutter_map_marker_cluster.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';
import '../services/location_service.dart';
import '../widgets/app_snackbar.dart';
import '../widgets/photo_pickers.dart';
import '../widgets/auth_dialog.dart';
import 'admin_screen.dart';
import 'map/add_cooler_dialog.dart';
import 'map/cooler_sheet.dart';
import 'map/map_markers.dart';
import 'map/map_widgets.dart';
import 'map/search_overlays.dart';
import 'profile_screen.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';

class MapScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MapScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<MapScreen> createState() => _MapScreenState();
}

class _MapScreenState extends State<MapScreen> {
  static const _fallback = LatLng(29.9600, 31.2500); // Cairo default
  final MapController _mapController = MapController();
  final ApiService _api = ApiService();
  final LocationService _location = LocationService();
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  final ImagePicker _imagePicker = ImagePicker();
  final PopupController _popupController = PopupController();

  List<WaterCooler> _coolers = [];
  List<WaterCooler> _searchResults = [];
  LatLng? _userLocation;
  bool _loading = true;
  bool _searchMode = false;
  bool _searching = false;
  bool _showSearchList = false;
  int _titleTapCount = 0;
  DateTime? _firstTapTime;
  Timer? _searchDebounce;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _searchController.addListener(_onSearchChanged);
    _initLocation();
  }

  void _onSearchChanged() {
    _searchDebounce?.cancel();
    _searchDebounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 500), () {
      if (mounted && _searchMode) _runSearch(_searchController.text);
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchDebounce?.cancel();
    _searchController.removeListener(_onSearchChanged);
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _handleTitleTap() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    if (_firstTapTime == null ||
        now.difference(_firstTapTime!) > const Duration(seconds: 3)) {
      _firstTapTime = now;
      _titleTapCount = 1;
    } else {
      _titleTapCount++;
    }
    if (_titleTapCount >= 5) {
      _titleTapCount = 0;
      _firstTapTime = null;
      Navigator.of(context)
          .push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AdminScreen()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initLocation() async {
    final loc = await _resolveLocation();
    setState(() => _userLocation = loc);
    await _fetchCoolers();
  }

  Future<LatLng> _resolveLocation() async {
    try {
      final position = await _location.resolveCurrentPosition();
      if (position != null) return LatLng(position.latitude, position.longitude);
    } on LocationServiceDisabledException catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar(e.message, isError: true);
    } on LocationPermissionDeniedException catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar(e.message, isError: true);
    } on LocationPermissionPermanentlyDeniedException catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar(e.message, isError: true);
    } catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar('Could not get location: $e', isError: true);
    }
    return _fallback;
  }

  Future<void> _fetchCoolers() async {
    final loc = _userLocation ?? _fallback;
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      final coolers = await _api.fetchNearbyCoolers(
          lat: loc.latitude, lng: loc.longitude);
      setState(() {
        _coolers = coolers;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _loading = false);
      _showSnackbar('Failed to load coolers: $e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _recenterOnUser() async {
    final loc = await _resolveLocation();
    setState(() => _userLocation = loc);
    _mapController.move(loc, 14.5);
    await _fetchCoolers();
  }

  Future<void> _runSearch(String query) async {
    if (query.trim().isEmpty) {
      setState(() {
        _searchResults = [];
        _showSearchList = false;
      });
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _searching = true);
    try {
      final results = await _api.searchCoolers(query);
      setState(() {
        _searchResults = results;
        _searching = false;
        _showSearchList = results.isNotEmpty;
      });
      if (results.isNotEmpty) {
        _mapController.move(
            LatLng(results.first.latitude, results.first.longitude), 15.0);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _searching = false);
      _showSnackbar('Search failed: $e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<bool> _ensureLoggedIn() async {
    if (ApiService.isLoggedIn) return true;
    return await _showAuthDialog();
  }

  Future<bool> _showAuthDialog() async {
    final result = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: true,
      builder: (ctx) => const AuthDialog(),
    );
    if (result == true) setState(() {});
    return result == true;
  }

  Future<void> _onMapLongPress(LatLng point) async {
    final ok = await _ensureLoggedIn();
    if (!ok || !mounted) return;
    final request = await showAddCoolerDialog(context);
    if (request != null) _createCoolerWithPhotos(request, point);
  }

  Future<void> _createCoolerWithPhotos(
      NewCoolerRequest request, LatLng point) async {
    try {
      final created = await _api.createCoolerAndReturnId(
        name: request.name,
        latitude: point.latitude,
        longitude: point.longitude,
        hasCups: request.hasCups,
        cupsType: request.cupsType,
      );

      var failedPhotos = 0;
      for (final p in request.photos) {
        try {
          await _api.uploadPhoto(
            coolerId: created,
            imageFile: File(p.file.path),
            photoCategory: p.category,
          );
        } catch (_) {
          failedPhotos++;
        }
      }
      if (failedPhotos == 0) {
        _showSnackbar('Cooler submitted successfully! +50 Trust pending.');
      } else {
        _showSnackbar(
          'Cooler submitted, but $failedPhotos of ${request.photos.length} '
          'photo(s) could not be uploaded. Open the cooler to add them again.',
          isError: true,
        );
      }
      await _fetchCoolers();
    } catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar('$e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _updateCoolerStatus(
      WaterCooler cooler, String newStatus) async {
    final ok = await _ensureLoggedIn();
    if (!ok || !mounted) return;
    try {
      await _api.updateCoolerStatus(
          coolerId: cooler.id, newStatus: newStatus);
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
      _showSnackbar('Status updated! Confirmed report earns points.');
      await _fetchCoolers();
    } catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar('Failed to update status: $e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _confirmWorking(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    try {
      final result = await _api.confirmCoolerWorking(cooler.id);
      final newScore = result['trust_score'] as int?;
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
      _showSnackbar(
        newScore != null
            ? '✓ Verified! +5 Points (Balance: $newScore pts)'
            : '✓ Verified working!',
      );
      await _fetchCoolers();
    } catch (e) {
      final msg = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
      _showSnackbar(msg, isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _uploadPhoto(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    final ok = await _ensureLoggedIn();
    if (!ok || !mounted) return;

    final category = await pickPhotoCategory(context);

    if (category == null || !mounted) return;
    
    final source = await pickImageSource(context);
    if (source == null) return;

    final picked = await _imagePicker.pickImage(
      source: source,
      imageQuality: 80,
      maxWidth: 1280,
      requestFullMetadata: false,
    );
    if (picked == null) return;

    try {
      await _api.uploadPhoto(
        coolerId: cooler.id,
        imageFile: File(picked.path),
        photoCategory: category,
      );
      _showSnackbar('Verification photo attached successfully!');
    } catch (e) {
      _showSnackbar('Failed to upload photo: $e', isError: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _launchDirections(WaterCooler cooler) async {
    final lat = cooler.latitude;
    final lng = cooler.longitude;
    final androidUrl =
        'https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=$lat,$lng&travelmode=walking';
    final iosUrl = 'maps://?daddr=$lat,$lng&dirflg=w';
    final fallbackUrl =
        'https://www.openstreetmap.org/directions?from=&to=$lat,$lng';
    final uri = Uri.parse(Platform.isIOS ? iosUrl : androidUrl);
    final fallback = Uri.parse(fallbackUrl);
    try {
      if (await canLaunchUrl(uri)) {
        await launchUrl(uri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      } else {
        await launchUrl(fallback, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      _showSnackbar('Could not open map navigation.', isError: true);
    }
  }

  void _showSnackbar(String message, {bool isError = false}) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    showAppSnackbar(context, message, isError: isError);
  }

  void _showCoolerSheet(WaterCooler cooler) {
    showCoolerSheet(
      context,
      cooler,
      onNavigate: () => _launchDirections(cooler),
      onConfirmWorking: () => _confirmWorking(cooler),
      onChangeStatus: (status) => _updateCoolerStatus(cooler, status),
      onAddPhoto: () => _uploadPhoto(cooler),
    );
  }

  List<WaterCooler> get _activeMarkers =>
      _searchMode && _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
          ? _searchResults
          : _coolers;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final initialCenter = _userLocation ?? _fallback;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: _searchMode
            ? TextField(
                controller: _searchController,
                autofocus: true,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    hintText: 'Search Landmark/Point…', border: InputBorder.none),
                onSubmitted: _runSearch,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
              )
            : GestureDetector(
                onTap: _handleTitleTap,
                child: const Text('Sabil s.a.',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 20, letterSpacing: -0.5)),
              ),
        centerTitle: !_searchMode,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        actions: [
          if (_searchMode) ...[
            if (_searching)
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.only(right: 16),
                child: Center(
                    child: SizedBox(
                        width: 18,
                        height: 18,
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white))),
              )
            else
              IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded),
                  onPressed: () => _runSearch(_searchController.text)),
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded),
              onPressed: () {
                _searchDebounce?.cancel();
                setState(() {
                  _searchMode = false;
                  _searchResults = [];
                  _searchController.clear();
                  _showSearchList = false;
                });
              },
            ),
          ] else ...[
            IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded),
                onPressed: () => setState(() => _searchMode = true)),
            IconButton(
              icon: Icon(
                ApiService.isLoggedIn
                    ? Icons.account_circle_rounded
                    : Icons.account_circle_outlined,
                color: ApiService.isLoggedIn ? AppColors.teal400 : null,
              ),
              tooltip: ApiService.isLoggedIn ? 'Profile Panel' : 'User Verification',
              onPressed: () async {
                if (ApiService.isLoggedIn) {
                  await Navigator.of(context).push(
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const ProfileScreen()));
                  setState(() {});
                } else {
                  await _showAuthDialog();
                }
              },
            ),
            if (_loading)
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.only(right: 16),
                child: Center(
                    child: SizedBox(
                        width: 18,
                        height: 18,
                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white))),
              )
            else
              IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
                  onPressed: _fetchCoolers),
          ],
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(
        onPressed: _recenterOnUser,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        tooltip: 'Recenter GPS',
        child: const Icon(Icons.my_location_rounded),
      ),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          FlutterMap(
            mapController: _mapController,
            options: MapOptions(
              initialCenter: initialCenter,
              initialZoom: 14.5,
              onLongPress: (_, point) => _onMapLongPress(point),
              onTap: (_, __) {
                if (_searchMode) FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
              },
            ),
            children: [
              TileLayer(
                urlTemplate:
                    'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',
                userAgentPackageName: 'com.sabil.app',
              ),
              if (_userLocation != null)
                MarkerLayer(markers: [buildUserMarker(_userLocation!)]),
              PopupScope(
                child: MarkerClusterLayerWidget(
                  options: MarkerClusterLayerOptions(
                    maxClusterRadius: 60,
                    size: const Size(44, 44),
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(50),
                    popupOptions: PopupOptions(
                      popupController: _popupController,
                      popupBuilder: (_, marker) => const SizedBox.shrink(),
                    ),
                    markers: _activeMarkers
                        .map((cooler) => buildCoolerMarker(
                            cooler, () => _showCoolerSheet(cooler)))
                        .toList(),
                    builder: (context, markers) =>
                        buildClusterBubble(markers.length),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          if (_searchMode && _searching) buildSearchingBanner(),
          if (_searchMode &&
              !_searching &&
              _searchController.text.isNotEmpty &&
              _searchResults.isEmpty)
            buildNoResultsBanner(_searchController.text),
          if (_showSearchList && _searchResults.isNotEmpty)
            buildSearchResultsList(
              results: _searchResults,
              onCollapse: () => setState(() => _showSearchList = false),
              onSelect: (cooler) {
                _mapController.move(
                  LatLng(cooler.latitude, cooler.longitude),
                  16.0,
                );
                setState(() => _showSearchList = false);
                _showCoolerSheet(cooler);
              },
            ),
          if (!_searchMode) ...[
            const Positioned(top: 12, right: 12, child: Legend()),
            const Positioned(
              bottom: 88,
              left: 0,
              right: 0,
              child: Center(child: HintBanner()),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/my_submissions_screen.dart">
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';

class MySubmissionsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const MySubmissionsScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<MySubmissionsScreen> createState() => _MySubmissionsScreenState();
}

class _MySubmissionsScreenState extends State<MySubmissionsScreen> {
  final ApiService _api = ApiService();
  List<WaterCooler> _submissions = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetch();
  }

  Future<void> _fetch() async {
    setState(() { _loading = true; _error = null; });
    try {
      final data = await _api.fetchMySubmissions();
      setState(() { _submissions = data; _loading = false; });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() { _error = 'Failed to load submissions.'; _loading = false; });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate50, // Ultra soft slate-white
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text(
          'My Submissions',
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 20, letterSpacing: -0.5),
        ),
        centerTitle: true,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
            onPressed: _fetch,
            tooltip: 'Refresh feed',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                color: AppColors.primary,
              ),
            )
          : _error != null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32.0),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.red.shade50,
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          ),
                          child: Icon(Icons.error_outline_rounded, color: Colors.red.shade600, size: 40),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        Text(
                          _error!,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate600, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, fontSize: 15),
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 18),
                        FilledButton.icon(
                          onPressed: _fetch,
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, size: 18),
                          label: const Text('Retry'),
                          style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : _submissions.isEmpty
                  ? Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32.0),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.primaryTint,
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              ),
                              child: const Icon(
                                Icons.add_location_alt_rounded,
                                size: 48,
                                color: AppColors.primary,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 24),
                            const Text(
                              'No Submissions Yet',
                              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate800),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 10),
                            const Text(
                              'Find clean water points in Egypt and report them by long-pressing on the home map view.',
                              style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate500, fontSize: 14, height: 1.5),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : ListView.builder(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 20),
                      itemCount: _submissions.length,
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) => _SubmissionCard(cooler: _submissions[i]),
                    ),
    );
  }
}

class _SubmissionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final WaterCooler cooler;
  const _SubmissionCard({required this.cooler});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isRejected = cooler.isRejected;
    final isApproved = cooler.isApproved;

    String statusLabel;
    Color statusColor;
    IconData statusIcon;
    String pointsText = '';
    Color pointsColor = Colors.transparent;

    if (isRejected) {
      statusLabel = 'Rejected';
      statusColor = AppColors.danger; // Slate Red
      statusIcon = Icons.cancel_rounded;
      pointsText = '-100 Trust';
      pointsColor = AppColors.danger;
    } else if (isApproved) {
      statusLabel = 'Approved';
      statusColor = AppColors.success; // Emerald Green
      statusIcon = Icons.check_circle_rounded;
      pointsText = '+50 Trust';
      pointsColor = AppColors.success;
    } else if (cooler.pendingStatus != null) {
      statusLabel = 'Status Pending';
      statusColor = AppColors.info; // Deep Blue
      statusIcon = Icons.swap_horiz_rounded;
    } else {
      statusLabel = 'Under Review';
      statusColor = AppColors.warning; // Amber
      statusIcon = Icons.hourglass_empty_rounded;
    }

    final verified = DateTime.tryParse(cooler.lastVerifiedAt);
    final formattedDate = verified != null
        ? '${verified.year}-${verified.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${verified.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        : cooler.lastVerifiedAt;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
        border: Border.all(
          color: AppColors.slate200,
          width: 0.8,
        ),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 46,
              height: 46,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              ),
              child: Icon(statusIcon, color: statusColor, size: 24),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 14),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          cooler.name,
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 15,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            color: AppColors.slate800,
                          ),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      if (pointsText.isNotEmpty)
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: pointsColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                            border: Border.all(color: pointsColor.withValues(alpha: 0.25)),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            pointsText,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 10,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                              color: pointsColor,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          statusLabel,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 11,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                            color: statusColor,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Icon(Icons.calendar_today_outlined, size: 12, color: Colors.grey.shade400),
                      const SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text(
                        formattedDate,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: Colors.grey.shade500,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  if (cooler.pendingStatus != null && !isRejected && !isApproved) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.infoTint,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.info_outline_rounded, size: 12, color: AppColors.infoDark),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          Text(
                            'Status Update: Mark as ${cooler.pendingStatus}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 11,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              color: AppColors.infoDark,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/onboarding_screen.dart">
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'map_screen.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';

class OnboardingScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const OnboardingScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<OnboardingScreen> createState() => _OnboardingScreenState();
}

class _OnboardingScreenState extends State<OnboardingScreen> {
  final PageController _controller = PageController();
  int _currentPage = 0;

  static const _pages = [
    _OnboardingPage(
      emoji: '💧',
      title: 'Find Clean Water,\nSimple and Free',
      body:
          'Sabil keeps you hydrated by locating community-reported public water coolers across Egypt, with accurate, real-time status updates.',
      gradientColors: [AppColors.primary, Color(0xFF14B8A6)],
    ),
    _OnboardingPage(
      emoji: '📍',
      title: 'Pinpoint and Share\nCoolers Around You',
      body:
          'Spotted a new public water cooler? Long-press on the map to list it. See one that\'s empty or broken? Flag it instantly to keep others informed.',
      gradientColors: [AppColors.teal600, AppColors.teal400],
    ),
    _OnboardingPage(
      emoji: '🏆',
      title: 'Earn Community Trust\nand Level Up',
      body:
          'Report coolers to earn +50 Trust Points. Reach a Trust Score of 500 points to unlock instant auto-approvals for all your submissions!',
      gradientColors: [Color(0xFF115E59), AppColors.primary],
    ),
  ];

  Future<void> _finish() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.setBool('onboarding_complete', true);
    if (!mounted) return;
    Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
      PageRouteBuilder(
        pageBuilder: (context, animation, secondaryAnimation) => const MapScreen(),
        transitionsBuilder: (context, animation, secondaryAnimation, child) {
          return FadeTransition(opacity: animation, child: child);
        },
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Align(
              alignment: Alignment.topRight,
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16.0, vertical: 8.0),
                child: TextButton(
                  onPressed: _finish,
                  child: const Text(
                    'Skip',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      fontSize: 15,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: PageView.builder(
                controller: _controller,
                itemCount: _pages.length,
                onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _currentPage = i),
                itemBuilder: (_, i) => _pages[i],
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(32, 0, 32, 40),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: List.generate(
                      _pages.length,
                      (i) => AnimatedContainer(
                        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                        width: _currentPage == i ? 28 : 8,
                        height: 8,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: _currentPage == i
                              ? AppColors.primary
                              : AppColors.teal100,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 36),
                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    child: FilledButton(
                      onPressed: _currentPage == _pages.length - 1
                          ? _finish
                          : () => _controller.nextPage(
                                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
                                curve: Curves.easeInOutCubic,
                              ),
                      style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 18),
                        elevation: 2,
                        shadowColor: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        _currentPage == _pages.length - 1
                            ? 'Get Started'
                            : 'Next',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 0.5),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _OnboardingPage extends StatelessWidget {
  final String emoji;
  final String title;
  final String body;
  final List<Color> gradientColors;

  const _OnboardingPage({

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/profile_screen.dart">
// lib/screens/profile_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../services/api_service.dart';
import 'profile/leaderboard_card.dart';
import 'profile/profile_card.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';

class ProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<ProfileScreen> createState() => _ProfileScreenState();
}

class _ProfileScreenState extends State<ProfileScreen> {
  final ApiService _api = ApiService();

  UserProfile? _profile;
  List<LeaderboardEntry> _leaderboard = [];
  bool _loadingProfile = true;
  bool _loadingLeaderboard = true;
  String? _profileError;
  String? _leaderboardError;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _fetchProfile();
    _fetchLeaderboard();
  }

  Future<void> _fetchProfile() async {
    setState(() {
      _loadingProfile = true;
      _profileError = null;
    });
    try {
      final profile = await _api.getUserProfile();
      setState(() {
        _profile = profile;
        _loadingProfile = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _profileError = 'Could not load profile.';
        _loadingProfile = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _fetchLeaderboard() async {
    setState(() {
      _loadingLeaderboard = true;
      _leaderboardError = null;
    });
    try {
      final board = await _api.getLeaderboard();
      setState(() {
        _leaderboard = board;
        _loadingLeaderboard = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _leaderboardError = 'Could not load leaderboard.';
        _loadingLeaderboard = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _logout() async {
    await ApiService.clearUserToken();
    if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate50,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text(
          'My Profile',
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18, letterSpacing: -0.5),
        ),
        centerTitle: true,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.logout_rounded),
            tooltip: 'Logout',
            onPressed: _logout,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        color: AppColors.primary,
        onRefresh: () async {
          await Future.wait([_fetchProfile(), _fetchLeaderboard()]);
        },
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          children: [
            ProfileCard(
              loading: _loadingProfile,
              error: _profileError,
              profile: _profile,
              onRetry: _fetchProfile,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            LeaderboardCard(
              loading: _loadingLeaderboard,
              error: _leaderboardError,
              entries: _leaderboard,
              currentUsername: _profile?.username,
              onRetry: _fetchLeaderboard,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

}
</document>

// lib/screens/sponsor_screen.dart

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../core/theme.dart';

class SponsorScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final Sponsor sponsor;

  const SponsorScreen({super.key, required this.sponsor});

  String _typeLabel(String type) => switch (type) {
        'Business' => 'Local Business Contribution',
        'Mosque' => 'Mosque / Religious Center',
        'Individual' => 'Private Generous Contributor',
        'NGO' => 'NGO / Humanitarian Aid',
        _ => type,
      };

  IconData _typeIcon(String type) => switch (type) {
        'Business' => Icons.storefront_rounded,
        'Mosque' => Icons.mosque_rounded,
        'Individual' => Icons.person_rounded,
        'NGO' => Icons.volunteer_activism_rounded,
        _ => Icons.business_rounded,
      };

  Future<void> _launchContact(BuildContext context, String contact) async {
    final phoneUri = Uri.parse('tel:$contact');
    final webUri = Uri.parse(
        contact.startsWith('http') ? contact : 'https://$contact');
    try {
      if (await canLaunchUrl(phoneUri)) {
        await launchUrl(phoneUri);
      } else if (await canLaunchUrl(webUri)) {
        await launchUrl(webUri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: const Text('Could not open contact.'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.red.shade700,
            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate50,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text(
          'Sponsor Profile',
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 18, letterSpacing: -0.5),
        ),
        centerTitle: true,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 0,
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        children: [
          // ─── Header Card ───────────────────────
          Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                colors: [AppColors.primary, AppColors.teal600],
                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                end: Alignment.bottomRight,
              ),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
                  blurRadius: 16,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 8),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 60,
                      height: 60,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      ),
                      child: Icon(
                        _typeIcon(sponsor.sponsorType),
                        color: Colors.white,
                        size: 28,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 16),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Text(
                                  sponsor.name,
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 20,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    letterSpacing: -0.5,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (sponsor.verified)
                                Container(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                      horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                                    border: Border.all(
                                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
                                  ),
                                  child: const Row(
                                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                    children: [
                                      Icon(Icons.verified_rounded,
                                          color: Colors.white, size: 12),
                                      SizedBox(width: 4),
                                      Text(
                                        'Verified',
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color: Colors.white,
                                          fontSize: 10,
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          Text(
                            _typeLabel(sponsor.sponsorType),
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              color: Colors.teal.shade100,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14, horizontal: 16),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.water_drop_rounded,
                          color: Colors.white, size: 18),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        'Maintains ${sponsor.coolerCount} Coolers in Cairo',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 14,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          // ─── Sadaka Jariya Note ─────────────────
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: AppColors.slate900.withValues(alpha: 0.03),
                  blurRadius: 12,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                ),
              ],
              border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate100),
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.successTint,
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.volunteer_activism_rounded,
                      color: AppColors.successDark, size: 24),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                const Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'Sadaka Jariya (On-going Charity)',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 15, color: AppColors.slate800),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Text(
                        'Providing free access to cool drinking water is of significant communal value. This sponsor funds refills, cup stock, maintenance, and repairs for the assigned coolers.',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.slate600, height: 1.5),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          // ─── Contact Button ─────────────────────
          if (sponsor.contact != null && sponsor.contact!.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: FilledButton.icon(
                onPressed: () => _launchContact(context, sponsor.contact!),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.phone_rounded, size: 18),
                label: const Text('Contact Sponsor'),
                style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                  elevation: 1,
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                  textStyle: const TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: 0.2),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
