<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/admin_login_dialog.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/admin_login_dialog.dart
//
// The admin sign-in pop-up. Returns true when the admin logged in.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';

Future<bool> showAdminLoginDialog(BuildContext context, ApiService api) async {
  final usernameController = TextEditingController();
  final passwordController = TextEditingController();
  bool loading = false;
  String? error;

  final success = await showDialog<bool>(
    context: context,
    barrierDismissible: false,
    builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
      builder: (ctx, setDialog) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Verify Administrative Account'),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: usernameController,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Admin Username', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
              enabled: !loading,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            TextField(
              controller: passwordController,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Password', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
              obscureText: true,
              enabled: !loading,
            ),
            if (error != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Text(error!,
                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red.shade700, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ],
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: loading ? null : () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(false),
            child: const Text('Back'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: loading
                ? null
                : () async {
                    final user = usernameController.text.trim();
                    final pass = passwordController;
                    if (user.isEmpty || pass.text.isEmpty) {
                      setDialog(() => error = 'Credentials required.');
                      return;
                    }
                    setDialog(() {
                      loading = true;
                      error = null;
                    });
                    try {
                      await api.adminLogin(username: user, password: pass.text);
                      if (ctx.mounted) Navigator.of(ctx).pop(true);
                    } catch (_) {
                      setDialog(() {
                        loading = false;
                        error = 'Invalid administrative credentials.';
                      });
                    }
                  },
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.primary),
            child: loading
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 16,
                    height: 16,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white),
                  )
                : const Text('Authenticate'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );

  usernameController.dispose();
  passwordController.dispose();
  return success == true;
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/create_sponsor_dialog.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/create_sponsor_dialog.dart
//
// The "Create New Sponsor" form. It calls the server itself, then tells the
// screen what happened through `notify` (message) and `onCreated` (refresh).

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';

Future<void> showCreateSponsorDialog(
  BuildContext context, {
  required ApiService api,
  required void Function(String message, {bool isError}) notify,
  required VoidCallback onCreated,
}) async {
  final nameCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final contactCtrl = TextEditingController();

  String selectedType = 'Business';
  final formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  bool loading = false;

  await showDialog(
    context: context,
    builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
      builder: (ctx, setDialog) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Create New Sponsor',
            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        content: Form(
          key: formKey,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextFormField(
                controller: nameCtrl,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Sponsor Entity Name', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                validator: (v) =>
                    v == null || v.trim().isEmpty ? 'Required' : null,
                enabled: !loading,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                initialValue: selectedType,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Sponsor Category', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                items: ['Business', 'Mosque', 'Individual', 'NGO']
                    .map((t) => DropdownMenuItem(value: t, child: Text(t)))
                    .toList(),
                onChanged: loading
                    ? null
                    : (v) => setDialog(() => selectedType = v!),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextFormField(
                controller: contactCtrl,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'Contact Information',
                    border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                enabled: !loading,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: loading ? null : () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: loading
                ? null
                : () async {
                    if (!formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
                    setDialog(() => loading = true);
                    try {
                      await api.createSponsor(
                        name: nameCtrl.text.trim(),
                        sponsorType: selectedType,
                        contact: contactCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty
                            ? null
                            : contactCtrl.text.trim(),
                      );
                      if (ctx.mounted) Navigator.of(ctx).pop();
                      notify('✓ Sponsor successfully created.');
                      onCreated();
                    } catch (e) {
                      setDialog(() => loading = false);
                      notify('Failed to create sponsor: $e', isError: true);
                    }
                  },
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.primary),
            child: loading
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 16,
                    height: 16,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white))
                : const Text('Create'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );
  nameCtrl.dispose();
  contactCtrl.dispose();
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/current_tab.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/current_tab.dart
//
// The Live tab: approved coolers with force-status, photo and sponsor actions.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../widgets/status_style.dart';
import 'admin_common.dart';

class AdminCurrentTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool loading;
  final List<WaterCooler> coolers;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onDelete;
  final void Function(WaterCooler, String) onForceStatus;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onAddPhoto;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onViewPhotos;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onManageSponsor;

  const AdminCurrentTab({super.key, 
    required this.loading,
    required this.coolers,
    required this.onDelete,
    required this.onForceStatus,
    required this.onAddPhoto,
    required this.onViewPhotos,
    required this.onManageSponsor,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (loading) return const AdminLoader();

    if (coolers.isEmpty) {
      return const AdminEmptyState(
        icon: Icons.water_drop_outlined,
        title: 'Map Index empty',
        subtitle: 'Once approved, coolers will populate here.',
      );
    }
    return ListView.builder(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 96),
      itemCount: coolers.length,
      itemBuilder: (_, i) => AdminLiveCard(
        cooler: coolers[i],
        onDelete: () => onDelete(coolers[i]),
        onForceStatus: (s) => onForceStatus(coolers[i], s),
        onAddPhoto: () => onAddPhoto(coolers[i]),
        onViewPhotos: () => onViewPhotos(coolers[i]),
        onManageSponsor: () => onManageSponsor(coolers[i]),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class AdminLiveCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final WaterCooler cooler;
  final VoidCallback onDelete;
  final void Function(String) onForceStatus;
  final VoidCallback onAddPhoto;
  final VoidCallback onViewPhotos;
  final VoidCallback onManageSponsor;

  const AdminLiveCard({super.key, 
    required this.cooler,
    required this.onDelete,
    required this.onForceStatus,
    required this.onAddPhoto,
    required this.onViewPhotos,
    required this.onManageSponsor,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final color = statusColor(cooler.status);
    final verified = DateTime.tryParse(cooler.lastVerifiedAt);

    final formattedDate = verified != null
        ? '${verified.year}-${verified.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${verified.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        : cooler.lastVerifiedAt;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withValues(alpha: 0.03),
            blurRadius: 8,
            offset: const Offset(0, 3),
          ),
        ],
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate200),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 44,
                  height: 44,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  ),
                  child:
                      Icon(statusIcon(cooler.status), color: color, size: 20),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 14),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        cooler.name,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate800),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Flexible(
                              child:
                                  StatusPill(label: cooler.status, color: color)),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          const Icon(Icons.calendar_today_outlined,
                              size: 11, color: AppColors.slate400),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              formattedDate,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.slate500),
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                              maxLines: 1,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                IconButton(
                  onPressed: onAddPhoto,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.add_a_photo_outlined),
                  color: AppColors.info,
                  tooltip: 'Admin Add Photo',
                  style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.infoTint,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                ),
                IconButton(
                  onPressed: onViewPhotos,
                  icon: Stack(
                    clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined),
                      if (cooler.photos.isNotEmpty)
                        Positioned(
                          right: -4,
                          top: -4,
                          child: Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(3),
                            decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.primary, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                            child: Text(
                              '${cooler.photos.length}',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  fontSize: 8,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  tooltip: 'Inspect Gallery',
                  style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryTint,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                ),
                IconButton(
                  onPressed: onDelete,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline_rounded),
                  color: AppColors.danger,
                  tooltip: 'Spam cleanup',
                  style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.dangerTint,
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                ),
                PopupMenuButton<String>(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.more_vert_rounded,
                      color: AppColors.slate400),
                  tooltip: 'Audit status options',
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                  onSelected: (value) {
                    if (value == '__sponsor__') {
                      onManageSponsor();
                    } else {
                      onForceStatus(value);
                    }
                  },
                  itemBuilder: (_) => [
                    _forceMenuItem('Working', AppColors.success, Icons.water_drop),
                    _forceMenuItem(
                        'Empty', AppColors.slate500, Icons.water_drop_outlined),
                    _forceMenuItem('Broken', AppColors.danger, Icons.report),
                    const PopupMenuDivider(),
                    PopupMenuItem<String>(
                      value: '__sponsor__',
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                            decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppColors.primaryTint,
                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            ),
                            child: const Icon(
                                Icons.volunteer_activism_outlined,
                                color: AppColors.primary,
                                size: 16),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Text(
                            cooler.sponsor != null
                                ? 'Edit sponsor mapping'
                                : 'Map Sponsor',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: AppColors.primary,
                              fontSize: 13,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (cooler.sponsor != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.successTint,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border:
                      Border.all(color: AppColors.successBorder),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.volunteer_activism_rounded,
                        color: AppColors.successDark, size: 14),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'Sponsored Sadie: ${cooler.sponsor!.name}',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: AppColors.successDark,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (cooler.sponsor!.verified)
                      const Icon(Icons.verified_rounded,
                          size: 14, color: AppColors.successDark),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  PopupMenuItem<String> _forceMenuItem(
      String status, Color color, IconData icon) {
    return PopupMenuItem<String>(
      value: status,
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1), shape: BoxShape.circle),
            child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 16),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Text('Verify $status',
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color, fontSize: 13)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/pending_tab.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/pending_tab.dart
//
// The Pending tab: new coolers, proposed status changes and unreviewed photos, as swipeable cards.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../widgets/status_style.dart';
import 'admin_common.dart';

class AdminPendingTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool loading;
  final List<WaterCooler> coolers;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onApprove;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onReject;
  final void Function(WaterCooler) onViewPhotos;

  const AdminPendingTab({super.key, 
    required this.loading,
    required this.coolers,
    required this.onApprove,
    required this.onReject,
    required this.onViewPhotos,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (loading) return const AdminLoader();

    if (coolers.isEmpty) {
      return const AdminEmptyState(
        icon: Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
        title: 'Review Queue Clear',
        subtitle: 'All newly proposed water coolers resolved.',
      );
    }
    return Column(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: double.infinity,
          color: AppColors.primaryTint,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 10),
          child: const Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.swipe_rounded, size: 14, color: AppColors.primary),
              SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text(
                'Swipe right to approve · Swipe left to reject',
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: ListView.builder(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 32),
            itemCount: coolers.length,
            itemBuilder: (_, i) => AdminSwipeablePendingCard(
              key: ValueKey(coolers[i].id),
              cooler: coolers[i],
              onApprove: () => onApprove(coolers[i]),
              onReject: () => onReject(coolers[i]),
              onViewPhotos: () => onViewPhotos(coolers[i]),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class AdminSwipeablePendingCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final WaterCooler cooler;
  final VoidCallback onApprove;
  final VoidCallback onReject;
  final VoidCallback onViewPhotos;

  const AdminSwipeablePendingCard({
    super.key,
    required this.cooler,
    required this.onApprove,
    required this.onReject,
    required this.onViewPhotos,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Dismissible(
      key: ValueKey(cooler.id),
      direction: DismissDirection.horizontal,
      confirmDismiss: (direction) async {
        if (direction == DismissDirection.startToEnd) {
          onApprove();
          return true;
        } else {
          onReject();
          return true;
        }
      },
      background: const AdminSwipeBg(
        color: AppColors.success,
        icon: Icons.check_circle_rounded,
        label: 'Approve',
        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
      ),
      secondaryBackground: const AdminSwipeBg(
        color: AppColors.danger,
        icon: Icons.cancel_rounded,
        label: 'Reject',
        alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
      ),
      child: AdminPendingCard(
        cooler: cooler,
        onApprove: onApprove,
        onReject: onReject,
        onViewPhotos: onViewPhotos,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class AdminSwipeBg extends StatelessWidget {
  final Color color;
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final Alignment alignment;

  const AdminSwipeBg({super.key, 
    required this.color,
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.alignment,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isLeft = alignment == Alignment.centerLeft;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
      alignment: alignment,
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: isLeft
            ? [
                Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 24),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(label,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 14)),
              ]
            : [
                Text(label,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 14)),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 24),
              ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class AdminPendingCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final WaterCooler cooler;
  final VoidCallback onApprove;
  final VoidCallback onReject;
  final VoidCallback onViewPhotos;

  const AdminPendingCard({super.key, 
    required this.cooler,
    required this.onApprove,
    required this.onReject,
    required this.onViewPhotos,
  });

  bool get _isStatusUpdate => cooler.pendingStatus != null;
  bool get _isNewProposal => !cooler.isApproved;

  Color get _typeColor {
    if (_isStatusUpdate) return AppColors.warning;
    if (_isNewProposal) return const Color(0xFF4F46E5);
    return AppColors.primary;
  }

  IconData get _typeIcon {
    if (_isStatusUpdate) return Icons.swap_horiz_rounded;
    if (_isNewProposal) return Icons.add_location_alt_rounded;
    return Icons.image_search_rounded;
  }

  String get _typeLabel {
    if (_isStatusUpdate) return 'Status Update Review';
    if (_isNewProposal) return 'New Location Proposal';
    return 'New Photo Submission';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final verified = DateTime.tryParse(cooler.lastVerifiedAt);

    final formattedDate = verified != null
        ? '${verified.year}-${verified.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-${verified.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')} '
            '${verified.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${verified.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
        : cooler.lastVerifiedAt;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate200),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: _typeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
              borderRadius:
                  const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(18)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(_typeIcon, size: 14, color: _typeColor),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(
                  _typeLabel,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: _typeColor,
                    letterSpacing: 0.2,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(cooler.name,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate800)),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                AdminMetaRow(
                  icon: Icons.location_on_outlined,
                  text:
                      'GPS: ${cooler.latitude.toStringAsFixed(6)}, ${cooler.longitude.toStringAsFixed(6)}',
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                AdminMetaRow(
                  icon: Icons.access_time_rounded,
                  text: 'Submitted: $formattedDate',
                ),
                if (_isStatusUpdate) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      StatusPill(
                          label: cooler.status,
                          color: statusColor(cooler.status)),
                      const Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),
                        child: Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_rounded,
                            size: 16, color: Colors.grey),
                      ),
                      StatusPill(
                        label: cooler.pendingStatus!,
                        color: statusColor(cooler.pendingStatus!),
                        isPending: true,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: onViewPhotos,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined, size: 15),
                  label: Text(
                    cooler.photos.isEmpty
                        ? 'No user photos'
                        : 'View ${cooler.photos.length} item${cooler.photos.length == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                  ),
                  style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                    foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                    side: BorderSide(
                        color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
                    textStyle: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                        onPressed: onReject,
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 16),
                        label: const Text('Reject'),
                        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                          foregroundColor: AppColors.danger,
                          side: BorderSide(
                              color: AppColors.danger.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 11),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: FilledButton.icon(
                        onPressed: onApprove,
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.check_rounded, size: 16),
                        label: const Text('Approve'),
                        style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 11),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/photo_gallery_sheet.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/photo_gallery_sheet.dart
//
// Bottom sheet showing a cooler's photos with approve / delete / add buttons.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/config.dart';
import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';

class AdminPhotoGallerySheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final WaterCooler cooler;
  final ApiService api;

  const AdminPhotoGallerySheet({super.key, required this.cooler, required this.api});

  @override
  State<AdminPhotoGallerySheet> createState() => _AdminPhotoGallerySheetState();
}

class _AdminPhotoGallerySheetState extends State<AdminPhotoGallerySheet> {
  List<CoolerPhoto> _photos = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    try {
      final photos = await widget.api.fetchCoolerPhotosAdmin(widget.cooler.id);
      setState(() {
        _photos = photos;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _error = 'Failed to load photos.';
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _approvePhoto(CoolerPhoto photo) async {
    try {
      await widget.api.approvePhoto(photo.id);
      setState(() {
        final idx = _photos.indexWhere((p) => p.id == photo.id);
        if (idx != -1) {
          _photos[idx] = CoolerPhoto(
            id: photo.id,
            coolerId: photo.coolerId,
            filePath: photo.filePath,
            uploadedAt: photo.uploadedAt,
            isApproved: true,
          );
        }
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
              content: const Text('Approval failed.'),
              backgroundColor: Colors.red.shade700),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deletePhoto(CoolerPhoto photo) async {
    try {
      await widget.api.deletePhoto(photo.id);
      setState(() => _photos.removeWhere((p) => p.id == photo.id));
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
              content: const Text('Delete failed.'),
              backgroundColor: Colors.red.shade700),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _openFullscreen(BuildContext context, String url) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
      builder: (_) => Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: Colors.black,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          elevation: 0,
        ),
        body: Center(
          child: InteractiveViewer(
            child: Image.network(
              url,
              headers: ApiService.adminImageHeaders,
              fit: BoxFit.contain,
              errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => const Icon(
                Icons.broken_image_outlined,
                color: Colors.white54,
                size: 64,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      constraints: BoxConstraints(
        maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.82,
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 40,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, 12),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined,
                    color: AppColors.primary, size: 20),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    widget.cooler.name,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                ),
                if (!_loading && _photos.isNotEmpty)
                  Container(
                    padding:
                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.primaryTint,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      '${_photos.length} item${_photos.length == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          color: AppColors.primary,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1, color: AppColors.slate100),
          Flexible(
            child: _loading
                ? const Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(40),
                    child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.primary)),
                  )
                : _error != null
                    ? Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(40),
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Icon(Icons.broken_image_outlined,
                                size: 48, color: Colors.grey.shade400),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text(_error!,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                    color: Colors.grey.shade600, fontSize: 14)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      )
                    : _photos.isEmpty
                        ? Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(60),
                            child: Column(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                Icon(Icons.camera_alt_outlined,
                                    size: 48, color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                                Text(
                                  'No user submitted photos.',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                      color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          )
                        : GridView.builder(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                            gridDelegate:
                                const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                              crossAxisCount: 2,
                              crossAxisSpacing: 10,
                              mainAxisSpacing: 10,
                              childAspectRatio: 1,
                            ),
                            itemCount: _photos.length,
                            itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                              final photo = _photos[i];
                              final url = '${ApiConfig.baseUrl}${photo.filePath}';
                              return GestureDetector(
                                onTap: () => _openFullscreen(context, url),
                                child: Stack(
                                  fit: StackFit.expand,
                                  children: [
                                    ClipRRect(
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                                      child: Image.network(
                                        url,
                                        headers: ApiService.adminImageHeaders,
                                        fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                        errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => Container(
                                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                              color: Colors.grey.shade100,
                                              borderRadius:
                                                  BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                                          child: Icon(
                                              Icons.broken_image_outlined,
                                              color: Colors.grey.shade400,
                                              size: 36),
                                        ),
                                        loadingBuilder: (_, child, progress) =>
                                            progress == null
                                                ? child
                                                : Container(
                                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                                        color: Colors.grey.shade500,
                                                        borderRadius:
                                                            BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                                                    child: const Center(
                                                        child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                                            strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white)),
                                                  ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    if (photo.isApproved)
                                      Positioned(
                                        top: 8,
                                        right: 8,
                                        child: Container(
                                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                              color: AppColors.success,
                                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                              border: Border.all(
                                                  color: Colors.white,
                                                  width: 1.5)),
                                          child: const Icon(Icons.check_rounded,
                                              color: Colors.white, size: 10),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    Positioned(
                                      bottom: 0,
                                      left: 0,
                                      right: 0,
                                      child: Container(
                                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                          borderRadius:
                                              const BorderRadius.vertical(
                                                  bottom: Radius.circular(14)),
                                          color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.65),
                                        ),
                                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                            vertical: 8),
                                        child: Row(
                                          mainAxisAlignment:
                                              MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                                          children: [
                                            if (!photo.isApproved)
                                              GestureDetector(
                                                onTap: () =>
                                                    _approvePhoto(photo),
                                                child: const Icon(
                                                    Icons.check_circle_outline,
                                                    color: Color(0xFF34D399),
                                                    size: 22),
                                              ),
                                            GestureDetector(
                                              onTap: () => _deletePhoto(photo),
                                              child: const Icon(
                                                  Icons.delete_outline,
                                                  color: AppColors.dangerLight,
                                                  size: 22),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/sponsor_assign_sheet.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/sponsor_assign_sheet.dart
//
// Bottom sheet to attach (or remove) a sponsor on a cooler.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';

class AdminSponsorAssignSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final WaterCooler cooler;
  final ApiService api;
  final List<Sponsor> sponsors;
  final VoidCallback onAssigned;
  final VoidCallback onRemoved;

  const AdminSponsorAssignSheet({super.key, 
    required this.cooler,
    required this.api,
    required this.sponsors,
    required this.onAssigned,
    required this.onRemoved,
  });

  @override
  State<AdminSponsorAssignSheet> createState() => _AdminSponsorAssignSheetState();
}

class _AdminSponsorAssignSheetState extends State<AdminSponsorAssignSheet> {
  bool _loading = false;
  String? _error;

  Future<void> _removeSponsor() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });

    try {
      await widget.api.removeSponsor(widget.cooler.id);
      widget.onRemoved();
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _loading = false;
        _error = 'Failed to remove sponsor association.';
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _assign(String id) async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await widget.api.assignSponsor(coolerId: widget.cooler.id, sponsorId: id);
      widget.onAssigned();
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() {
        _loading = false;
        _error = 'Failed to assign sponsor.';
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sponsor = widget.cooler.sponsor;

    return Container(
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      constraints: BoxConstraints(
        maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.6,
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 40,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, 12),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.volunteer_activism_outlined,
                    color: AppColors.primary, size: 20),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    sponsor != null
                        ? 'Manage sponsor association'
                        : 'Assign sponsor',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1, color: AppColors.slate100),
          Expanded(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  if (sponsor != null) ...[
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.primaryTint,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.volunteer_activism_rounded,
                              color: AppColors.primary),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              sponsor.name,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14, color: AppColors.primary),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    const Text(
                      'Sponsor represents verified status sadaka. Removing Association will orphan this cooler.',
                      style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.slate500, height: 1.4),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    if (_error != null) ...[
                      Text(_error!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red, fontSize: 13)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    ],
                    OutlinedButton(
                      onPressed: _loading ? null : _removeSponsor,
                      style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                        foregroundColor: Colors.red,
                        side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.red),
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                      ),
                      child: _loading
                          ? const SizedBox(width: 14, height: 14, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                          : const Text('Remove Assigned Sponsor', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ),
                  ] else ...[
                    const Text(
                      'Select sponsor profile from records:',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate600),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: widget.sponsors.isEmpty
                          ? const Center(child: Text('Create a sponsor first in the Sponsors tab.'))
                          : ListView.builder(
                              itemCount: widget.sponsors.length,
                              itemBuilder: (ctx, i) {
                                final s = widget.sponsors[i];
                                return ListTile(
                                  title: Text(s.name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                                  subtitle: Text(s.sponsorType, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11)),
                                  trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, size: 18),
                                  onTap: () => _assign(s.id),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/admin/sponsors_tab.dart">
// lib/screens/admin/sponsors_tab.dart
//
// The Sponsors tab: list of sponsors with verify / delete actions.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';
import 'admin_common.dart';

class AdminSponsorsTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool loading;
  final List<Sponsor> sponsors;
  final ApiService api;
  final VoidCallback onRefresh;
  final void Function(String message, {bool isError}) snack;

  const AdminSponsorsTab({super.key, 
    required this.loading,
    required this.sponsors,
    required this.api,
    required this.onRefresh,
    required this.snack,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (loading) return const AdminLoader();

    if (sponsors.isEmpty) {
      return const AdminEmptyState(
        icon: Icons.volunteer_activism_outlined,
        title: 'Sponsor list is empty',
        subtitle: 'Incentivize local mosques and shops, then add profiles.',
      );
    }

    return ListView.builder(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      itemCount: sponsors.length,
      itemBuilder: (ctx, i) {
        final sponsor = sponsors[i];
        return Card(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
          color: Colors.white,
          elevation: 0,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate200)),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.infoTint,
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.volunteer_activism_rounded, color: AppColors.info),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(sponsor.name,
                              style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16, color: AppColors.slate800)),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text('${sponsor.sponsorType} · ${sponsor.coolerCount} Coolers Maintained',
                              style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate500, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (sponsor.verified)
                      const Icon(Icons.verified_rounded, color: AppColors.success)
                  ],
                ),
                if (sponsor.contact != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.link, size: 14, color: AppColors.slate400),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(sponsor.contact!, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate700, fontSize: 13)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                  children: [
                    if (!sponsor.verified)
                      OutlinedButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () async {
                          try {
                            await api.verifySponsor(sponsor.id);
                            snack('Sponsor profile verified.');
                            onRefresh();
                          } catch (e) {
                            snack('Verification failed: $e', isError: true);
                          }
                        },
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.verified_rounded, size: 16),
                        label: const Text('Verify'),
                        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                            foregroundColor: AppColors.success,
                            side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.success),
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10))),
                      ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    OutlinedButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () async {
                        try {
                          await api.deleteSponsor(sponsor.id);
                          snack('Sponsor profile deleted successfully.');
                          onRefresh();
                        } catch (e) {
                          snack('Deletion failed: $e', isError: true);
                        }
                      },
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline_rounded, size: 16),
                      label: const Text('Delete'),
                      style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                          foregroundColor: AppColors.danger,
                          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.danger),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10))),
                    ),
                  ],
                )
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/map/add_cooler_dialog.dart">
// lib/screens/map/add_cooler_dialog.dart
//
// The "Add Public Cooler Point" form. It only collects what the person typed
// and picked; it does not talk to the server. It returns a NewCoolerRequest
// (or null if cancelled) and the caller sends it.

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import 'map_widgets.dart';

/// A photo chosen in the form, with what it shows ('Cooler' or 'Cups').
class PhotoDraft {
  final XFile file;
  final String category;
  PhotoDraft(this.file, this.category);
}

/// Everything the add-cooler form collects.
class NewCoolerRequest {
  final String name;
  final List<PhotoDraft> photos;
  final bool? hasCups;
  final String? cupsType;
  NewCoolerRequest({
    required this.name,
    required this.photos,
    this.hasCups,
    this.cupsType,
  });
}

Future<NewCoolerRequest?> showAddCoolerDialog(BuildContext context) async {
  final picker = ImagePicker();
  final controller = TextEditingController();
  final formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  List<PhotoDraft> pickedPhotos = [];
  String pickedCategory = 'Cooler';
  bool? hasCups;
  String? cupsType;
  
  return showDialog<NewCoolerRequest>(
    context: context,
    builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
      builder: (ctx, setDialog) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('Add Public Cooler Point', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: -0.5)),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),
        content: SizedBox(
          width: double.maxFinite,
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Form(
              key: formKey,
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: controller,
                    autofocus: true,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'Label Name / Landmark',
                      hintText: 'e.g. Next to El-Tawheed Mosque',
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12))),
                    ),
                    validator: (value) {
                      if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
                        return 'Required';
                      }
                      if (value.trim().length > 255) return 'Length limited to 255 chars';
                      return null;
                    },
                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  const Text(
                    'Does it have clean cups?',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.slate800),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      CupsToggleChip(
                        label: 'Yes',
                        icon: Icons.coffee_rounded,
                        color: AppColors.success,
                        selected: hasCups == true,
                        onTap: () => setDialog(() {
                          hasCups = true;
                        }),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      CupsToggleChip(
                        label: 'No',
                        icon: Icons.coffee_outlined,
                        color: AppColors.danger,
                        selected: hasCups == false,
                        onTap: () => setDialog(() {
                          hasCups = false;
                          cupsType = null;
                        }),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      CupsToggleChip(
                        label: 'Unsure',
                        icon: Icons.help_outline_rounded,
                        color: AppColors.slate500,
                        selected: hasCups == null,
                        onTap: () => setDialog(() {
                          hasCups = null;
                          cupsType = null;
                        }),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  const Text(
                    'Attach verification photos (Recommended)',
                    style:
                        TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.slate800),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: PhotoCategoryChip(
                          label: 'Cooler Unit',
                          icon: Icons.water_drop_outlined,
                          selected: pickedCategory == 'Cooler',
                          onTap: () => setDialog(() => pickedCategory = 'Cooler'),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: PhotoCategoryChip(
                          label: 'Cups Rack',
                          icon: Icons.coffee_outlined,
                          selected: pickedCategory == 'Cups',
                          onTap: () => setDialog(() => pickedCategory = 'Cups'),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () async {
                            final x = await picker.pickImage(
                              source: ImageSource.gallery,
                              maxWidth: 1920,
                              imageQuality: 85,
                              requestFullMetadata: false,
                            );
                            if (x != null) setDialog(() => pickedPhotos.add(PhotoDraft(x, pickedCategory)));
                          },
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined, size: 18),
                          label: const Text('Galleries'),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () async {
                            final x = await picker.pickImage(
                              source: ImageSource.camera,
                              maxWidth: 1920,
                              imageQuality: 85,
                              requestFullMetadata: false,
                            );
                            if (x != null) setDialog(() => pickedPhotos.add(PhotoDraft(x, pickedCategory)));
                          },
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt_outlined, size: 18),
                          label: const Text('Camera'),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  if (pickedPhotos.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    SizedBox(
                      height: 100,
                      child: ListView.builder(
                        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                        itemCount: pickedPhotos.length,
                        itemBuilder: (ctx, i) {
                          final p = pickedPhotos[i];
                          return Stack(
                            children: [
                              Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 10),
                                child: ClipRRect(
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                  child: Image.file(
                                    File(p.file.path),
                                    width: 100,
                                    height: 100,
                                    fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              Positioned(
                                top: 4,
                                right: 14,
                                child: GestureDetector(
                                  onTap: () => setDialog(() => pickedPhotos.removeAt(i)),
                                  child: const CircleAvatar(
                                    radius: 12,
                                    backgroundColor: AppColors.danger,
                                    child: Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 13, color: Colors.white),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              Positioned(
                                bottom: 4,
                                left: 4,
                                child: Container(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                                  ),
                                  child: Text(
                                    p.category,
                                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
            child: const Text('Cancel'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () {
              if (formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
                Navigator.of(ctx).pop(NewCoolerRequest(
                  name: controller.text.trim(),
                  photos: List<PhotoDraft>.from(pickedPhotos),
                  hasCups: hasCups,
                  cupsType: cupsType,
                ));
              }
            },
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.primary),
            child: const Text('Propose Location'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );
}
