<document path="mobile/lib/screens/map/cooler_info.dart">
// lib/screens/map/cooler_info.dart
//
// Plain functions that turn a cooler's data into words, colours and icons:
// the cups line ("Out of cup supplies") and how fresh the last check is.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';

IconData cupsIcon(bool? hasCups) {
  if (hasCups == null) return Icons.help_outline_rounded;
  return hasCups ? Icons.coffee_rounded : Icons.coffee_outlined;
}

Color cupsColor(bool? hasCups) {
  if (hasCups == null) return AppColors.slate500;
  return hasCups ? AppColors.success : AppColors.danger;
}

String cupsLabel(bool? hasCups, String? cupsType) {
  if (hasCups == null) return 'Unreported cups stock';
  if (!hasCups) return 'Out of cup supplies';
  if (cupsType == 'Communal') return 'Dispensing Reusable Cups';
  if (cupsType == 'Disposable') return 'Dispensing Disposable Stock';
  return 'Cups available';
}

StalenessInfo stalenessInfo(String lastVerifiedAt) {
  final verified = DateTime.tryParse(lastVerifiedAt);
  if (verified == null) return const StalenessInfo('Unknown status freshness', Colors.grey);
  final days = DateTime.now().difference(verified).inDays;
  if (days <= 3) {
    return StalenessInfo(
        days == 0 ? 'Verified today' : 'Verified $days day${days == 1 ? '' : 's'} ago',
        AppColors.success);
  }
  if (days <= 14) return StalenessInfo('Stale: $days days ago', AppColors.warning);
  if (days <= 30) return StalenessInfo('Stale: $days days ago', AppColors.danger);
  final weeks = (days / 7).floor();
  return StalenessInfo(
      '$weeks weeks ago — requires fresh verification', AppColors.danger);
}

class StalenessInfo {
  final String text;
  final Color color;
  const StalenessInfo(this.text, this.color);
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/map/cooler_sheet.dart">
// lib/screens/map/cooler_sheet.dart
//
// The bottom sheet that opens when a cooler marker is tapped: status, freshness,
// cups, "Still working" and status buttons, add-photo button, sponsor badge.
// It does no server work itself; the screen passes in what each button does.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../widgets/status_style.dart';
import 'cooler_info.dart';
import 'map_widgets.dart';

void showCoolerSheet(
  BuildContext context,
  WaterCooler cooler, {
  required VoidCallback onNavigate,
  required VoidCallback onConfirmWorking,
  required void Function(String newStatus) onChangeStatus,
  required VoidCallback onAddPhoto,
}) {
  final staleness = stalenessInfo(cooler.lastVerifiedAt);

  final verifiedDate = DateTime.tryParse(cooler.lastVerifiedAt);
  final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
  final diff = verifiedDate != null ? now.difference(verifiedDate) : const Duration(hours: 1);
  final canConfirm = diff.inMinutes >= 60;

  showModalBottomSheet(
    context: context,
    isScrollControlled: true,
    backgroundColor: Colors.white,
    shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))),
    builder: (_) => Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 16, 24, 36),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 40,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.grey.shade300,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(cooler.name,
                    style: Theme.of(context)
                        .textTheme
                        .titleLarge
                        ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppColors.slate800, letterSpacing: -0.5)),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              IconButton.filled(
                onPressed: () => onNavigate(),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.directions_rounded),
                tooltip: 'Navigate',
                style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                    foregroundColor: Colors.white),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),
          InfoRow(
            icon: statusIcon(cooler.status),
            iconColor: statusColor(cooler.status),
            label: 'Operational Status',
            value: cooler.status,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          InfoRow(
            icon: Icons.verified_user_outlined,
            iconColor: staleness.color,
            label: 'Verified Freshness',
            value: staleness.text,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          InfoRow(
            icon: cupsIcon(cooler.hasCups),
            iconColor: cupsColor(cooler.hasCups),
            label: 'Cup Supplies',
            value: cupsLabel(cooler.hasCups, cooler.cupsType),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          if (cooler.status == 'Working') ...[
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: canConfirm
                  ? FilledButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () => onConfirmWorking(),
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.verified_rounded, size: 18),
                      label: const Text('Report: Still Working'),
                      style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                      ),
                    )
                  : FilledButton.icon(
                      onPressed: null,
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.timer_outlined, size: 18),
                      label: Text('Verified (${60 - diff.inMinutes}m left for point reward)'),
                      style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                        disabledBackgroundColor: AppColors.slate100,
                        disabledForegroundColor: AppColors.slate400,
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
          ],
          const Text('Change reported status',
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.slate600)),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: StatusButton(
                  label: 'Working',
                  color: AppColors.success,
                  icon: Icons.water_drop,
                  isActive: cooler.status == 'Working',
                  // The server refuses "report the status it already has", so do nothing here.
                  onTap: cooler.status == 'Working' ? () {} : () => onChangeStatus('Working'),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: StatusButton(
                  label: 'Empty',
                  color: AppColors.slate500,
                  icon: Icons.water_drop_outlined,
                  isActive: cooler.status == 'Empty',
                  // The server refuses "report the status it already has", so do nothing here.
                  onTap: cooler.status == 'Empty' ? () {} : () => onChangeStatus('Empty'),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: StatusButton(
                  label: 'Broken',
                  color: AppColors.danger,
                  icon: Icons.report,
                  isActive: cooler.status == 'Broken',
                  // The server refuses "report the status it already has", so do nothing here.
                  onTap: cooler.status == 'Broken' ? () {} : () => onChangeStatus('Broken'),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () => onAddPhoto(),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt_outlined, size: 18),
              label: const Text('Add Status Evidence Photo'),
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary),
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          if (cooler.sponsor != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 18),
            SponsorBadge(sponsor: cooler.sponsor!),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/map/map_markers.dart">
// lib/screens/map/map_markers.dart
//
// The shapes drawn on the map: the blue "you are here" dot, one marker per
// cooler (coloured by status, with a dot when the last check is old), and the
// numbered bubble that groups nearby markers.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../../widgets/status_style.dart';

Marker buildUserMarker(LatLng loc) => Marker(
      point: loc,
      width: 48,
      height: 48,
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 48,
            height: 48,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.18),
                shape: BoxShape.circle),
          ),
          Container(
            width: 18,
            height: 18,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.blue,
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2.5),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.blue.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
                    blurRadius: 6,
                    spreadRadius: 1)
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

Marker buildCoolerMarker(WaterCooler cooler, VoidCallback onTap) {
    final color = statusColor(cooler.status);
    final stalenessDays = DateTime.now()
        .difference(
            DateTime.tryParse(cooler.lastVerifiedAt) ??
                DateTime.now())
        .inDays;
    return Marker(
      point: LatLng(cooler.latitude, cooler.longitude),
      width: 40,
      height: 40,
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            Container(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border:
                    Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                    blurRadius: 6,
                    spreadRadius: 1,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Icon(statusIcon(cooler.status),
                  color: Colors.white, size: 20),
            ),
            if (stalenessDays > 14)
              Positioned(
                right: 0,
                top: 0,
                child: Container(
                  width: 12,
                  height: 12,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: stalenessDays > 30
                        ? AppColors.danger
                        : AppColors.warning,
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    border: Border.all(
                        color: Colors.white, width: 1.5),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
}

Widget buildClusterBubble(int count) {
                  return Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 2),
                      boxShadow: [
                        BoxShadow(
                          color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
                          blurRadius: 8,
                          spreadRadius: 1,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    child: Center(
                      child: Text(
                        '$count',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          fontSize: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
}

// lib/screens/map/map_widgets.dart
//
// Small, reusable pieces used by the map screen and its pop-ups:
// legend, hint banner, info row, status button, cups chips, sponsor badge.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../models/water_cooler.dart';
import '../sponsor_screen.dart';

class HintBanner extends StatelessWidget {
  const HintBanner({super.key});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.65),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
      child: const Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(Icons.touch_app_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: 15),
          SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text('Long-press map location to propose cooler',
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class InfoRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final Color iconColor;
  final String label;
  final String value;
  const InfoRow({super.key, required this.icon,
      required this.iconColor,
      required this.label,
      required this.value});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: iconColor, size: 20),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Text('$label: ',
            style:
                const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.slate600)),
        Expanded(
            child: Text(value,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate800),
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class Legend extends StatelessWidget {
  const Legend({super.key});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.94),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08), blurRadius: 10)
        ],
      ),
      child: const Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          LegendItem(color: AppColors.success, label: 'Working'),
          SizedBox(height: 6),
          LegendItem(color: AppColors.slate500, label: 'Empty'),
          SizedBox(height: 6),
          LegendItem(color: AppColors.danger, label: 'Broken'),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class LegendItem extends StatelessWidget {
  final Color color;
  final String label;
  const LegendItem({super.key, required this.color, required this.label});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Container(
            width: 10,
            height: 10,
            decoration:
                BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle)),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate600)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class CupsToggleChip extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color color;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  const CupsToggleChip({super.key, 
    required this.label,
    required this.icon,
    required this.color,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onTap,
  });
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? color.withValues(alpha: 0.12) : Colors.transparent,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          border: Border.all(
            color: selected ? color : Colors.grey.shade300,
            width: selected ? 1.5 : 1,
          ),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(icon, size: 14, color: selected ? color : Colors.grey),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                fontWeight:
                    selected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                color: selected ? color : Colors.grey.shade600,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),

Widget buildSearchResultsList({

  required VoidCallback onCollapse,

}) {

      top: 12,
      left: 16,

                            color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                    trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded,
                        color: Colors.grey.shade300),
                    onTap: () => onSelect(cooler),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';
import 'profile_error_card.dart';

class LeaderboardCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool loading;
  final String? error;
  final List<LeaderboardEntry> entries;
  final String? currentUsername;
  final VoidCallback onRetry;

  const LeaderboardCard({
    super.key,
    required this.loading,
    required this.error,
    required this.entries,
    required this.currentUsername,
    required this.onRetry,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
            blurRadius: 12,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
        border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate100),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 20, 20, 12),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.leaderboard_rounded,
                    color: AppColors.primary, size: 22),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                const Text(
                  'Community Leaderboard',
                  style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate800),
                ),
                const Spacer(),
                if (loading)
                  const SizedBox(
                      width: 16,
                      height: 16,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: AppColors.primary)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1, color: AppColors.slate100),
          if (error != null)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: ProfileErrorCard(
                  message: error!,
                  onRetry: onRetry),
            )
          else if (!loading && entries.isEmpty)
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(32),
              child: Center(
                  child: Text('No participants recorded.',
                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey))),
            )
          else
            ListView.separated(
              shrinkWrap: true,
              physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
              itemCount: entries.length,
              separatorBuilder: (_, __) =>
                  const Divider(height: 1, indent: 20, endIndent: 20, color: AppColors.slate100),
              itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                final entry = entries[i];
                final isMe = currentUsername == entry.username;
                return _LeaderboardRow(
                    entry: entry, isCurrentUser: isMe);
              },
            ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _LeaderboardRow extends StatelessWidget {
  final LeaderboardEntry entry;
  final bool isCurrentUser;

  const _LeaderboardRow(
      {required this.entry, required this.isCurrentUser});

  String _rankEmoji(int rank) => switch (rank) {
        1 => '🥇',
        2 => '🥈',
        3 => '🥉',
        _ => '#$rank',
      };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final rankWidget = entry.rank <= 3
        ? Text(_rankEmoji(entry.rank), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22))
        : Text(
            '#${entry.rank}',
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate400),
          );

    return Container(
      color:
          isCurrentUser ? AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.04) : null,
      padding:
          const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 14),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 36,
            child: Align(
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              child: rankWidget,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Container(
            width: 38,
            height: 38,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isCurrentUser
                  ? AppColors.teal100
                  : AppColors.slate100,
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: Center(
              child: Text(
                entry.username.isNotEmpty
                    ? entry.username[0].toUpperCase()
                    : '?',
                style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: isCurrentUser
                        ? AppColors.primary
                        : AppColors.slate600,
                    fontSize: 15),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Expanded(
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Text(
                  entry.username,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 14,
                    color: isCurrentUser
                        ? AppColors.primary
                        : AppColors.slate700,
                  ),
                ),
                if (isCurrentUser) ...[
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                        horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.teal100,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    ),
                    child: const Text('You',
                        style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10,
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            '${entry.trustScore} pts',
            style: const TextStyle(
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                fontSize: 14,
                color: AppColors.primary),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// lib/screens/profile/profile_card.dart
//
// The big teal card at the top of the profile: avatar, trust level, points, progress bar.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/theme.dart';
import '../../services/api_service.dart';
import '../my_submissions_screen.dart';
import 'profile_error_card.dart';
import 'trust_level.dart';

class ProfileCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool loading;
  final String? error;
  final UserProfile? profile;
  final VoidCallback onRetry;

  const ProfileCard({
    super.key,
    required this.loading,
    required this.error,
    required this.profile,
    required this.onRetry,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (loading) {
      return Container(
        height: 180,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
          border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate100),
        ),
        child: const Center(
          child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.primary),
        ),
      );
    }

    if (error != null || profile == null) {
      return ProfileErrorCard(message: error ?? 'Unknown error.', onRetry: onRetry);
    }

    final data = profile!;
    final level = trustLevelFor(data.trustScore);
    final isAutoApproved = data.trustScore >= autoApproveThreshold;
    final progress = trustProgress(data.trustScore);

    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: const LinearGradient(
          colors: [AppColors.primary, AppColors.teal600],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
            blurRadius: 18,
            offset: const Offset(0, 8),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 60,
                height: 60,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.18),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  border: Border.all(color: Colors.white30, width: 2),
                ),
                child: Center(
                  child: Text(
                    data.username.isNotEmpty
                        ? data.username[0].toUpperCase()
                        : '?',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 26,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Colors.white),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 16),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      data.username,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 20,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.white,
                          letterSpacing: -0.5),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.14),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(level.emoji,
                              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
                          const SizedBox(width: 4),
                          Text(
                            level.label,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 11,
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Text('Trust Points Earned',
                  style: TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white70,
                      fontSize: 13,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
              Text(
                '${data.trustScore} pts',
                style: const TextStyle(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 22,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                    letterSpacing: -0.5),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),

          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
              backgroundColor: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.18),
              valueColor:
                  const AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Colors.white),
              minHeight: 8,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            isAutoApproved
                ? '🚀 Submissions auto-approved instantly!'
                : '${autoApproveThreshold - data.trustScore} pts until instant auto-approvals',
            style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: Colors.teal.shade50,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(
                  MaterialPageRoute(
                      builder: (_) => const MySubmissionsScreen())),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.list_alt_rounded,
                  color: Colors.white, size: 18),
              label: const Text('My Submissions Dashboard',
                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                side: BorderSide(
                    color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/screens/profile/trust_level.dart">
// lib/screens/profile/trust_level.dart
//
// Trust levels (Newcomer ... Community Champion) and progress towards the next one.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Score at which a user's submissions are approved instantly.
/// Keep in step with AUTO_APPROVE_THRESHOLD in backend/sabil/users/trust.py.
const int autoApproveThreshold = 500;

class TrustLevel {
  final String label;
  final String emoji;
  final Color color;

  const TrustLevel(this.label, this.emoji, this.color);
}

TrustLevel trustLevelFor(int score) {
  if (score >= 1000) {
    return const TrustLevel('Community Champion', '💎', Color(0xFF00BCD4));
  }
  if (score >= autoApproveThreshold) {
    return const TrustLevel('Verified Reporter', '🏆', Color(0xFF4CAF50));
  }
  if (score >= 250) {
    return const TrustLevel('Trusted', '⭐', Color(0xFF2196F3));
  }
  if (score >= 100) {
    return const TrustLevel('Contributor', '🤝', Color(0xFF9C27B0));
  }
  return const TrustLevel('Newcomer', '🌱', Color(0xFF607D8B));
}

/// 0.0 to 1.0: how far the score is between the previous and the next milestone.
double trustProgress(int score) {
  final milestones = [100, 250, autoApproveThreshold, 1000];
  final next = milestones.firstWhere((m) => m > score, orElse: () => score + 1);
  final previous = milestones.lastWhere((m) => m <= score, orElse: () => 0);
  if (next == previous) return 1.0;
  return ((score - previous) / (next - previous)).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/services/admin_api.dart">
// lib/services/admin_api.dart
//
// Server calls only an admin may make: the review queue, approving/rejecting
// coolers and photos, forcing a status, and deleting. (Admin sponsor actions
// are in sponsors_api.dart; the admin login is in auth_api.dart.)

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../core/config.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';

mixin class AdminApi {
  // ─── Photos ───

  Future<CoolerPhoto> adminUploadPhoto({
    required String coolerId,
    required File imageFile,
    String photoCategory = 'Cooler',
  }) async {
    final request =
        http.MultipartRequest('POST', apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/photos'));
    SessionStore.adminHeaders().forEach((k, v) => request.headers[k] = v);
    request.files.add(await http.MultipartFile.fromPath(
      'file',
      imageFile.path,
      contentType: mediaTypeFor(imageFile.path),
    ));
    request.fields['photo_category'] = photoCategory;

    final streamed = await request.send().timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    final response = await http.Response.fromStream(streamed);
    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      return CoolerPhoto.fromJson(jsonDecode(response.body));
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to upload photo (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> approvePhoto(String photoId) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(apiUri('/admin/photos/$photoId/approve'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to approve photo (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> deletePhoto(String photoId) async {
    final response = await http
        .delete(apiUri('/admin/photos/$photoId'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 204) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to delete photo (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<List<CoolerPhoto>> fetchCoolerPhotosAdmin(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/photos'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => CoolerPhoto.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch photos (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  // ─── Coolers ───

  Future<List<WaterCooler>> fetchPendingCoolers() async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/admin/coolers/pending'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => WaterCooler.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch pending coolers (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<List<WaterCooler>> fetchCurrentCoolers() async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/admin/coolers/current'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => WaterCooler.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch current coolers (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> approveCooler(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/approve'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to approve cooler (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> rejectCooler(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/reject'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to reject cooler (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> rejectStatusChange(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/reject-status'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to reject status change (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> forceUpdateStatus({
    required String coolerId,
    required String newStatus,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(
          apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/force-status'),
          headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders({'Content-Type': 'application/json'}),
          body: jsonEncode({'status': newStatus}),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to force update status (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> deleteCooler(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .delete(apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 204) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to delete cooler (${response.statusCode}).');
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/services/api_client.dart">
// lib/services/api_client.dart
//
// Small helpers shared by every API file. Knows nothing about coolers or users,
// only how to build a URL, read an error message, and label an image upload.

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:http_parser/http_parser.dart';

import '../core/config.dart';

/// Full URL for a server path, e.g. apiUri('/coolers/nearby').
Uri apiUri(String path) => Uri.parse('${ApiConfig.baseUrl}$path');

/// The server's error text ("detail"), or [fallback] if there is none.
String extractError(http.Response response, String fallback) {
  try {
    final body = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    return (body['detail'] ?? fallback).toString();
  } catch (_) {
    return fallback;
  }
}

/// The image content type to send for a file, judged by its extension.
MediaType mediaTypeFor(String path) {
  final ext = path.split('.').last.toLowerCase();
  if (ext == 'png') return MediaType('image', 'png');
  if (ext == 'webp') return MediaType('image', 'webp');
  return MediaType('image', 'jpeg');
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/services/api_service.dart">
// lib/services/api_service.dart
//
// The one class the screens use to talk to the server. It used to hold ~600
// lines of code; now it only combines the smaller pieces:
//
//   AuthApi      register, login, profile, leaderboard, admin login   (auth_api.dart)
//   CoolersApi   find / add / report / confirm / upload photo         (coolers_api.dart)
//   SponsorsApi  sponsor pages and sponsor admin                      (sponsors_api.dart)
//   AdminApi     review queue and moderation                          (admin_api.dart)
//   SessionStore who is logged in, tokens                             (session_store.dart)
//
// Existing screens keep calling ApiService().something(); new code may use the
// smaller pieces directly, e.g. AdminApi().fetchPendingCoolers().

import '../models/user_profile.dart';
import 'admin_api.dart';
import 'auth_api.dart';
import 'coolers_api.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';
import 'sponsors_api.dart';

// UserProfile and LeaderboardEntry used to be defined in this file; they are
// re-exported so screens that import api_service.dart still see them.
export '../models/leaderboard_entry.dart';
export '../models/user_profile.dart';

class ApiService with AuthApi, CoolersApi, SponsorsApi, AdminApi {
  // ─── Session (static, shared by the whole app) ───

  static bool get isLoggedIn => SessionStore.isLoggedIn;
  static UserProfile? get cachedProfile => SessionStore.cachedProfile;

  static void setAdminToken(String? token) => SessionStore.setAdminToken(token);
  static void clearAdminToken() => SessionStore.clearAdminToken();

  static Future<void> setUserToken(String? token) =>
      SessionStore.setUserToken(token);
  static Future<void> clearUserToken() => SessionStore.clearUserToken();

  /// Sent with image requests in the admin screens: unapproved photos are only
  /// served to an admin (or the uploader), so the admin's login token must go along.
  static Map<String, String> get adminImageHeaders => SessionStore.adminHeaders();

  /// Call once at app start: restores a saved login if it is still valid.
  static Future<void> restoreSession() =>
      SessionStore.restoreSession(() => AuthApi().getUserProfile());
}

<document path="mobile/lib/services/auth_api.dart">
// lib/services/auth_api.dart
//
// Server calls about people: register, log in, profile, leaderboard, and the
// admin login.
//
// `mixin class` lets this be used on its own (AuthApi().register(...)) AND mixed
// into ApiService (class ApiService with AuthApi, ...), so old code keeps working.

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../core/config.dart';
import '../models/leaderboard_entry.dart';
import '../models/user_profile.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';

mixin class AuthApi {
  Future<UserProfile> register({
    required String username,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .post(
          apiUri('/auth/register'),
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'username': username, 'password': password}),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);

    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      final body = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final token = body['access_token'] as String?;
      if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Register response did not include access token.');
      }
      await SessionStore.setUserToken(token);
      final profile = UserProfile.fromJson(body);
      SessionStore.cachedProfile = profile;
      return profile;
    }
    if (response.statusCode == 409) throw Exception('Username already taken.');
    throw Exception(extractError(response, 'Registration failed (${response.statusCode}).'));
  }

  Future<UserProfile> userLogin({
    required String username,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .post(
          apiUri('/auth/login'),
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'username': username, 'password': password}),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final body = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final token = body['access_token'] as String?;
      if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Login response did not include access token.');
      }
      await SessionStore.setUserToken(token);
      final profile = UserProfile.fromJson(body);
      SessionStore.cachedProfile = profile;
      return profile;
    }
    throw Exception('Invalid username or password.');
  }

  Future<UserProfile> getUserProfile() async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/auth/me'), headers: SessionStore.userHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final profile = UserProfile.fromJson(
          jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>);
      SessionStore.cachedProfile = profile;
      return profile;
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch profile (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<List<LeaderboardEntry>> getLeaderboard() async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/users/leaderboard'))
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => LeaderboardEntry.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch leaderboard (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<String> adminLogin({
    required String username,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .post(
          apiUri('/auth/admin/login'),
          headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
          body: jsonEncode({'username': username, 'password': password}),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final body = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      final token = body['access_token'] as String?;
      if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
        throw Exception('Admin login response missing access token.');
      }
      SessionStore.setAdminToken(token);
      return token;
    }
    throw Exception('Admin login failed (${response.statusCode}).');
  }
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/services/coolers_api.dart">
// lib/services/coolers_api.dart
//
// Server calls for coolers that any user can make: find them, add one, report
// a status, confirm "still working", and upload a photo.

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../core/config.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';

mixin class CoolersApi {
  // ─── Read ───

  Future<List<WaterCooler>> fetchNearbyCoolers({
    required double lat,
    required double lng,
    double radiusKm = 5.0,
  }) async {
    final uri = apiUri('/coolers/nearby?lat=$lat&lng=$lng&radius_km=$radiusKm');
    final response = await http.get(uri).timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => WaterCooler.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch nearby coolers (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<List<WaterCooler>> searchCoolers(String query) async {
    final uri = apiUri('/coolers/search?q=${Uri.encodeComponent(query.trim())}');
    final response = await http.get(uri).timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => WaterCooler.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Search failed (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<List<WaterCooler>> fetchMySubmissions() async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/coolers/my'), headers: SessionStore.userHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => WaterCooler.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch submissions (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<List<CoolerPhoto>> fetchCoolerPhotos(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/coolers/$coolerId/photos'))
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => CoolerPhoto.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch photos (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  // ─── Write ───

  Future<String> createCoolerAndReturnId({
    required String name,
    required double latitude,
    required double longitude,
    String status = 'Working',
    bool? hasCups,
    String? cupsType,
  }) async {
    final body = <String, dynamic>{
      'name': name,
      'latitude': latitude,
      'longitude': longitude,
      'status': status,
    };
    if (hasCups != null) body['has_cups'] = hasCups;
    if (cupsType != null) body['cups_type'] = cupsType;

    final response = await http
        .post(
          apiUri('/coolers'),
          headers: SessionStore.userHeaders({'Content-Type': 'application/json'}),
          body: jsonEncode(body),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);

    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      final responseBody = jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
      return responseBody['id'] as String;
    }
    throw Exception(extractError(response, 'Failed to create cooler (${response.statusCode}).'));
  }

  Future<bool> updateCoolerStatus({
    required String coolerId,
    required String newStatus,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(
          apiUri('/coolers/$coolerId/status'),
          headers: SessionStore.userHeaders({'Content-Type': 'application/json'}),
          body: jsonEncode({'status': newStatus}),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception(
        extractError(response, 'Failed to update status (${response.statusCode}).'));
  }

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> confirmCoolerWorking(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .post(
          apiUri('/coolers/$coolerId/confirm'),
          headers: SessionStore.userHeaders(),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>;
    }
    if (response.statusCode == 429) {
      throw Exception(extractError(response, 'Too many confirmations.'));
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to confirm cooler (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<CoolerPhoto> uploadPhoto({
    required String coolerId,
    required File imageFile,
    String photoCategory = 'Cooler',
  }) async {
    final request = http.MultipartRequest('POST', apiUri('/coolers/$coolerId/photos'));
    SessionStore.userHeaders().forEach((k, v) => request.headers[k] = v);
    request.files.add(await http.MultipartFile.fromPath(
      'file',
      imageFile.path,
      contentType: mediaTypeFor(imageFile.path),
    ));
    request.fields['photo_category'] = photoCategory;

    final streamed = await request.send().timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    final response = await http.Response.fromStream(streamed);
    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      return CoolerPhoto.fromJson(jsonDecode(response.body));
    }
    throw Exception(extractError(response, 'Failed to upload photo (${response.statusCode}).'));
  }
}

<document path="mobile/lib/services/location_service.dart">
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';

class LocationService {
  /// Resolves a fix without blocking indefinitely (emulators often stall on
  /// high-accuracy GPS). Uses last-known when available, then a bounded wait.
  Future<Position?> resolveCurrentPosition() async {
    final serviceEnabled = await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled();
    if (!serviceEnabled) {
      throw LocationServiceDisabledException();
    }

    LocationPermission permission = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
    if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
      permission = await Geolocator.requestPermission();
      if (permission == LocationPermission.denied) {
        throw LocationPermissionDeniedException();
      }
    }

    if (permission == LocationPermission.deniedForever) {
      throw LocationPermissionPermanentlyDeniedException();
    }

    final cached = await Geolocator.getLastKnownPosition();
    if (cached != null) {
      return cached;
    }

    try {
      return await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(
        desiredAccuracy: LocationAccuracy.medium,
        timeLimit: const Duration(seconds: 12),
      );
    } on TimeoutException {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

class LocationServiceDisabledException implements Exception {
  final String message = 'Location services are disabled.';
}

class LocationPermissionDeniedException implements Exception {
  final String message = 'Location permission denied. Showing default area.';
}

class LocationPermissionPermanentlyDeniedException implements Exception {
  final String message = 'Location permission permanently denied.';
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/services/session_store.dart">
// lib/services/session_store.dart
//
// Remembers who is logged in. Holds the user token (also saved on the phone in
// secure storage so the login survives closing the app) and the admin token
// (memory only), and builds the "Authorization" header the server expects.

import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

import '../models/user_profile.dart';

class SessionStore {
  SessionStore._();

  static const _storage = FlutterSecureStorage();

  static String? _adminToken;
  static String? _userToken;

  /// The logged-in user's profile from the last time we fetched it.
  static UserProfile? cachedProfile;

  // ─── Admin token (not saved to disk) ───

  static void setAdminToken(String? token) => _adminToken = token;
  static void clearAdminToken() => _adminToken = null;

  // ─── User token (saved to secure storage) ───

  static Future<void> setUserToken(String? token) async {
    _userToken = token;
    if (token != null) {
      await _storage.write(key: 'user_token', value: token);
    } else {
      await _storage.delete(key: 'user_token');
    }
  }

  static Future<void> clearUserToken() async {
    _userToken = null;
    cachedProfile = null;
    await _storage.delete(key: 'user_token');
  }

  /// Called once when the app starts. If a token was saved last time, check it
  /// is still valid by fetching the profile; if not, forget it.
  static Future<void> restoreSession(
      Future<UserProfile> Function() fetchProfile) async {
    final stored = await _storage.read(key: 'user_token');
    if (stored == null) return;
    _userToken = stored;
    try {
      cachedProfile = await fetchProfile();
    } catch (_) {
      _userToken = null;
      await _storage.delete(key: 'user_token');
    }
  }

  static bool get isLoggedIn => _userToken != null && _userToken!.isNotEmpty;

  // ─── Request headers ───

  static Map<String, String> adminHeaders([Map<String, String>? extra]) {
    final merged = <String, String>{...?extra};
    if (_adminToken != null && _adminToken!.isNotEmpty) {
      merged['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $_adminToken';
    }
    return merged;
  }

  static Map<String, String> userHeaders([Map<String, String>? extra]) {
    final merged = <String, String>{...?extra};
    if (_userToken != null && _userToken!.isNotEmpty) {
      merged['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $_userToken';
    }
    return merged;
  }
}
</document>

// lib/services/sponsors_api.dart
//
// Server calls about sponsors: the public list/detail pages, and the admin
// actions (create, verify, delete, attach to a cooler).

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

import '../core/config.dart';
import '../models/water_cooler.dart';
import 'api_client.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';

mixin class SponsorsApi {
  // ─── Public ───

  Future<List<Sponsor>> fetchAllSponsors() async {
    final response =
        await http.get(apiUri('/sponsors')).timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final List<dynamic> data = jsonDecode(response.body);
      return data.map((e) => Sponsor.fromJson(e)).toList();
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch sponsors (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<Sponsor> fetchSponsor(String sponsorId) async {
    final response = await http
        .get(apiUri('/sponsors/$sponsorId'))
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return Sponsor.fromJson(jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>);
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to fetch sponsor (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  // ─── Admin ───

  Future<Sponsor> createSponsor({
    required String name,
    required String sponsorType,
    String? contact,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .post(
          apiUri('/admin/sponsors'),
          headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders({'Content-Type': 'application/json'}),
          body: jsonEncode({
            'name': name,
            'sponsor_type': sponsorType,
            if (contact != null && contact.isNotEmpty) 'contact': contact,
          }),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 201) {
      return Sponsor.fromJson(jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>);
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to create sponsor (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> verifySponsor(String sponsorId) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(apiUri('/admin/sponsors/$sponsorId/verify'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to verify sponsor (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<bool> deleteSponsor(String sponsorId) async {
    final response = await http
        .delete(apiUri('/admin/sponsors/$sponsorId'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 204) return true;
    throw Exception('Failed to delete sponsor (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<WaterCooler> assignSponsor({
    required String coolerId,
    required String sponsorId,
  }) async {
    final response = await http
        .patch(
          apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/assign-sponsor'),
          headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders({'Content-Type': 'application/json'}),
          body: jsonEncode({'sponsor_id': sponsorId}),
        )
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return WaterCooler.fromJson(
          jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>);
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to assign sponsor (${response.statusCode}).');
  }

  Future<WaterCooler> removeSponsor(String coolerId) async {
    final response = await http
        .delete(apiUri('/admin/coolers/$coolerId/assign-sponsor'),
            headers: SessionStore.adminHeaders())
        .timeout(ApiConfig.timeout);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      return WaterCooler.fromJson(
          jsonDecode(response.body) as Map<String, dynamic>);
    }
    throw Exception('Failed to remove sponsor (${response.statusCode}).');
  }
}

// lib/widgets/app_snackbar.dart

// The little message bar shown at the bottom of the screen. Teal for normal

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

void showAppSnackbar(BuildContext context, String message, {bool isError = false}) {
  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(
    content: Text(
      message,
      style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, fontSize: 13),
    ),
    backgroundColor: isError ? AppColors.danger : AppColors.primary,
    behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
    margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
  ));
}

<document path="mobile/lib/widgets/auth_dialog.dart">
// lib/widgets/auth_dialog.dart
//
// The login / register pop-up. Show it with:
//   final loggedIn = await showDialog<bool>(context: context, builder: (_) => const AuthDialog());
// It closes with `true` when the person logged in or registered.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/theme.dart';
import '../services/api_service.dart';

class AuthDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const AuthDialog({super.key});
  @override
  State<AuthDialog> createState() => _AuthDialogState();
}

class _AuthDialogState extends State<AuthDialog>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final TabController _tabs;
  final _loginUserCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _loginPassCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _regUserCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _regPassCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _loginKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _regKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final ApiService _api = ApiService();
  bool _loading = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabs = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _tabs.addListener(() => setState(() => _error = null));
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabs.dispose();
    _loginUserCtrl.dispose();
    _loginPassCtrl.dispose();
    _regUserCtrl.dispose();
    _regPassCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    final isLogin = _tabs.index == 0;
    final key = isLogin ? _loginKey : _regKey;
    if (!key.currentState!.validate()) return;
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      if (isLogin) {
        await _api.userLogin(
            username: _loginUserCtrl.text.trim(),
            password: _loginPassCtrl.text);
      } else {
        await _api.register(
            username: _regUserCtrl.text.trim(),
            password: _regPassCtrl.text);
      }
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() {
        _loading = false;
        _error = e.toString().replaceFirst('Exception: ', '');
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Access Sabil Network',
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, letterSpacing: -0.5)),
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 340,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TabBar(
              controller: _tabs,
              tabs: const [Tab(text: 'Login'), Tab(text: 'Register')],
              labelColor: AppColors.primary,
              indicatorColor: AppColors.primary,
              unselectedLabelColor: Colors.grey,
              labelStyle: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            SizedBox(
              height: 180,
              child: TabBarView(
                controller: _tabs,
                children: [
                  Form(
                    key: _loginKey,
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        TextFormField(
                          controller: _loginUserCtrl,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                              labelText: 'Username',
                              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)))),
                          validator: (v) =>
                              v == null || v.trim().isEmpty ? 'Required' : null,
                          enabled: !_loading,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 10),
                        TextFormField(
                          controller: _loginPassCtrl,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                              labelText: 'Password',
                              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)))),
                          obscureText: true,
                          validator: (v) =>
                              v == null || v.isEmpty ? 'Required' : null,
                          enabled: !_loading,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Form(
                    key: _regKey,
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        TextFormField(
                          controller: _regUserCtrl,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                              labelText: 'Username',
                              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)))),
                          validator: (v) {
                            if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) return 'Required';
                            if (v.trim().length < 3) return 'Min 3 chars';
                            if (!RegExp(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$")
                                .hasMatch(v.trim())) {
                              return 'Alpha-numerics only';
                            }
                            return null;
                          },
                          enabled: !_loading,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 10),
                        TextFormField(
                          controller: _regPassCtrl,
                          decoration: const InputDecoration(
                              labelText: 'Password (8+ chars)',
                              border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)))),
                          obscureText: true,
                          validator: (v) {
                            if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'Required';
                            if (v.length < 8) return 'Min 8 characters';
                            return null;
                          },
                          enabled: !_loading,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            if (_error != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(_error!,
                  style:
                      TextStyle(color: Colors.red.shade700, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
            onPressed: _loading ? null : () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
            child: const Text('Cancel')),
        FilledButton(
          onPressed: _loading ? null : _submit,
          style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.primary),
          child: _loading
              ? const SizedBox(
                  width: 16,
                  height: 16,
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                      strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white))
              : Text(_tabs.index == 0 ? 'Login' : 'Register'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
</document>

<document path="mobile/lib/widgets/photo_pickers.dart">
// lib/widgets/photo_pickers.dart
//
// The two bottom sheets shown when adding a photo to a cooler:
//   1) pickPhotoCategory: "what is the photo of?"  -> 'Cooler' or 'Cups' (null if dismissed)
//   2) pickImageSource:   "camera or gallery?"     -> ImageSource (null if dismissed)

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';

import '../core/theme.dart';

const _sheetShape = RoundedRectangleBorder(
  borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
);

/// The small grey bar at the top of a bottom sheet.
class _SheetHandle extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SheetHandle();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: 40,
      height: 4,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.grey.shade300,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
      ),
    );
  }
}

Future<String?> pickPhotoCategory(BuildContext context) {
  return showModalBottomSheet<String>(
    context: context,
    shape: _sheetShape,
    builder: (ctx) => SafeArea(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const _SheetHandle(),
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
              child: Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: Text(
                  'Categorize Photo Attachment',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 15,
                    color: AppColors.slate800,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.water_drop_outlined, color: AppColors.primary),
              title: const Text('Entire Cooler Unit'),
              subtitle: const Text('Verify physical address and condition'),
              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, 'Cooler'),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.coffee_outlined, color: AppColors.primary),
              title: const Text('Cups Rack Stock'),
              subtitle: const Text('Refill status of disposable stock'),
              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, 'Cups'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

Future<ImageSource?> pickImageSource(BuildContext context) {
  return showModalBottomSheet<ImageSource>(
    context: context,
    shape: _sheetShape,
    builder: (ctx) => SafeArea(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const _SheetHandle(),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt_outlined, color: AppColors.primary),
              title: const Text('Capture with Device Camera'),
              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, ImageSource.camera),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined, color: AppColors.primary),
              title: const Text('Import from Photo Stream'),
              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, ImageSource.gallery),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

<document path="mobile/lib/widgets/status_style.dart">
// lib/widgets/status_style.dart
//
// How a cooler's status (Working / Empty / Broken) looks: its colour, its icon,
// and the little rounded "pill" label. Written once and used by every screen.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/theme.dart';

Color statusColor(String status) => switch (status) {
      'Working' => AppColors.success,
      'Empty' => AppColors.slate500,
      'Broken' => AppColors.danger,
      _ => Colors.blue,
    };

IconData statusIcon(String status) => switch (status) {
      'Working' => Icons.water_drop,
      'Empty' => Icons.water_drop_outlined,
      'Broken' => Icons.report,
      _ => Icons.help_outline,
    };

/// A small rounded label, e.g. "Working". [isPending] draws it bolder to show
/// a change that is still waiting for an admin.
class StatusPill extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final bool isPending;

  const StatusPill({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.color,
    this.isPending = false,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 3),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withValues(alpha: isPending ? 0.15 : 0.1),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: color.withValues(alpha: isPending ? 0.6 : 0.3),
          width: isPending ? 1.5 : 1,
        ),
      ),
      child: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
          color: color,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
</document>

<document path="tools/pack_code.py">
"""Packs every source file of the project into one dated text file.

Run from anywhere:   python tools/pack_code.py
Output:              <repo root>/coolman_dump_<YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS>.txt

Each file is wrapped as <document path="..."> ... </document> so an AI
assistant (or a person) can see where every file begins and ends.
Older dumps are never overwritten and never included in a new dump.
"""
import os
from datetime import datetime

PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

IGNORE_DIRS = {
    '.git', '__pycache__', 'venv', '.venv', 'node_modules', 'uploads',
    '.dart_tool', 'build', '.idea', '.vscode', '.gradle',
    'ios', 'web', 'windows', 'macos', 'linux', 'android',
}
ALLOWED_EXTS = {'.dart', '.py', '.yaml', '.yml', '.xml', '.json', '.md', '.txt', '.toml'}


def generate_code_dump():
    now = datetime.now()
    output_file = os.path.join(
        PROJECT_ROOT, f"coolman_dump_{now.strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.txt"
    )
    count = 0
    with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out:
        out.write("### SABIL / COOLMAN FULL CODEBASE DUMP ###\n")
        out.write(f"### GENERATED ON: {now.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p')} ###\n\n")
        for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(PROJECT_ROOT):
            dirnames[:] = sorted(d for d in dirnames if d not in IGNORE_DIRS)
            for filename in sorted(filenames):
                if os.path.splitext(filename)[1].lower() not in ALLOWED_EXTS:
                    continue
                if filename.startswith("coolman_dump_"):
                    continue
                path = os.path.join(dirpath, filename)
                rel = os.path.relpath(path, PROJECT_ROOT).replace(os.sep, "/")
                out.write(f'<document path="{rel}">\n')
                try:
                    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        content = f.read()
                    out.write(content)
                    if not content.endswith('\n'):
                        out.write('\n')
                except Exception as e:
                    out.write(f"// Error reading file: {e}\n")
                out.write('</document>\n\n')
                count += 1
    print(f"Packed {count} files into {output_file}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_code_dump()
</document>
